          delete payload.caption;
          res = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
        }

        // 🟢 菜单键盘自动删除：时间到后删除机器人回复 + 用户触发消息
        if (matchedMenu.autoDeleteMinutes && matchedMenu.autoDeleteMinutes > 0) {
          try {
            if (res) {
              const resData = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
              if (resData.ok && resData.result) {
                const expireAt = new Date().getTime() + (Number(matchedMenu.autoDeleteMinutes) * 60 * 1000);
                const queueId = `menu_del_${new Date().getTime()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
                writeToFirebase(`delete_queue/${queueId}`, {
                  chat_id: String(chatId),
                  bot_msg_id: resData.result.message_id,
                  user_msg_id: message.message_id,
                  expire_at: expireAt
                });
              }
            }
          } catch (e) {}
        }
        return;
      }
    }

    // 🚀 核心新增：全局关键词回复拦截 (毫秒级响应，优先于底层状态判断执行，且仅在无向导状态时触发)
    if (!state && text) {
      const kwReplies = readFromFirebase('system_config/keyword_replies') || {};
      let matchedData = null;
      
      for (let key in kwReplies) {
        const kwConf = kwReplies[key];
        if ((kwConf.mode === 'exact' && text.trim() === kwConf.keyword) || 
            (kwConf.mode === 'fuzzy' && text.includes(kwConf.keyword))) {
           matchedData = kwConf; 
           break; // 命中立即跳出
        }
      }
      
      if (matchedData) {
        let kwKb = {};
        if (matchedData.buttonsRaw) {
          let keyboard = [];
          matchedData.buttonsRaw.split('\n').forEach(row => {
            let btnRow = [];
            row.split('|').forEach(btn => {
              let parts = btn.split('-');
              if (parts.length >= 2) {
                let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
                let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                let styleStr = ""; 
                if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
                  styleStr = lastP.toLowerCase(); urlParts.pop();
                }
                let urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();
                if (!urlObj.startsWith('http') && !urlObj.startsWith('tg://')) urlObj = 'https://' + urlObj;
                let btnConf = { text: parts[0].trim(), url: urlObj };
                if (styleStr) btnConf.style = styleStr;
                btnRow.push(btnConf);
              }
            });
            if (btnRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(btnRow);
          });
          if (keyboard.length > 0) kwKb = { inline_keyboard: keyboard };
        }

        // 🟢 修复：新增 reply_to_message_id，实现触发关键词时“引用并回复”用户的原始消息
        let payload = { 
          chat_id: chatId, 
          caption: matchedData.text || "", 
          parse_mode: "HTML", 
          reply_to_message_id: message.message_id 
        };
        
        if (Object.keys(kwKb).length > 0) payload.reply_markup = kwKb;

        let res; // 声明返回变量以获取机器人发出的 message_id
        if (matchedData.mediaId) {
          if (matchedData.mediaType === 'photo') { payload.photo = matchedData.mediaId; res = sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload); }
          else { payload.video = matchedData.mediaId; res = sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload); }
        } else {
          payload.text = matchedData.text || "";
          delete payload.caption;
          res = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
        }
        
        // 🟢 核心新增：关键词与触发词自动删除逻辑 (加入底层 Firebase 队列)
        if (matchedData.autoDeleteMinutes && matchedData.autoDeleteMinutes > 0) {
          try {
            if (res) {
              const resData = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
              if (resData.ok && resData.result) {
                // 计算过期时间：当前时间戳 + 设定分钟数的毫秒值
                const expireAt = new Date().getTime() + (matchedData.autoDeleteMinutes * 60 * 1000);
                const queueId = `kw_del_${new Date().getTime()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
                // 将机器人回复的消息 ID 和用户的触发词消息 ID 一并存入删除队列
                writeToFirebase(`delete_queue/${queueId}`, {
                  chat_id: chatId,
                  bot_msg_id: resData.result.message_id,
                  user_msg_id: message.message_id, // 连带用户的触发词一起处理
                  expire_at: expireAt
                });
              }
            }
          } catch(e) {}
        }
        return; // 彻底拦截，结束线程
      }
    }

    // 检查用户是否处于"等待输入"状态
    if (state && state.startsWith('WAITING_FOR_')) {
      const field = state.replace('WAITING_FOR_', '');
      
      if (field.startsWith('FBREPLY_')) {
        // 🟢 核心新增：接收并处理管理员对投诉建议的回复
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 回复内容只能是纯文本，请重新输入！");
          return;
        }

        // 🟢 解析新格式：FBREPLY_{targetUserId}_{feedbackId}（兼容旧格式：FBREPLY_{targetUserId}）
        const fbreplyRaw = field.replace('FBREPLY_', '');
        const fbreplyParts = fbreplyRaw.split('_');
        // targetUserId 是纯数字（第0段），feedbackId 是 fb_时间戳_随机数（第1段起拼回）
        const targetUserId = fbreplyParts[0];
        const feedbackId = fbreplyParts.length >= 2 ? fbreplyParts.slice(1).join('_') : null;

        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
        
        // 构造回复内容并私聊发给目标用户
        const replyToUser = `🔔 <b>管理员回复了您的投诉/建议：</b>\n\n💬 内容：\n<code>${escapeHtml(text)}</code>`;
        const pushResult = sendMessage(targetUserId, replyToUser, null, "HTML");
        
        // 验证发送结果，并反馈给管理员
        let isSuccess = false;
        try {
          if (pushResult && JSON.parse(pushResult.getContentText()).ok) {
            isSuccess = true;
          }
        } catch (e) {}

        if (isSuccess) {
          // 🟢 回复成功后，更新 Firebase 状态为"已回复"，封锁其他管理员再次尝试
          if (feedbackId) {
            const adminName = message.from.first_name || `管理员${userId}`;
            updateFirebase(`feedback_queue/${feedbackId}`, {
              handled: true,
              replied: true,
              handlerName: adminName,
              handlerId: String(userId)
            });
          }
          sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 回复已成功发送给用户 <code>${targetUserId}</code>！`, null, "HTML");
        } else {
          sendMessage(chatId, `❌ 发送失败！用户 <code>${targetUserId}</code> 可能已屏蔽/封锁本机器人。`, null, "HTML");
        }
        return;

      } else if (field === 'RANKING_ADDRESS') {
        // 🟢 上榜向导第1步：接收地址
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 地址只能是纯文本，请重新输入！", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] });
          return;
        }
        const rkDr1 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
        let rkD1 = rkDr1 ? JSON.parse(rkDr1) : {};
        rkD1.address = text;
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId, JSON.stringify(rkD1));
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_RANKING_PRICE');
        sendMessage(chatId, "✅ 地址已保存！\n\n💰 <b>第2步 / 共6步：请发送您的【服务价格】</b>\n（例：200 USDT起 / 300 USDT/小时）", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] }, "HTML");
        return;

      } else if (field === 'RANKING_PRICE') {
        // 🟢 上榜向导第2步：接收价格
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 价格只能是纯文本，请重新输入！", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] });
          return;
        }
        const rkDr2 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
        let rkD2 = rkDr2 ? JSON.parse(rkDr2) : {};
        rkD2.price = text;
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId, JSON.stringify(rkD2));
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_RANKING_SERVICES');
        sendMessage(chatId, "✅ 价格已保存！\n\n💆 <b>第3步 / 共6步：请填写您的【服务项目】</b>\n（例：全身推拿 / 精油SPA / 正骨）", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] }, "HTML");
        return;

      } else if (field === 'RANKING_SERVICES') {
        // 🟢 上榜向导第3步：接收服务项目
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 服务项目只能是纯文本，请重新输入！", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] });
          return;
        }
        const rkDr3 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
        let rkD3 = rkDr3 ? JSON.parse(rkDr3) : {};
        rkD3.services = text;
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId, JSON.stringify(rkD3));
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_RANKING_TYPE');
        sendMessage(chatId, "✅ 服务项目已保存！\n\n✨ <b>第4步 / 共6步：请填写您的【类型特色】</b>\n（例：御姐型 / 邻家清纯 / 技术派）", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] }, "HTML");
        return;

      } else if (field === 'RANKING_TYPE') {
        // 🟢 上榜向导第4步：接收类型特色，并初始化照片数组
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 类型特色只能是纯文本，请重新输入！", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] });
          return;
        }
        const rkDr4 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
        let rkD4 = rkDr4 ? JSON.parse(rkDr4) : {};
        rkD4.type_feature = text;
        rkD4.photos = [];
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId, JSON.stringify(rkD4));
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_RANKING_PHOTOS');
        sendMessage(chatId, "✅ 类型特色已保存！\n\n📸 <b>第5步 / 共6步：请逐张上传【5张照片】</b>\n\n每发一张机器人会提示进度，发满5张后自动引导您上传课表。\n（若照片不足5张，可点提示中的跳过按钮）", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] }, "HTML");
        return;

      } else if (field === 'RANKING_PHOTOS') {
        // 🟢 上榜向导第5步：接收照片（累积至5张后自动进入课表）
        const rkDr5 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
        if (!rkDr5) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "❌ 申请数据已丢失，请重新点击【📋申请上榜】按钮开始提交。");
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          return;
        }
        let rkD5 = JSON.parse(rkDr5);
        if (!rkD5.photos) rkD5.photos = [];
        if (photo && photo.length > 0) {
          rkD5.photos.push(photo[photo.length - 1].file_id);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId, JSON.stringify(rkD5));
          const cnt = rkD5.photos.length;
          if (cnt >= 5) {
            // 5张已满，自动进入课表上传
            PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_RANKING_SCHEDULE');
            sendMessage(chatId, `✅ <b>第 5/5 张照片已收到！</b>\n\n📅 <b>请发送您的【课表图片】（1张）：</b>\n\n若无课表可点击下方跳过。`,
              { inline_keyboard: [
                [{ text: "⏭️ 跳过课表，进入下一步", callback_data: "RANK_SKIP_SCHEDULE" }],
                [{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]
              ] }, "HTML");
          } else {
            sendMessage(chatId, `✅ <b>第 ${cnt}/5 张照片已收到！</b>  还需 ${5 - cnt} 张，请继续发送。`,
              { inline_keyboard: [
                [{ text: `⏭️ 跳过剩余照片，去上传课表`, callback_data: "RANK_PHOTOS_DONE" }],
                [{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]
              ] }, "HTML");
          }
        } else {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 格式错误！请直接发送<b>图片</b>（不支持视频/文件）。", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] }, "HTML");
        }
        return;

      } else if (field === 'RANKING_SCHEDULE') {
        // 🟢 上榜向导第5步(续)：接收课表图片，然后进入标签选择
        const rkDr6 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
        if (!rkDr6) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "❌ 申请数据已丢失，请重新点击【📋申请上榜】按钮开始提交。");
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          return;
        }
        if (photo && photo.length > 0) {
          let rkD6 = JSON.parse(rkDr6);
          rkD6.schedule = photo[photo.length - 1].file_id;
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId, JSON.stringify(rkD6));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_RANKING_TAGS');
          sendRankingTagsPanel(chatId, userId, null);
        } else {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 格式错误！课表请直接发送<b>图片</b>，或点击下方跳过。",
            { inline_keyboard: [
              [{ text: "⏭️ 跳过课表，进入下一步", callback_data: "RANK_SKIP_SCHEDULE" }],
              [{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]
            ] }, "HTML");
        }
        return;

      } else if (field === 'RANKING_TAGS') {
        // 🟢 上榜向导最终步：接收手动输入的自定义标签，并直接提交
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 请发送纯文本标签，或点击上方标签按钮选择后点击提交！", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] });
          return;
        }
        const rkDr7 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
        if (!rkDr7) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "❌ 申请数据已丢失，请重新点击【📋申请上榜】按钮开始提交。");
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          return;
        }
        let rkD7 = JSON.parse(rkDr7);
        // 合并手动标签与已选标签
        const manualTags = text.split(/[\s,，]+/).map(t => t.replace(/#/g,'').trim()).filter(t => t.length > 0);
        rkD7.tags = [...new Set([...(rkD7.tags || []), ...manualTags])];
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId, JSON.stringify(rkD7));
        submitRankingApplication(chatId, userId, message.from);
        return;

      } else if (field.startsWith('RANKING_REJ_REASON_')) {
        // 🟢 新增：接收管理员填写的上榜申请拒绝理由
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 拒绝理由只能是纯文本，请重新输入！");
          return;
        }
        const rrParts = field.replace('RANKING_REJ_REASON_', '').split('_');
        const rrSubmissionId = rrParts[0]; // 例：rnk1750000000000
        const rrSubmitterId = rrParts[1];  // 例：8990999607
        const rrSub = readFromFirebase(`ranking_submissions/${rrSubmissionId}`);
        if (!rrSub || rrSub.status !== 'pending') {
          sendMessage(chatId, "❌ 该申请已不存在或已被其他管理员处理。");
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          return;
        }
        writeToFirebase(`ranking_submissions/${rrSubmissionId}/status`, 'rejected');
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
        sendMessage(chatId, "✅ 拒绝理由已成功发送给该用户！");
        sendMessage(rrSubmitterId, `❌ <b>上榜申请未通过审核</b>\n\n您提交的上榜申请（编号：<code>${rrSubmissionId}</code>）未通过管理员审核。\n\n📝 <b>管理员给出的拒绝理由：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(text)}</code>`, null, "HTML");
        return;

      // === 🟢 新增：接收强制关注频道的设置输入 ===
      } else if (field === 'FORCESUB_CHANNEL') {
        const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
        
        // 允许管理员输入“关闭”来彻底停用该功能
        if (text === '关闭') {
          writeToFirebase('system_config/force_sub_channel', null);
          writeToFirebase('system_config/force_sub_link', null);
          CacheService.getScriptCache().remove('global_force_sub'); // 清除缓存，立刻放行
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          sendMessage(chatId, "✅ <b>强制关注频道功能已彻底关闭，群员限制已解除。</b>", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          return;
        }
        
        const parts = text.split('\n');
        if (parts.length < 2) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ <b>格式错误</b>：请严格分两行输入。\n第一行：频道ID (如 -100123456789)\n第二行：频道加入链接 (如 https://t.me/xxx)\n\n👉 请重新输入，或回复 <code>关闭</code> 来停用此功能。", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          return;
        }
        
        const channelId = parts[0].trim();
        const inviteLink = parts[1].trim();
        
        // 校验机器人是否在目标频道内，若不在，群员即便加了频道，机器人也无法验证
        try {
          const checkRes = sendTelegramMessage("getChatMember", { chat_id: channelId, user_id: BOT_TOKEN.split(':')[0] });
          const resData = JSON.parse(checkRes.getContentText());
          if (!resData.ok || !['administrator', 'creator'].includes(resData.result.status)) {
            sendMessage(chatId, `⚠️ <b>连通校验失败</b>：机器人不在目标频道内，或未被设为管理员。\n报错原因为：<code>${resData.description}</code>\n\n👉 为能准确验证群员身份，请先将本机器人拉入该频道并设为管理员，然后再次重新输入。`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return;
          }
        } catch(e) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 校验请求异常，请检查网络通信状态或频道 ID 格式是否有误。", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          return;
        }
        
        writeToFirebase('system_config/force_sub_channel', channelId);
        writeToFirebase('system_config/force_sub_link', inviteLink);
        CacheService.getScriptCache().remove('global_force_sub'); // 强制刷新缓存
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
        sendMessage(chatId, `✅ <b>强制关注频道已成功开启！</b>\n\n📌 频道ID：<code>${channelId}</code>\n🔗 链接：${inviteLink}\n\n所有普通群员及私聊用户，必须加入该频道后才能发言操作。若用户中途退群，底层缓存将在 5 分钟内识别并重新封锁其发言权限。`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
        return;
       // 🟢 防垃圾：接收管理员添加违禁词
      } else if (field === "AS_FW_ADD") {
        if (!text || !String(text).trim()) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 请发送有效的违禁词文本。");
          return;
        }
        const parts = String(text).split(/[\n,，]+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        const cfg = getAntispamConfig();
        const words = (cfg.forbidden_words.words || []).slice();
        parts.forEach(p => {
          if (words.indexOf(p) === -1) words.push(p);
        });
        saveAntispamSection("forbidden_words", { words: words });
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty("state_" + userId);
        sendMessage(
          chatId,
          `✅ 已添加 ${parts.length} 个词，当前共 ${words.length} 个。`,
          { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回词库", callback_data: "AS_FW_MANAGE" }]] },
          "HTML"
        );
        return;
      // 🟢 修复：彻底移除遗留的 MEDIA (评分上传) 状态拦截，并为关键词向导 (KW_) 补充放行条件，防止跳入错乱逻辑
            } else if ((field.startsWith('LB_') || field.startsWith('WELCOME_') || field.startsWith('STARTMSG_') || field.startsWith('KW_') || field.startsWith('MENU_') || field === 'CHECKIN_KEYWORDS' || field === 'MENU_PROMPT') && !field.startsWith('LB_EDITBTNSONLY_')) {
        // 🟢 核心修复：拦截发榜向导输入，并严格排除 LB_EDIT_ 防止状态路由冲突导致接收新文案时“没反应”
        const lbDraftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_lb_' + userId);
        let lbDraft = lbDraftStr ? JSON.parse(lbDraftStr) : {};
        const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };

        // 🟢 核心修复新增：统一处理定时发布(LB)向导验证失败时的消息清理，防止错误提示与旧面板无限堆叠
        const sendLbError = (errorMsg) => {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
            const prevErrId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
            if (prevErrId) { try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevErrId }); } catch(e) {} }
            const errRes = sendMessage(chatId, errorMsg, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            try {
                const resData = JSON.parse(errRes.getContentText());
                if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
            } catch(e) {}
        };

        if (field === 'LB_TARGET') {
          if (!text || !/^-?\d+$/.test(text.trim())) {
             sendLbError("⚠️ ID 格式不正确，请输入纯数字（带 -100 前缀）。");
             return;
          }
          
          const targetChatId = text.trim();
          
          // 🟢 核心新增：提取机器人自身 ID，调用 API 校验是否在目标群/频道且具备管理员权限
          const botId = BOT_TOKEN.split(':')[0];
          const checkRes = sendTelegramMessage("getChatMember", { chat_id: targetChatId, user_id: botId });
          
          try {
            const resData = JSON.parse(checkRes.getContentText());
            
            // 1. 目标不存在、没加群或前缀写错的情况
            if (!resData.ok) {
               sendLbError(`⚠️ <b>目标校验失败！</b>\n原因为：<code>${resData.description}</code>\n\n👉 <b>排查建议：</b>\n1. 检查 ID 是否准确且包含 <code>-100</code> 前缀。\n2. 确保机器人已经被拉入该群组或频道内。`);
               return;
            }
            
            // 2. 身份校验情况：必须为 administrator (管理员) 或 creator (群主)
            const botStatus = resData.result.status;
            if (!['administrator', 'creator'].includes(botStatus)) {
               sendLbError(`⚠️ <b>权限校验未通过！</b>\n当前机器人在目标内的身份仅为：<code>${botStatus}</code>\n\n👉 为保证定时发送、自动删除及置顶等操作均可成功执行，请先前往该群组/频道将机器人设为<b>【管理员】</b>后，再次输入 ID。`);
               return;
            }
          } catch (err) {
             sendLbError("⚠️ 校验请求异常，请检查机器人网络通信状态或稍后重试。");
             return;
          }

          // ✅ 校验全部通过，执行原有流程
          try {
             const chatRes = sendTelegramMessage("getChat", { chat_id: targetChatId });
             const chatData = JSON.parse(chatRes.getContentText());
             if (chatData.ok && chatData.result && chatData.result.title) {
                 writeToFirebase(`chat_names/${targetChatId}`, chatData.result.title);
             }
          } catch(e) {}

          lbDraft.target = targetChatId;
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_LB_CONTENT');
          
          // 🟢 自动删除管理员发出的 ID 消息及机器人的上一步提示
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const nextRes = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 权限与目标校验通过！已设为：<code>${lbDraft.target}</code>\n\n👉 <b>第二步：请发送要发布的【文本】或【图片/视频】（若带媒体可附带文字说明）。</b>`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          try {
             const resData = JSON.parse(nextRes.getContentText());
             if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
          } catch(e) {}
        }

       // 🟢 新增：欢迎消息配置 - 接收图文/视频
        else if (field === 'WELCOME_CONTENT') {
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          let wlDraft = {};
          const formattedText = parseTelegramHtml(message);

          if (photo && photo.length > 0) {
            wlDraft.mediaId = photo[photo.length - 1].file_id;
            wlDraft.mediaType = 'photo';
            wlDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else if (video) {
            wlDraft.mediaId = video.file_id;
            wlDraft.mediaType = 'video';
            wlDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else if (text) {
            wlDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else {
            // 🟢 修复：与下方 WELCOME_BUTTONS 步骤的报错处理对齐——同样清理输入与旧提示，并接力追踪新提示ID
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
            const prevBadMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
            if (prevBadMsgId) { try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevBadMsgId }); } catch(e) {} }
            const errRes = sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 不支持的消息类型，请发送纯文本、图片或视频。", cancelKeyboard);
            try {
              const resData = JSON.parse(errRes.getContentText());
              if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
            } catch(e) {}
            return;
          }
          
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_welcome_' + userId, JSON.stringify(wlDraft));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_WELCOME_BUTTONS');
          
          // 🟢 新增：自动删除管理员发出的图文内容及机器人的上一步提示
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const nextRes = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 内容已暂存。\n\n👉 <b>第二步：请配置内联按钮。</b>\n\n输入格式：\n<code>按钮名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选) | 按钮2 - 链接</code>\n\n颜色样式（点击下方标签可直接复制）：\n• <code>primary</code>  🔵 蓝色\n• <code>success</code>  🟢 绿色\n• <code>danger</code>   🔴 红色\n\n示例：<code>点击加入 - t.me/xxx - primary</code>\n*(若不需要按钮请直接回复"跳过")*`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          
          // 记录新的提示消息 ID
          try {
             const resData = JSON.parse(nextRes.getContentText());
             if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }

        // 🟢 新增：欢迎消息配置 - 接收按钮（含链接合法性拦截）
        else if (field === 'WELCOME_BUTTONS') {
          const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_welcome_' + userId);
          if (!draftStr) { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 状态过期，请重试。"); return; }
          let wlDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };

          if (text.trim() !== '跳过') {
            const rawText = text.trim();
            let isValid = true;
            let errorMsg = "";

            const rows = rawText.split('\n');
            outerLoop:
            for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
              const btns = rows[i].split('|');
              for (let j = 0; j < btns.length; j++) {
                const btnText = btns[j].trim();
                if (!btnText) continue;

                const parts = btnText.split('-');
                if (parts.length < 2) {
                  isValid = false;
                  errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>格式错误</b>：第 ${i+1} 行的按钮 <code>${escapeHtml(btnText)}</code> 缺少 "<code>-</code>" 分隔符。\n\n👉 请按照 <code>按钮名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选)</code> 格式重新输入，或回复"跳过"。`;
                  break outerLoop;
                }

                let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
                let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                if (lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                  urlParts.pop();
                  lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                }
                if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
                  urlParts.pop();
                  lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                  if (lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) urlParts.pop();
                }
                const urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();

                if (/\s/.test(urlObj)) {
                  isValid = false;
                  errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>链接无效</b>：按钮链接 <code>${escapeHtml(urlObj)}</code> 中包含非法空格。\n\n👉 链接必须是连续字符，请检查后重新输入。`;
                  break outerLoop;
                }
                const lowerUrl = urlObj.toLowerCase();
                if (!lowerUrl.startsWith('http://') && !lowerUrl.startsWith('https://') && !lowerUrl.startsWith('tg://')) {
                  if (!lowerUrl.includes('.')) {
                    isValid = false;
                    errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>链接无效</b>：<code>${escapeHtml(urlObj)}</code> 格式不符合要求。\n\n👉 请输入完整网址 (如 t.me/xxx 或 baidu.com) 或 tg:// 链接。\n注：不支持直接填写 @用户名，请改为 <code>t.me/用户名</code> 格式。`;
                    break outerLoop;
                  }
                }
              }
            }

            if (!isValid) {
              // 🟢 新增：错误时也自动删除用户输入和旧提示
              try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
              const prevErrId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
              if (prevErrId) { try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevErrId }); } catch(e) {} }
              
              const errRes = sendMessage(chatId, errorMsg, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
              try {
                const resData = JSON.parse(errRes.getContentText());
                if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
              } catch(e) {}
              return; // 保留 WAITING_FOR_WELCOME_BUTTONS 状态，让用户重新填写
            }
            wlDraft.buttonsRaw = rawText;
          } else {
            wlDraft.buttonsRaw = "";
          }

          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_welcome_' + userId, JSON.stringify(wlDraft));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_WELCOME_TIME');
          const timeKeyboard = {
            inline_keyboard: [
              [{ text: "🕒 1分钟后删除", callback_data: "WELCOME_DEL_TIME_60" }, { text: "🕒 5分钟后删除", callback_data: "WELCOME_DEL_TIME_300" }],
              [{ text: "🕒 10分钟后删除", callback_data: "WELCOME_DEL_TIME_600" }, { text: "🕒 1小时后删除", callback_data: "WELCOME_DEL_TIME_3600" }],
              [{ text: "⏭️ 跳过（不设倒计时，仅替换上一条）", callback_data: "WELCOME_DEL_TIME_0" }],
              [{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
            ]
          };

          // 🟢 新增：自动删除管理员发出的按钮配置消息及机器人的上一步提示
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId2 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId2) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId2 }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const nextRes2 = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 按钮已配置。\n\n👉 <b>可选：设置欢迎消息自动删除时间</b>\n\n• 选时间：到点后自动删掉本条欢迎\n• 点「跳过」：不设倒计时，仅在下一位新人进群时替换上一条（防刷屏）\n\n*(本步可跳过，不影响欢迎功能开启)*`, timeKeyboard, "HTML");
          
          try {
             const resData = JSON.parse(nextRes2.getContentText());
             if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }

        // 🚀 新增：/start 消息配置 - 接收图文/视频
        else if (field === 'STARTMSG_CONTENT') {
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          let smDraft = {};
          const formattedText = parseTelegramHtml(message);

          if (photo && photo.length > 0) {
            smDraft.mediaId = photo[photo.length - 1].file_id;
            smDraft.mediaType = 'photo';
            smDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else if (video) {
            smDraft.mediaId = video.file_id;
            smDraft.mediaType = 'video';
            smDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else if (text) {
            smDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
            const prevBadMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
            if (prevBadMsgId) { try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevBadMsgId }); } catch(e) {} }
            const errRes = sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 不支持的消息类型，请发送纯文本、图片或视频。", cancelKeyboard);
            try {
              const resData = JSON.parse(errRes.getContentText());
              if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
            } catch(e) {}
            return;
          }

          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_startmsg_' + userId, JSON.stringify(smDraft));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_STARTMSG_BUTTONS');

          // 🟢 自动删除管理员发出的图文内容及机器人的上一步提示
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const nextRes = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 内容已暂存。\n\n👉 <b>第二步：请配置内联按钮。</b>\n\n输入格式：\n<code>按钮名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选) | 按钮2 - 链接</code>\n\n颜色样式（点击下方标签可直接复制）：\n• <code>primary</code>  🔵 蓝色\n• <code>success</code>  🟢 绿色\n• <code>danger</code>   🔴 红色\n\n示例：<code>点击加入 - t.me/xxx - primary</code>\n*(若不需要按钮请直接回复"跳过")*`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");

          try {
             const resData = JSON.parse(nextRes.getContentText());
             if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }

        // 🚀 新增：/start 消息配置 - 接收内联按钮（含链接合法性拦截，支持"跳过"）
        else if (field === 'STARTMSG_BUTTONS') {
          const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_startmsg_' + userId);
          if (!draftStr) { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 状态过期，请重试。"); return; }
          let smDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };

          if (text.trim() !== '跳过') {
            const rawText = text.trim();
            let isValid = true;
            let errorMsg = "";

            const rows = rawText.split('\n');
            outerLoop:
            for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
              const btns = rows[i].split('|');
              for (let j = 0; j < btns.length; j++) {
                const btnText = btns[j].trim();
                if (!btnText) continue;

                const parts = btnText.split('-');
                if (parts.length < 2) {
                  isValid = false;
                  errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>格式错误</b>：第 ${i+1} 行的按钮 <code>${escapeHtml(btnText)}</code> 缺少 "<code>-</code>" 分隔符。\n\n👉 请按照 <code>按钮名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选)</code> 格式重新输入，或回复"跳过"。`;
                  break outerLoop;
                }

                let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
                let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
                  urlParts.pop();
                }
                const urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();

                if (/\s/.test(urlObj)) {
                  isValid = false;
                  errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>链接无效</b>：按钮链接 <code>${escapeHtml(urlObj)}</code> 中包含非法空格。\n\n👉 链接必须是连续字符，请检查后重新输入。`;
                  break outerLoop;
                }
                const lowerUrl = urlObj.toLowerCase();
                if (!lowerUrl.startsWith('http://') && !lowerUrl.startsWith('https://') && !lowerUrl.startsWith('tg://')) {
                  if (!lowerUrl.includes('.')) {
                    isValid = false;
                    errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>链接无效</b>：<code>${escapeHtml(urlObj)}</code> 格式不符合要求。\n\n👉 请输入完整网址 (如 t.me/xxx 或 baidu.com) 或 tg:// 链接。\n注：不支持直接填写 @用户名，请改为 <code>t.me/用户名</code> 格式。`;
                    break outerLoop;
                  }
                }
              }
            }

            if (!isValid) {
              try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
              const prevErrId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
              if (prevErrId) { try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevErrId }); } catch(e) {} }

              const errRes = sendMessage(chatId, errorMsg, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
              try {
                const resData = JSON.parse(errRes.getContentText());
                if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
              } catch(e) {}
              return; // 保留 WAITING_FOR_STARTMSG_BUTTONS 状态，让用户重新填写
            }
            smDraft.buttonsRaw = rawText;
          } else {
            smDraft.buttonsRaw = "";
          }

          // 🟢 跳过或校验通过后直接保存生效，/start 消息无需像群欢迎语一样再选择自动删除时间
          writeToFirebase('system_config/start_msg', smDraft);

          // 清理用户本次输入与上一步提示消息
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId2 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId2) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId2 }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_startmsg_' + userId);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);

          const backKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          sendMessage(chatId, `✅ <b>/start 消息已配置成功！</b>\n\n此后用户发送 /start 时将展示您刚配置的内容${smDraft.buttonsRaw ? '与按钮' : ''}。`, backKeyboard, "HTML");
          return;
        }

        // 🚀 新增：关键词配置向导 1 - 接收关键词与匹配模式
        else if (field === 'KW_INFO') {
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          if (!text || !text.includes('-')) {
            sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ <b>格式错误</b>：请使用 <code>-</code> 分隔关键词和匹配模式。\n\n示例：<code>客服 - 模糊</code>", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return;
          }
          
          const parts = text.split('-');
          const kwText = parts[0].trim();
          const kwMode = parts[1].trim() === '模糊' ? 'fuzzy' : 'exact';
          
          let kwDraft = { keyword: kwText, mode: kwMode };
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_kw_' + userId, JSON.stringify(kwDraft));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_KW_CONTENT');
          
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevKw1 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('kw_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevKw1) {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: Number(prevKw1) }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const kwStep2Res = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 关键词 <code>${escapeHtml(kwText)}</code> (${kwMode === 'fuzzy' ? '模糊匹配' : '精准匹配'}) 已暂存。\n\n👉 <b>第二步 (2/4)：请发送回复的【内容】。</b>\n\n支持纯文本、图片或视频文件（发图文时请附带说明文字）。`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          try {
            const resData = JSON.parse(kwStep2Res.getContentText());
            if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('kw_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, String(resData.result.message_id));
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }

        // 🚀 新增：关键词配置向导 2 - 接收图文/视频
        else if (field === 'KW_CONTENT') {
          const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_kw_' + userId);
          if (!draftStr) { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 状态过期，请重试。"); return; }
          let kwDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          
          const formattedText = parseTelegramHtml(message);

          if (photo && photo.length > 0) {
            kwDraft.mediaId = photo[photo.length - 1].file_id;
            kwDraft.mediaType = 'photo';
            kwDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else if (video) {
            kwDraft.mediaId = video.file_id;
            kwDraft.mediaType = 'video';
            kwDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else if (text) {
            kwDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
            sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 不支持的消息类型，请发送纯文本、图片或视频。", cancelKeyboard);
            return;
          }

          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_kw_' + userId, JSON.stringify(kwDraft));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_KW_BUTTONS');
          
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevKw2 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('kw_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevKw2) {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: Number(prevKw2) }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const kwStep3Res = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 回复内容已接收。\n\n👉 <b>第三步 (3/4)：请配置内联按钮。</b>\n\n输入格式：\n<code>按钮名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选) | 按钮2 - 链接</code>\n\n颜色样式：\n• <code>primary</code>  🔵 蓝色\n• <code>success</code>  🟢 绿色\n• <code>danger</code>   🔴 红色\n\n*(若不需要按钮，请直接回复「跳过」)*`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          try {
            const resData = JSON.parse(kwStep3Res.getContentText());
            if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('kw_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, String(resData.result.message_id));
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }

        // 🚀 新增：关键词配置向导 3 - 接收内联按钮
        else if (field === 'KW_BUTTONS') {
          const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_kw_' + userId);
          if (!draftStr) { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 状态过期，请重试。"); return; }
          let kwDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);

          if (text.trim() !== '跳过') {
             kwDraft.buttonsRaw = text.trim();
          } else {
             kwDraft.buttonsRaw = "";
          }

          // 将数据暂存，进入时间配置状态
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_kw_' + userId, JSON.stringify(kwDraft));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_KW_TIME');

          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevKw3 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('kw_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevKw3) {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: Number(prevKw3) }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          const kwStep4Res = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 按钮已配置。\n\n👉 <b>第四步 (4/4)：请设置自动删除时间（0-30 的纯数字，代表分钟）。</b>\n\n*(如需永久保留不删除，请直接回复数字 0)*`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          try {
            const resData = JSON.parse(kwStep4Res.getContentText());
            if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('kw_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, String(resData.result.message_id));
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }

        // 🚀 新增：关键词配置向导 4 - 接收自动删除时间并落库
        else if (field === 'KW_TIME') {
          const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_kw_' + userId);
          if (!draftStr) { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 状态过期，请重试。"); return; }
          let kwDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
          
          const timeVal = parseInt(text.trim());
          if (isNaN(timeVal) || timeVal < 0 || timeVal > 30) {
              const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
              sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ <b>输入无效</b>：请输入 0 到 30 之间的纯数字（分钟）。", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
              return;
          }
          
          kwDraft.autoDeleteMinutes = timeVal;

          // 安全转码作为节点 Key 落库，防止特殊字符报错 (编辑模式下优先复用原 Key 保持索引干净)
          const safeKey = kwDraft.safeKey || Utilities.base64Encode(kwDraft.keyword, Utilities.Charset.UTF_8).replace(/=/g, '');
          delete kwDraft.safeKey; // 落库前清洗冗余的系统字段
          writeToFirebase(`system_config/keyword_replies/${safeKey}`, kwDraft);

          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevKw4 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('kw_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevKw4) {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: Number(prevKw4) }); } catch(e) {}
          }
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_kw_' + userId);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('kw_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);

          const backKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          sendMessage(chatId, `✅ <b>关键词回复已配置成功！</b>\n\n📌 触发词：<code>${escapeHtml(kwDraft.keyword)}</code>\n🎯 模式：${kwDraft.mode === 'exact' ? '精准匹配' : '模糊匹配'}\n🕒 自动删除：${timeVal > 0 ? timeVal + ' 分钟后' : '不删除'}`, backKeyboard, "HTML");
          return;
        }
        // 🟢 接收打卡触发词配置
        else if (field === 'CHECKIN_KEYWORDS') {
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW" }]] };
          let parts = [];
          if (text && text.includes('|')) {
            parts = text.split('|').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
          } else if (text) {
            parts = text.split(/\n+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
          }
          if (parts.length !== 3) {
            sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 请按格式发送三个词：\n<code>在岗词\n下班词\n休息词</code>\n或：<code>在岗词 | 下班词 | 休息词</code>", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return;
          }
          const [onlineKw, offlineKw, leaveKw] = parts;
          if (onlineKw === offlineKw || onlineKw === leaveKw || offlineKw === leaveKw) {
            sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 三个触发词不能相同，请重新发送。", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return;
          }
          writeToFirebase('system_config/checkin_keywords', {
            online: onlineKw,
            offline: offlineKw,
            leave: leaveKw
          });
         invalidateCheckInKeywordCache();
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const backKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回设置", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW" }]] };
          sendMessage(chatId,
            `✅ <b>打卡触发词已更新</b>\n\n🟢 在岗：<code>${escapeHtml(onlineKw)}</code>\n🔴 下班：<code>${escapeHtml(offlineKw)}</code>\n🟡 休息：<code>${escapeHtml(leaveKw)}</code>`,
            backKb, "HTML");
          return;
        }
                // 🟢 接收 /menu 启动提示语
        else if (field === 'MENU_PROMPT') {
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT" }]] };
          if (!text || !String(text).trim()) {
            sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 提示语不能为空，请重新发送。", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return;
          }
          const newPrompt = String(text).trim();
          if (newPrompt.length > 2000) {
            sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 过长（建议 ≤2000 字），请精简后重发。", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return;
          }
          writeToFirebase('system_config/menu_prompt_text', newPrompt);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);

          // 🟢 删除用户刚发的输入，保持屏幕干净
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch (e) {}

          const panelKb = { inline_keyboard: [
            [{ text: "✏️ 修改提示语", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT_EDIT" }],
            [{ text: "🔄 恢复默认", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT_RESET" }],
            [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
          ]};
          const panelText =
            `✅ <b>/menu 提示语已更新</b>\n\n` +
            `用户发送 /menu 时，与底部键盘一起发出的说明文字：\n\n` +
            `<code>${escapeHtml(newPrompt)}</code>\n\n` +
            `<i>支持多行；留空无效，将继续使用默认文案。</i>`;

          // 🟢 优先原地改回设置面板（无多余成功气泡）
          const promptMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('menu_prompt_edit_msg_id_' + userId);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('menu_prompt_edit_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (promptMsgId) {
            try {
              editMessageText(chatId, Number(promptMsgId), panelText, panelKb, "HTML");
              return;
            } catch (e) {}
          }
          // 兜底：引导消息已不在时再发一条
          sendMessage(chatId, panelText, panelKb, "HTML");
          return;
        }
        // 🟢 菜单按钮向导 1：按钮文字
        else if (field === 'MENU_LABEL') {
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          if (!text || !text.trim()) {
            sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 请发送有效的按钮文字。", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return;
          }
          // 🟢 解析可选颜色：支持「文字 - primary」或「文字 primary」
          let raw = text.trim().replace(/[－—–]/g, '-');
          let label = raw;
          let btnStyle = "";
          const styleMatch = raw.match(/^(.*?)\s*[- ]\s*(primary|success|danger)\s*$/i);
          if (styleMatch) {
            label = styleMatch[1].trim();
            btnStyle = styleMatch[2].toLowerCase();
          }
          if (!label) {
            sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 请发送有效的按钮文字。", cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return;
          }
          // 检查重名
          const allMenu = readFromFirebase('system_config/menu_buttons') || {};
          for (let k in allMenu) {
            if (allMenu[k] && allMenu[k].label === label) {
              sendMessage(chatId, `⚠️ 按钮文字 <code>${escapeHtml(label)}</code> 已存在，请换一个。`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
              return;
            }
          }
          const order = Object.keys(allMenu).length + 1;
          const draftObj = { label: label, order: order };
          if (btnStyle) draftObj.style = btnStyle;
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_menu_' + userId, JSON.stringify(draftObj));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_MENU_CONTENT');

          // 🟢 清理：删用户输入 + 上一条机器人提示
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevPrompt1 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('menu_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevPrompt1) {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: Number(prevPrompt1) }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const styleHint = btnStyle ? `\n🎨 颜色：<code>${btnStyle}</code>` : "";
          const step2Res = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 按钮文字 <code>${escapeHtml(label)}</code> 已暂存。${styleHint}\n\n👉 <b>第二步 (2/4)：请发送点击后弹出的【内容】。</b>\n\n支持纯文本、图片或视频（图文可附说明）。`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          try {
            const resData = JSON.parse(step2Res.getContentText());
            if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('menu_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, String(resData.result.message_id));
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }
        // 🟢 菜单按钮向导 2：内容
                else if (field === 'MENU_CONTENT') {
          const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_menu_' + userId);
          if (!draftStr) { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 状态过期，请重试。"); return; }
          let mDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          const isKeep = text && String(text).trim() === '保持';
          const isEditMode = !!mDraft.safeKey;

          if (isKeep && isEditMode) {
            // 保持草稿中已有 text/media，不覆盖
          } else {
            const formattedText = parseTelegramHtml(message);
            if (photo && photo.length > 0) {
              mDraft.mediaId = photo[photo.length - 1].file_id;
              mDraft.mediaType = 'photo';
              mDraft.text = formattedText;
            } else if (video) {
              mDraft.mediaId = video.file_id;
              mDraft.mediaType = 'video';
              mDraft.text = formattedText;
            } else if (text) {
              // 新建或编辑换文：纯文字时清掉旧媒体，避免残留
              mDraft.text = formattedText;
              if (!isEditMode || !isKeep) {
                mDraft.mediaId = null;
                mDraft.mediaType = null;
              }
            } else {
              sendMessage(chatId,
                isEditMode
                  ? "⚠️ 请发送纯文本、图片或视频，或回复 <code>保持</code> 沿用原文。"
                  : "⚠️ 请发送纯文本、图片或视频。",
                cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
              return;
            }
          }
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_menu_' + userId, JSON.stringify(mDraft));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_MENU_BUTTONS');

          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevPrompt2 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('menu_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevPrompt2) {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: Number(prevPrompt2) }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const curBtn = (mDraft.buttonsRaw || "").trim();
          const btnShow = curBtn
            ? (curBtn.length > 600 ? curBtn.slice(0, 600) + "…" : curBtn)
            : "（无内联按钮）";
          const step3Kb = isEditMode
            ? { inline_keyboard: [
                [{ text: "📌 保持原按钮", callback_data: "MENU_KEEP_BUTTONS" }],
                [{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
              ] }
            : cancelKeyboard;
          const step3Text = isEditMode
            ? `✅ ${isKeep ? "已保持原内容" : "内容已暂存"}。\n\n👉 <b>第三步 (3/4)：内联按钮（可选）</b>\n\n` +
              `📄 <b>当前按钮配置</b>（可复制）：\n<code>${escapeHtml(btnShow)}</code>\n\n` +
              `格式：\n<code>按钮名 - 链接 - 颜色(可选) | 按钮2 - 链接</code>\n` +
              `颜色：<code>primary</code> / <code>success</code> / <code>danger</code>\n\n` +
              `发送新配置，或点「保持原按钮」/ 回复 <code>保持</code>；清空请回 <code>跳过</code>。`
            : `✅ 内容已暂存。\n\n👉 <b>第三步 (3/4)：请配置内联按钮（可选）。</b>\n\n格式：\n<code>按钮名 - 链接 - 颜色(可选) | 按钮2 - 链接</code>\n\n颜色样式（点击下方标签可直接复制）：\n• <code>primary</code>  🔵 蓝色\n• <code>success</code>  🟢 绿色\n• <code>danger</code>   🔴 红色\n\n示例：<code>联系客服 - t.me/xxx - primary</code>\n\n*(不需要按钮请直接回复「跳过」)*`;

          const step3Res = sendMessage(chatId, step3Text, step3Kb, "HTML");
          try {
            const resData = JSON.parse(step3Res.getContentText());
            if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('menu_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, String(resData.result.message_id));
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }
        // 🟢 菜单按钮向导 3：内联按钮（可跳过）→ 进入第 4 步自动删除时间
                else if (field === 'MENU_BUTTONS') {
          const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_menu_' + userId);
          if (!draftStr) { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 状态过期，请重试。"); return; }
          let mDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          const isEditMode = !!mDraft.safeKey;
          const t = text ? String(text).trim() : "";

          if (t === '保持' && isEditMode) {
            // 保持原 buttonsRaw
          } else if (t && t !== '跳过') {
            mDraft.buttonsRaw = t;
          } else {
            mDraft.buttonsRaw = "";
          }
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_menu_' + userId, JSON.stringify(mDraft));
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_MENU_TIME');

          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevPrompt3 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('menu_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevPrompt3) {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: Number(prevPrompt3) }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const curMin = parseInt(mDraft.autoDeleteMinutes, 10) || 0;
          const step4Kb = isEditMode
            ? { inline_keyboard: [
                [{ text: "📌 保持原时间", callback_data: "MENU_KEEP_TIME" }],
                [{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
              ] }
            : cancelKeyboard;
          const step4Text = isEditMode
            ? `✅ ${t === '保持' ? "已保持原内联按钮" : "按钮已配置"}。\n\n👉 <b>第四步 (4/4)：自动删除时间</b>\n\n` +
              `📄 当前：<code>${curMin}</code> 分钟${curMin > 0 ? "（到期删回复+触发消息）" : "（关闭）"}\n\n` +
              `请发送 <code>0</code>～<code>60</code>，或点「保持原时间」/ 回复 <code>保持</code>。`
            : `✅ 按钮已配置。\n\n👉 <b>第四步 (4/4)：请设置自动删除时间。</b>\n\n` +
              `请发送 <code>0</code>～<code>60</code> 的纯数字（单位：分钟）。\n\n` +
              `• <code>0</code> = 关闭自动删除（永久保留）\n` +
              `• <code>1</code>～<code>60</code> = 到达分钟数后，机器人回复 + 用户触发消息一并删除\n\n` +
              `示例：<code>0</code> / <code>1</code> / <code>10</code> / <code>60</code>`;

          const step4Res = sendMessage(chatId, step4Text, step4Kb, "HTML");
          try {
            const resData = JSON.parse(step4Res.getContentText());
            if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('menu_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, String(resData.result.message_id));
          } catch(e) {}
          return;
        }

        // 🟢 菜单按钮向导 4：自动删除时间并落库（0=关，1～60 分钟）
        else if (field === 'MENU_TIME') {
          const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_menu_' + userId);
          if (!draftStr) { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 状态过期，请重试。"); return; }
          let mDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
          const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };

                    const isEditMode = !!mDraft.safeKey;
          const t = String(text || "").trim();
          let timeVal;
          if (t === '保持' && isEditMode) {
            timeVal = parseInt(mDraft.autoDeleteMinutes, 10) || 0;
          } else {
            timeVal = parseInt(t, 10);
            if (isNaN(timeVal) || timeVal < 0 || timeVal > 60) {
              sendMessage(chatId,
                isEditMode
                  ? "⚠️ <b>输入无效</b>：请输入 0～60，或回复 <code>保持</code> 沿用原时间。"
                  : "⚠️ <b>输入无效</b>：请输入 0 到 60 之间的纯数字（分钟）。\n<code>0</code>=关闭；<code>1</code>～<code>60</code>=自动删除。",
                cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
              return;
            }
          }
          mDraft.autoDeleteMinutes = timeVal;
          // 后面 safeKey / writeToFirebase / 成功提示逻辑保持不变

          // 编辑时沿用原 safeKey；新建用短固定 ID
          const safeKey = mDraft.safeKey || ('mb_' + new Date().getTime() + '_' + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000));
          delete mDraft.safeKey;
          writeToFirebase(`system_config/menu_buttons/${safeKey}`, mDraft);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_menu_' + userId);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);

          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevPrompt4 = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('menu_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevPrompt4) {
            try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: Number(prevPrompt4) }); } catch(e) {}
          }
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('menu_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);

          const backKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          sendMessage(chatId,
            `✅ <b>菜单按钮已保存！</b>\n\n` +
            `📌 按钮：<code>${escapeHtml(mDraft.label)}</code>\n` +
            `🕒 自动删除：${timeVal > 0 ? (timeVal + ' 分钟后（机器人回复 + 触发消息一并删除）') : '关闭（不删除）'}\n` +
            `用户发送 /menu 后即可看到该按钮。`,
            backKeyboard, "HTML");
          return;
        }

        else if (field === 'LB_CONTENT') {
          const formattedText = parseTelegramHtml(message); // 🟢 应用解析器

          if (photo && photo.length > 0) {
            lbDraft.mediaId = photo[photo.length - 1].file_id;
            lbDraft.mediaType = 'photo';
            lbDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else if (video) {
            lbDraft.mediaId = video.file_id;
            lbDraft.mediaType = 'video';
            lbDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else if (text) {
            lbDraft.text = formattedText;
          } else {
            sendLbError("⚠️ 不支持的消息类型，请发送纯文本、图片或视频。");
            return;
          }
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_LB_BUTTONS');
          
          // 🟢 自动删除管理员发送的内容消息及机器人的上一步提示
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const nextRes = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 内容已接收。\n\n👉 <b>第三步：请配置内联按钮（可选）。</b>\n\n输入格式：\n<code>按钮1名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选) - STATUS:老师名(可选) | 按钮2名称 - 链接</code>\n\n*(颜色支持：primary蓝, success绿, danger红)\n*(STATUS配置：绑定老师后，按钮将自动显示[🟢在岗/🔴下班/🟡休息]等状态并与打卡系统实时同步！)*\n\n示例：<code>联系苍老师 - t.me/xxx - primary - STATUS:苍老师</code>\n*(同一行用 | 隔开，不同行换行。若不需要按钮，请回复“跳过”)*`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          try {
             const resData = JSON.parse(nextRes.getContentText());
             if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
          } catch(e) {}
        }
        
        else if (field === 'LB_BUTTONS') {
          if (text.trim() !== '跳过') {
            // 🟢 自动纠正：全角竖线/横线 → 半角，避免「｜」被当成链接的一部分
            let normalizedText = text.trim()
              .replace(/[｜│]/g, '|')
              .replace(/[－—–]/g, '-');

            let isValid = true;
            let errorMsg = "";
            // 纠正后的干净配置（写入草稿，后续发送/编辑都用这版）
            let correctedRows = [];

            const rows = normalizedText.split('\n');
            for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
              const btns = rows[i].split('|');
              let correctedBtns = [];
              for (let j = 0; j < btns.length; j++) {
                const btnText = btns[j].trim();
                if (!btnText) continue; // 跳过用户多打的无用竖线
                
                const parts = btnText.split('-').map(p => p.trim()).filter(p => p !== '');
                if (parts.length < 2) {
                  isValid = false;
                  errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>格式错误</b>：第 ${i+1} 行的按钮 <code>${escapeHtml(btnText)}</code> 缺少 "<code>-</code>" 分隔符。\n\n👉 请按照 <code>按钮名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选)</code> 的格式重新输入，或回复“跳过”。`;
                  break;
                }
                
                const btnLabel = parts[0];
                let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
                let styleToken = "";
                let statusToken = "";
                
                // 从末尾剥离 STATUS: / 颜色，顺序兼容两种写法
                let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1] : "";
                if (lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                    statusToken = lastP;
                    urlParts.pop();
                    lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1] : "";
                }
                if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
                    styleToken = lastP.toLowerCase();
                    urlParts.pop();
                    lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1] : "";
                    if (lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                        statusToken = lastP;
                        urlParts.pop();
                    }
                }
                
                // 🟢 自动纠正：去掉链接内误打空格；@用户名 → t.me/用户名
                let urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '');
                if (urlObj.startsWith('@')) {
                  urlObj = 't.me/' + urlObj.slice(1);
                }
                
                if (!urlObj) {
                  isValid = false;
                  errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>链接无效</b>：第 ${i+1} 行按钮 <code>${escapeHtml(btnLabel)}</code> 缺少有效链接。`;
                  break;
                }
                
                // 纠正后仍含空白（理论上已清空）才判失败
                if (/\s/.test(urlObj)) {
                  isValid = false;
                  errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>链接无效</b>：按钮链接 <code>${escapeHtml(urlObj)}</code> 中包含非法空格。\n\n👉 链接必须是连续的有效字符，请检查后重新输入。`;
                  break;
                }
                
                const lowerUrl = urlObj.toLowerCase();
                if (!lowerUrl.startsWith('http://') && !lowerUrl.startsWith('https://') && !lowerUrl.startsWith('tg://')) {
                  if (!lowerUrl.includes('.')) {
                    isValid = false;
                    errorMsg = `⚠️ <b>链接无效</b>：<code>${escapeHtml(urlObj)}</code> 格式不符合要求。\n\n👉 请输入完整的网址 (如 t.me/xxx 或 baidu.com) 或 tg:// 协议链接。\n注：直接写 @用户名 已自动转为 t.me/用户名。`;
                    break;
                  }
                }

                // 拼回标准片段：名称 - 链接 [- 颜色] [- STATUS:老师]
                let piece = `${btnLabel} - ${urlObj}`;
                if (styleToken) piece += ` - ${styleToken}`;
                if (statusToken) piece += ` - ${statusToken}`;
                correctedBtns.push(piece);
              }
              if (!isValid) break;
              if (correctedBtns.length > 0) correctedRows.push(correctedBtns.join(' | '));
            }

            if (!isValid) {
              // 🟢 修复：调用独立排错清理程序，并拦截保持 WAITING_FOR_LB_BUTTONS 等待输入状态
              sendLbError(errorMsg);
              return; 
            }

            // 🟢 写入纠正后的配置，后续发送/同步都用干净数据
            lbDraft.buttonsRaw = correctedRows.join('\n');
          }
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_LB_TIME');
          
          // 🟢 自动删除管理员发送的按钮配置消息及机器人的上一步提示
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const nextRes = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 按钮已配置。\n\n👉 <b>第四步：请设置【推送时间】和【结束时间】</b>\n\n支持格式：\n1. 相对时间：<code>10分钟后, 2小时后</code> 或 <code>1天后, 3周后</code>\n2. 绝对时间：<code>2026-06-25 10:00, 2026-06-26 10:00</code>\n\n*(逗号左边是发送时间，右边是删除时间。若不删除，只需输入一个时间)*\n\n示例回复：<code>10分钟后, 2小时后</code>`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
          try {
             const resData = JSON.parse(nextRes.getContentText());
             if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
          } catch(e) {}
        }

        else if (field === 'LB_TIME') {
          const timeParts = text.split(/[,，]/).map(s => s.trim());
          const sendTime = parseCustomTime(timeParts[0]);
          if (!sendTime) {
             sendLbError("⚠️ 推送时间解析失败，请检查格式后重新输入。");
             return;
          }
          lbDraft.sendTime = sendTime;
          if (timeParts[1]) {
             const endTime = parseCustomTime(timeParts[1]);
             if (endTime) {
                 lbDraft.endTime = endTime;
             } else {
                 // 🛠️ 修复：结束时间解析失败必须报错拦截，不能静默清空后当成“永久有效”继续往下走
                 sendLbError("⚠️ 结束时间解析失败，请检查格式后重新输入（如 2小时后 / 1天后 或 2026-06-25 10:00）。\n若不需要自动删除，只输入发送时间即可，不要加逗号。");
                 return;
             }
          } else {
             delete lbDraft.endTime; // 🟢 核心修复：如果本次只输入了发送时间，必须清空可能残留的结束时间草稿，防止上一次配置共用污染
          }
          
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_LB_PIN');
          const pinKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [
            [{ text: "📌 自动置顶 (静音)", callback_data: "LB_PIN_YES" }, { text: "✖️ 不置顶", callback_data: "LB_PIN_NO" }],
            [{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
          ]};
          
          // 🟢 自动删除管理员发送的时间消息及机器人的上一步提示
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId }); } catch(e) {}
          }

          const nextRes = sendMessage(chatId, `✅ 时间已解析。\n发送时间：${new Date(lbDraft.sendTime).toLocaleString('zh-CN', {timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai'})}\n删除时间：${lbDraft.endTime ? new Date(lbDraft.endTime).toLocaleString('zh-CN', {timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai'}) : '不删除'}\n\n👉 <b>第四步：发布时是否自动将该消息置顶？</b>`, pinKeyboard, "HTML");
          try {
             const resData = JSON.parse(nextRes.getContentText());
             if (resData.ok) PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, resData.result.message_id);
          } catch(e) {}
        }
        else if (field === 'LB_LOOP') {
          const loopMatch = text.trim().match(/^(\d+)(分钟|小时|天|周)$/);
          if (!loopMatch) {
             sendLbError("⚠️ 格式不正确，请输入如 30分钟, 1小时, 2小时, 12小时, 1天, 1周等");
             return;
          }
          const val = parseInt(loopMatch[1]);
          if (val <= 0) {
             sendLbError("⚠️ 循环时间必须大于0，请重新输入：");
             return;
          }
          const unit = loopMatch[2];
          let loopMs = 0;
          if (unit === '分钟') loopMs = val * 60 * 1000;
          if (unit === '小时') loopMs = val * 60 * 60 * 1000;
          if (unit === '天') loopMs = val * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
          if (unit === '周') loopMs = val * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

          lbDraft.loopInterval = loopMs;
          lbDraft.loopText = text.trim();
          lbDraft.status = "pending";
          lbDraft.createdAt = new Date().getTime();
          
          const taskId = 'TASK_' + new Date().getTime();
          writeToFirebase(`schedule_queue/${taskId}`, lbDraft);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_lb_' + userId);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);

          // 🟢 自动删除管理员发送的循环设置消息及机器人的上一步提示，保证最终屏幕仅剩成功提醒
          try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: message.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
          const prevMsgId = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          if (prevMsgId) {
             try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: prevMsgId }); } catch(e) {}
             PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('lb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
          }

          const backKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
          sendMessage(chatId, `✅ <b>循环定时发布任务已成功创建！</b>\n\n🆔 任务编号：<code>${taskId}</code>\n🎯 目标：${lbDraft.target}\n⏱ 首发时间：${new Date(lbDraft.sendTime).toLocaleString('zh-CN', {timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai'})}\n⏳ 结束时间：${lbDraft.endTime ? new Date(lbDraft.endTime).toLocaleString('zh-CN', {timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai'}) : '永久有效'}\n📌 自动置顶：${lbDraft.pin ? '是' : '否'}\n🔁 循环间隔：${lbDraft.loopText}\n\n系统将在指定时间自动执行并自动清理上一条消息。`, backKeyboard, "HTML");
          return;
        }
        
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_lb_' + userId, JSON.stringify(lbDraft));
        return;
    } else if (field.startsWith('LB_EDITBOTH_')) {
        // 🟢 新增：文字+按钮两步编辑 —— 第 1 步：接收新文字，暂存后引导输按钮
        const taskId = field.replace('LB_EDITBOTH_', '');
        const task = readFromFirebase(`schedule_queue/${taskId}`);
        const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消编辑并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_LB_MANAGE_0" }]] };
        if (!task) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "❌ 任务记录已丢失，无法完成编辑同步。", cancelKeyboard);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          return;
        }
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 请发送纯文本内容！", cancelKeyboard);
          return;
        }
 
        // 🟢 暂存带有原生态 HTML 的新文字
        const formattedText = parseTelegramHtml(message);
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('pending_edit_text_' + userId, formattedText);
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_LB_EDITBTNS_' + taskId);
 
        const currentBtnsDisplay = escapeHtml(task.buttonsRaw || "");
        sendMessage(chatId,
          `✅ 新文字已暂存！\n\n` +
          `📝 <b>第 2/2 步：请回复新的内联按钮配置</b>\n\n` +
          `当前按钮配置：\n<code>${currentBtnsDisplay}</code>\n\n` +
          `输入格式（与创建时相同）：\n` +
          `<code>按钮1名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选) - STATUS:老师名(可选) | 按钮2名称 - 链接</code>\n\n` +
          `颜色支持：primary蓝, success绿, danger红\n` +
          `如需保留原有按钮不变，请直接回复：<code>跳过</code>`,
          cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
        return;
 
    } else if (field.startsWith('LB_EDITBTNS_')) {
        // 🟢 新增：文字+按钮两步编辑 —— 第 2 步：接收新按钮，与暂存文字一并同步到频道
        const taskId = field.replace('LB_EDITBTNS_', '');
        const task = readFromFirebase(`schedule_queue/${taskId}`);
        const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回管理列表", callback_data: "SETTINGS_LB_MANAGE_0" }]] };
 
        if (!task) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "❌ 任务记录已丢失，无法完成编辑同步。", cancelKeyboard);
          PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
          return;
        }
        if (!text) {
          sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 请发送纯文本内容！", cancelKeyboard);
          return;
        }
 
        // 🟢 取出第一步暂存的新文字
        const newText = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('pending_edit_text_' + userId) || task.text || "";
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('pending_edit_text_' + userId);
 
        // 🟢 解析新按钮（回复"跳过"则沿用原有按钮）
        let newButtonsRaw = task.buttonsRaw || "";
        let newKeyboard = null;
 
        if (text.trim() !== '跳过') {
          let isValid = true;
          let parsedKeyboard = [];
          const rows = text.trim().split('\n');
          for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
            let btnRow = [];
            const btns = rows[i].split('|');
            for (let j = 0; j < btns.length; j++) {
              const btnText = btns[j].trim();
              if (!btnText) continue;
              const parts = btnText.split('-');
              if (parts.length < 2) { isValid = false; break; }
              let urlObj = "";
              let styleObj = null;
              let statusBind = null; // 🟢 修复①：声明 statusBind，用于承接 STATUS 老师名
                let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
                
                let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                if (lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                    statusBind = lastP.substring(7).trim(); // 🟢 修复②：正确提取老师名
                    urlParts.pop();
                    lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                }
                if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
                    styleObj = lastP.toLowerCase();
                    urlParts.pop();
                    lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                    if (!statusBind && lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) { // 🟢 修复③：颜色写在 STATUS 前时也能捕获
                        statusBind = lastP.substring(7).trim();
                        urlParts.pop();
                    }
                }
              urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();
                if (!urlObj.startsWith('http') && !urlObj.startsWith('tg://')) urlObj = 'https://' + urlObj;
                
                // 🟢 修复：将 btnText 改名为 finalBtnText，防止与循环开头的 const btnText 变量名冲突
                let finalBtnText = parts[0].trim();
                if (statusBind) {
                   const tData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${statusBind}`) || {};
                   let sText = "🔴下班";
                   if (tData.status === 'online') sText = "🟢在岗";
                   else if (tData.status === 'leave') sText = "🟡休息";
                   finalBtnText += ` [${sText}]`;
                }
                let btnDef = { text: finalBtnText, url: urlObj };
                if (styleObj) btnDef.style = styleObj;
                btnRow.push(btnDef);
            }
            if (!isValid) break;
            if (btnRow.length > 0) parsedKeyboard.push(btnRow);
          }
 
          if (!isValid || parsedKeyboard.length === 0) {
            sendMessage(chatId,
              `⚠️ <b>按钮格式错误！</b>请按照 <code>按钮名称 - 链接 - 颜色(可选)</code> 格式重新回复，` +
              `或直接回复 <code>跳过</code> 保留原按钮：`,
              cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
            return; // 保持状态，等用户重新输入
          }
 
          newButtonsRaw = text.trim();
          newKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: parsedKeyboard };
        } else {
          // 用户选择跳过：还原原有按钮结构
          if (task.buttonsRaw) {
            let parsedKeyboard = [];
            task.buttonsRaw.split('\n').forEach(row => {
              let btnRow = [];
              row.split('|').forEach(btn => {
                let parts = btn.split('-');
                if (parts.length >= 2) {
                  let urlObj = "";
                  let styleObj = null;
              let statusBind = null;
              let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
