  if (data === "SETTINGS_TIER_CHANNEL") {
    const curTiers = readFromFirebase('system_config/tier_channels') || {};
    const bId = curTiers.B || "未设置";
    const tierKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: "⭐ 设置 B级 频道", callback_data: "ADMIN_TIER_B_START" }],
        [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
      ]
    };
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `🏆 <b>配置分级频道</b>\n\n当前分级频道状态：\n⭐ B级频道：<code>${bId}</code>\n\n👉 请点击下方按钮设置对应等级的频道ID：`, tierKeyboard, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 修复：进入分级频道输入等待状态
  if (data === "ADMIN_TIER_B_START") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_ADMIN_TIER_B');
    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TIER_CHANNEL" }]] };
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `⭐ <b>设置 B级 频道</b>\n\n👉 请回复频道的纯数字 ID (需带 -100 前缀)\n回复“清空”可解除配置：`, cancelKb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // ================= 🟢 定时切换老师状态 =================
  if (data.startsWith("SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_") && !data.startsWith("SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_LIST")) {
    const page = parseInt(data.replace("SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_", "")) || 0;
    // 进入向导时清空旧草稿
    if (page === 0 && data === "SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_0") {
      setSchsDraft(userId, { selected: {} });
    }
    try {
      sendSchStatusPicker(chatId, messageId, userId, page);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("sendSchStatusPicker:", e);
      try { sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 打开定时切换状态失败：" + e); } catch (e2) {}
    }
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 新格式：SCHS_TOGGLE_{page}_{index} ；兼容旧 SCHS_TOGGLE.{enc}.{page}
  if (data.startsWith("SCHS_TOGGLE_") || data.startsWith("SCHS_TOGGLE.")) {
    let name = "";
    let page = 0;
    if (data.startsWith("SCHS_TOGGLE_")) {
      const rest = data.replace("SCHS_TOGGLE_", "");
      const parts = rest.split("_");
      page = parseInt(parts[0], 10) || 0;
      const idx = parseInt(parts[1], 10) || 0;
      const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
      const names = Object.keys(allTeachers)
        .filter(n => {
          const t = allTeachers[n];
          if (!t || typeof t !== 'object') return false;
          // 🟢 与 sendSchStatusPicker 统一，避免页码索引与全选名单错位
          return !!(t.message_id || t.tg_id);
        })
        .sort((a, b) => (allTeachers[a].added_at || 0) - (allTeachers[b].added_at || 0));
      const PAGE_SIZE = 12;
      name = names[page * PAGE_SIZE + idx] || "";
    } else {
      const parts = data.slice("SCHS_TOGGLE.".length).split(".");
      const enc = parts[0];
      page = parseInt(parts[1]) || 0;
      try { name = decodeTeacherFromDeepLink(enc); } catch (e) {
        answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "解析失败", true); return;
      }
    }
    if (!name) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "老师不存在或列表已变化", true);
      return;
    }
    let draft = getSchsDraft(userId);
    if (!draft.selected) draft.selected = {};
    if (draft.selected[name]) delete draft.selected[name];
    else draft.selected[name] = true;
    setSchsDraft(userId, draft);
    sendSchStatusPicker(chatId, messageId, userId, page);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  if (data.startsWith("SCHS_PAGE_")) {
    const page = parseInt(data.replace("SCHS_PAGE_", "")) || 0;
    sendSchStatusPicker(chatId, messageId, userId, page);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  if (data === "SCHS_ALL") {
    const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
    let draft = getSchsDraft(userId);
    draft.selected = {};
    let count = 0;
    const MAX_BATCH = 200;
    // 🟢 与 sendSchStatusPicker / 管理老师状态 统一：仅 message_id 或 tg_id，排除幽灵残留
    Object.keys(allTeachers).forEach(n => {
      if (count >= MAX_BATCH) return;
      const t = allTeachers[n];
      if (t && typeof t === 'object' && (t.message_id || t.tg_id)) {
        draft.selected[n] = true;
        count++;
      }
    });
    setSchsDraft(userId, draft);
    sendSchStatusPicker(chatId, messageId, userId, 0);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, count >= MAX_BATCH ? ("✅ 已选前 " + MAX_BATCH + " 位（单次上限）") : "✅ 已全选");
    return;
  }

  if (data === "SCHS_CLEAR") {
    setSchsDraft(userId, { selected: {} });
    sendSchStatusPicker(chatId, messageId, userId, 0);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已清空选择");
    return;
  }

  if (data === "SCHS_GOTO_STATUS") {
    const draft = getSchsDraft(userId);
    const cnt = Object.keys(draft.selected || {}).length;
    if (cnt === 0) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "请至少选择 1 位老师", true);
      return;
    }
    const kb = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [
          { text: "🟢 在岗", callback_data: "SCHS_ST_online" },
          { text: "🔴 下班", callback_data: "SCHS_ST_offline" },
          { text: "🟡 休息", callback_data: "SCHS_ST_leave" }
        ],
        [{ text: "🔙 返回选老师", callback_data: "SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_0" }]
      ]
    };
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `⏰ <b>定时切换状态 (2/3)</b>\n\n已选 <b>${cnt}</b> 位老师。\n请选择到期后要切换成的状态：`,
      kb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  if (data.startsWith("SCHS_ST_")) {
    const st = data.replace("SCHS_ST_", "");
    if (!["online", "offline", "leave"].includes(st)) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "无效状态", true); return;
    }
    let draft = getSchsDraft(userId);
    draft.status = st;
    setSchsDraft(userId, draft);
    sendSchStatusTimePicker(chatId, messageId, st, userId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🔁 每日循环开关
  if (data === "SCHS_LOOP_TOGGLE") {
    let draft = getSchsDraft(userId);
    draft.loop = !draft.loop;
    setSchsDraft(userId, draft);
    if (!draft.status) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "请先选状态", true); return;
    }
    sendSchStatusTimePicker(chatId, messageId, draft.status, userId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, draft.loop ? "已开启每日循环" : "已关闭每日循环");
    return;
  }
  if (data === "SCHS_AT_INFO") {
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "点下方时刻按钮即可，已过则预约次日同一时间（北京时间）", false);
    return;
  }
  if (data === "SCHS_REL_INFO") {
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "从现在起算的相对延时；开循环则每次执行后再顺延同等时长", false);
    return;
  }

  if (data.startsWith("SCHS_T_")) {
    // 相对：SCHS_T_{m|h|d|w}_{数字}
    // 绝对：SCHS_T_at_HHMM  例 SCHS_T_at_0300
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const statusMap = { online: "🟢在岗", offline: "🔴下班", leave: "🟡休息" };
    const unitLabel = { m: "分钟", h: "小时", d: "天", w: "周" };

    let draft = getSchsDraft(userId);
    if (!draft || !draft.selected || Object.keys(draft.selected).length === 0) draft = null;
    if (!draft || !draft.status || !draft.selected || Object.keys(draft.selected).length === 0) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "草稿失效，请重新设置", true); return;
    }
    const names = Object.keys(draft.selected);
    const loopOn = !!draft.loop;

    let executeAt = 0;
    let delayLabel = "";
    let scheduleType = "relative"; // relative | daily_at
    let intervalMs = 0;
    let dailyHh = null, dailyMm = null;

    if (parts[2] === "at") {
      // 绝对时刻 HHMM
      const hm = parts[3] || "";
      if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(hm)) {
        answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "时间无效", true); return;
      }
      const hh = parseInt(hm.slice(0, 2), 10);
      const mm = parseInt(hm.slice(2, 4), 10);
      if (hh < 0 || hh > 23 || mm < 0 || mm > 59) {
        answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "时间无效", true); return;
      }
      executeAt = getNextDailyExecuteAt(hh, mm);
      delayLabel = `每天 ${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(mm).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      scheduleType = "daily_at";
      dailyHh = hh;
      dailyMm = mm;
    } else {
      const unit = parts[2];
      const val = parseInt(parts[3], 10) || 0;
      if (!val || !["m", "h", "d", "w"].includes(unit)) {
        answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "时间无效", true); return;
      }
      if (unit === "h" && (val < 1 || val > 24)) {
        answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "小时仅支持 1–24", true); return;
      }
      if (unit === "m") intervalMs = val * 60 * 1000;
      else if (unit === "h") intervalMs = val * 60 * 60 * 1000;
      else if (unit === "d") intervalMs = val * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
      else if (unit === "w") intervalMs = val * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
      executeAt = new Date().getTime() + intervalMs;
      delayLabel = `${val}${unitLabel[unit]}`;
      scheduleType = "relative";
    }

    const taskId = "schs_" + new Date().getTime() + "_" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000);
    const taskPayload = {
      teacher_names: names,
      target_status: draft.status,
      execute_at: executeAt,
      created_by: String(userId),
      created_at: new Date().getTime(),
      delay_label: delayLabel,
      loop: loopOn,
      schedule_type: scheduleType
    };
    if (scheduleType === "relative" && loopOn) {
      taskPayload.interval_ms = intervalMs;
    }
    if (scheduleType === "daily_at") {
      taskPayload.daily_hh = dailyHh;
      taskPayload.daily_mm = dailyMm;
    }

    writeToFirebase(`status_schedule_queue/${taskId}`, taskPayload);
    clearSchsDraft(userId);

    const timeStr = new Date(executeAt).toLocaleString("zh-CN", { timeZone: "Asia/Shanghai" });
    const kb = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: "📋 查看定时任务", callback_data: "SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_LIST" }],
        [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
      ]
    };
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `✅ <b>定时任务已创建</b>\n\n` +
      `👥 老师：${names.map(n => escapeHtml(n)).join("、")}\n` +
      `📌 目标状态：${statusMap[draft.status]}\n` +
      `⏳ 时间：${delayLabel}\n` +
      `🔁 每日循环：${loopOn ? "✅ 开" : "❌ 关"}\n` +
      `🕒 预计执行：${timeStr}\n\n` +
      `时间到后会自动改状态并同步在岗榜/关注通知。` +
      (loopOn ? "\n循环任务执行后会自动预约下一次。" : ""),
      kb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "✅ 已预约");
    return;
  }

  if (data === "SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_LIST") {
    sendSchStatusTaskList(chatId, messageId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  if (data.startsWith("SCHS_DEL_")) {
    const tid = data.replace("SCHS_DEL_", "");
    writeToFirebase(`status_schedule_queue/${tid}`, null);
    sendSchStatusTaskList(chatId, messageId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "✅ 已取消该任务");
    return;
  }
  // ================= 定时切换状态结束 =================
// 🟢 新增：处理设置面板的主菜单返回
                if (data === "SETTINGS_MAIN") {
    const settingsKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: "🔗 绑定老师/列表", callback_data: "SETTINGS_BIND_0" }, { text: "🏷️ 分类标签", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TAGS" }],
        [{ text: "⏱ 标签榜自动删除", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL" }, { text: "📝 打卡触发词", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW" }],
        [{ text: "📢 频道相关", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHANNEL" }, { text: "📋 管理老师状态", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSTATUS_0" }],
        [{ text: "📅 新建定时发布", callback_data: "SETTINGS_LB_START" }, { text: "📝 管理发布记录", callback_data: "SETTINGS_LB_MANAGE_0" }],
        [{ text: "👋 欢迎消息设置", callback_data: "SETTINGS_WELCOME_START" }, { text: "🚀 /start 消息设置", callback_data: "SETTINGS_STARTMSG_START" }],
        [{ text: "➕ 新增关键词", callback_data: "SETTINGS_KW_START" }, { text: "⚙️ 管理关键词", callback_data: "SETTINGS_KW_MANAGE_0" }],
        // 🟢 与 /settings 入口对齐：补上菜单键盘入口
        [{ text: "⌨️ 新增菜单按钮", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_START" }, { text: "📋 管理菜单键盘", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_MANAGE_0" }],
        [{ text: "⏰ 定时切换状态", callback_data: "SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_0" }, { text: "📋 定时状态任务", callback_data: "SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_LIST" }],
        [{ text: "🛡️ 防垃圾设置", callback_data: "SETTINGS_ANTISPAM" }, { text: "📣 强制关注频道", callback_data: "SETTINGS_FORCESUB_START" }],
        [{ text: "✖️ 关闭", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]
      ]
    };
    // 注意：这里需要补上 HTML 解析模式参数，否则 <b> 标签会作为纯文本暴露
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, "⚙️ <b>管理员控制面板</b>\n请选择您要进行的操作：", settingsKeyboard, "HTML");
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
    // 🟢 修复：返回主面板时，自动清除绑定老师和老师状态的搜索关键词缓存
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('bind_kw_' + userId);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('tstatus_kw_' + userId);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('tstatus_kw_' + chatId); // 兼容原代码的 chatId 缓存
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
// 🟢 升级：启动欢迎消息配置向导 — 有配置时先展示完整预览，无配置直接进入编辑
  if (data === "SETTINGS_WELCOME_START") {
    const curConf = readFromFirebase('system_config/welcome_msg');

    if (curConf) {
      // ✅ 已有配置 → 展示完整预览，全部内容支持点击复制
      let previewText = `👋 <b>【欢迎消息】当前配置预览</b>\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n`;

      if (curConf.mediaId) {
        const mediaLabel = curConf.mediaType === 'photo' ? '🖼️ 图片' : '🎬 视频';
        previewText += `📎 <b>媒体类型：</b>${mediaLabel}\n`;
        previewText += `🆔 <b>文件ID（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.mediaId)}</code>\n\n`;
      } else {
        previewText += `📎 <b>媒体类型：</b>纯文字（无图/视频）\n\n`;
      }

      previewText += `📝 <b>正文内容（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.text || '（未填写）')}</code>\n\n`;

      if (curConf.buttonsRaw) {
        previewText += `🔘 <b>按钮/链接配置（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.buttonsRaw)}</code>\n\n`;
      } else {
        previewText += `🔘 <b>按钮/链接配置：</b>（当前无按钮）\n\n`;
      }

            const autoDeleteMin = curConf.autoDeleteTime > 0 ? (curConf.autoDeleteTime / 1000 / 60) : 0;
      // 🟢 旧数据无 enabled 字段时默认视为开启
      const isEnabled = curConf.enabled !== false;
      previewText += `🔌 <b>功能状态：</b>${isEnabled ? '✅ 已开启' : '❌ 已关闭'}\n`;
      previewText += `⏱️ <b>自动删除：</b>${autoDeleteMin > 0 ? autoDeleteMin + ' 分钟后删除' : '不自动删除（仅替换上一条）'}\n\n`;
      previewText += `<i>💡 点击上方蓝色内容块可直接一键复制</i>`;
      const previewKeyboard = {
        inline_keyboard: [
          [{ text: isEnabled ? "❌ 关闭欢迎消息" : "✅ 开启欢迎消息", callback_data: "SETTINGS_WELCOME_TOGGLE" }],
          [{ text: "✏️ 重新配置欢迎消息", callback_data: "SETTINGS_WELCOME_EDIT" }],
          [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
        ]
      };
      
      // 🟢 新增：实现无缝媒体预览（采用类似车评面板的瞬间替换机制）
      if (curConf.mediaId) {
        try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId }); } catch(e) {}
        const payload = { chat_id: chatId, caption: previewText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: previewKeyboard };
        if (curConf.mediaType === 'photo') {
           payload.photo = curConf.mediaId;
           sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload);
        } else {
           payload.video = curConf.mediaId;
           sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload);
        }
      } else {
        sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
          chat_id: chatId,
          message_id: messageId,
          text: previewText,
          parse_mode: "HTML",
          reply_markup: previewKeyboard
        });
      }
    } else {
      // ⚠️ 尚未配置 → 直接进入设置第一步
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_WELCOME_CONTENT');
      // 🟢 记录占位初始提示的 ID，供后续输入环节直接抹除
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, messageId);
      const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
        chat_id: chatId,
        message_id: messageId,
        text: `👋 <b>设置群组欢迎消息</b>\n\n⚠️ 当前尚未配置任何欢迎消息。\n\n👉 <b>第一步：请发送欢迎消息的正文内容</b>\n\n*(支持图文/视频。在文案中使用 <code>{user}</code> 占位符，将自动替换为新人的 @提及 链接)*\n\n例如：<code>欢迎 {user} 来到本群！</code>`,
        parse_mode: "HTML",
        reply_markup: cancelKeyboard
      });
    }
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 欢迎消息总开关：仅翻转 enabled，不改动文案/按钮/删除时间
  if (data === "SETTINGS_WELCOME_TOGGLE") {
    const curConf = readFromFirebase('system_config/welcome_msg');
    if (!curConf || !curConf.text) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 请先配置欢迎消息内容", true);
      return;
    }
    const nextEnabled = curConf.enabled === false; // 当前关 → 开；否则关
    updateFirebase('system_config/welcome_msg', { enabled: nextEnabled });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, nextEnabled ? "✅ 欢迎消息已开启" : "❌ 欢迎消息已关闭", true);

    // 刷新预览页文案与按钮（与 SETTINGS_WELCOME_START 预览逻辑一致）
    const autoDeleteMin = curConf.autoDeleteTime > 0 ? (curConf.autoDeleteTime / 1000 / 60) : 0;
    let previewText = `👋 <b>【欢迎消息】当前配置预览</b>\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n`;
    if (curConf.mediaId) {
      const mediaLabel = curConf.mediaType === 'photo' ? '🖼️ 图片' : '🎬 视频';
      previewText += `📎 <b>媒体类型：</b>${mediaLabel}\n`;
      previewText += `🆔 <b>文件ID（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.mediaId)}</code>\n\n`;
    } else {
      previewText += `📎 <b>媒体类型：</b>纯文字（无图/视频）\n\n`;
    }
    previewText += `📝 <b>正文内容（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.text || '（未填写）')}</code>\n\n`;
    if (curConf.buttonsRaw) {
      previewText += `🔘 <b>按钮/链接配置（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.buttonsRaw)}</code>\n\n`;
    } else {
      previewText += `🔘 <b>按钮/链接配置：</b>（当前无按钮）\n\n`;
    }
    previewText += `🔌 <b>功能状态：</b>${nextEnabled ? '✅ 已开启' : '❌ 已关闭'}\n`;
    previewText += `⏱️ <b>自动删除：</b>${autoDeleteMin > 0 ? autoDeleteMin + ' 分钟后删除' : '不自动删除（仅替换上一条）'}\n\n`;
    previewText += `<i>💡 点击上方蓝色内容块可直接一键复制</i>`;
    const previewKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: nextEnabled ? "❌ 关闭欢迎消息" : "✅ 开启欢迎消息", callback_data: "SETTINGS_WELCOME_TOGGLE" }],
        [{ text: "✏️ 重新配置欢迎消息", callback_data: "SETTINGS_WELCOME_EDIT" }],
        [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
      ]
    };
    try {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, previewText, previewKeyboard, "HTML");
    } catch (e) {}
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 新增：从预览页点击"重新配置"后进入编辑第一步
  if (data === "SETTINGS_WELCOME_EDIT") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_WELCOME_CONTENT');
    const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
    // 🟢 修复：预览页若带图/视频，原消息是媒体消息而非纯文本。裸调 sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText",...)
    // 会被 TG 报错 "there is no text in the message to edit"，又因 muteHttpExceptions:true 且未检查返回值，
    // 报错被静默吞掉 → 界面无变化 → 点击无反应。改用已封装"编辑失败自动降级为删图+发新文字"兜底逻辑的 editMessageText()
    // 🟢 核心修复：预览带图时，editMessageText 内部会把这张图片消息删掉、改发一条全新的文字消息，原 messageId 早已失效。
    // 这里必须改用它的返回值（真正生效的最新 message_id）来记录"过渡提示"，不然后面清理时拿着失效旧ID去删——删了个寂寞，
    // 真正挡在屏幕上的这条新提示反而没人清，导致"重新设置"流程残留垃圾消息，达不到干净整洁的效果
    const promptMsgId = editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `👋 <b>重新设置群组欢迎消息</b>\n\n👉 <b>第一步：请发送欢迎消息的正文内容</b>\n\n*(支持图文/视频。在文案中使用 <code>{user}</code> 占位符，将自动替换为新人的 @提及 链接)*\n\n例如：<code>欢迎 {user} 来到本群！</code>`, cancelKeyboard);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, promptMsgId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🚀 新增：启动 /start 消息配置向导 — 有配置时先展示完整预览，无配置直接进入编辑
  if (data === "SETTINGS_STARTMSG_START") {
    const curConf = readFromFirebase('system_config/start_msg');

    if (curConf) {
      // ✅ 已有配置 → 展示完整预览，全部内容支持点击复制
      let previewText = `🚀 <b>【/start 消息】当前配置预览</b>\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n`;

      if (curConf.mediaId) {
        const mediaLabel = curConf.mediaType === 'photo' ? '🖼️ 图片' : '🎬 视频';
        previewText += `📎 <b>媒体类型：</b>${mediaLabel}\n`;
        previewText += `🆔 <b>文件ID（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.mediaId)}</code>\n\n`;
      } else {
        previewText += `📎 <b>媒体类型：</b>纯文字（无图/视频）\n\n`;
      }

      previewText += `📝 <b>正文内容（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.text || '（未填写）')}</code>\n\n`;

      if (curConf.buttonsRaw) {
        previewText += `🔘 <b>按钮/链接配置（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(curConf.buttonsRaw)}</code>\n\n`;
      } else {
        previewText += `🔘 <b>按钮/链接配置：</b>（当前无按钮）\n\n`;
      }

      previewText += `<i>💡 支持 {user}（用户昵称）与 {id}（用户ID）占位符；点击上方蓝色内容块可直接一键复制</i>`;
      const previewKeyboard = {
        inline_keyboard: [
          [{ text: "✏️ 重新配置 /start 消息", callback_data: "SETTINGS_STARTMSG_EDIT" }],
          [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
        ]
      };

      // 🟢 实现无缝媒体预览（沿用欢迎消息面板同款的瞬间替换机制）
      if (curConf.mediaId) {
        try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId }); } catch(e) {}
        const payload = { chat_id: chatId, caption: previewText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: previewKeyboard };
        if (curConf.mediaType === 'photo') {
           payload.photo = curConf.mediaId;
           sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload);
        } else {
           payload.video = curConf.mediaId;
           sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload);
        }
      } else {
        sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
          chat_id: chatId,
          message_id: messageId,
          text: previewText,
          parse_mode: "HTML",
          reply_markup: previewKeyboard
        });
      }
    } else {
      // ⚠️ 尚未配置 → 直接进入设置第一步
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_STARTMSG_CONTENT');
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, messageId);
      const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
        chat_id: chatId,
        message_id: messageId,
        text: `🚀 <b>设置 /start 启动消息</b>\n\n⚠️ 当前尚未配置，用户发送 /start 时将使用系统默认欢迎语。\n\n👉 <b>第一步：请发送 /start 消息的正文内容</b>\n\n*(支持图文/视频。可使用 <code>{user}</code> 占位符替换为用户昵称，<code>{id}</code> 占位符替换为用户ID)*\n\n例如：<code>👋Hi, {user}-【欢迎光临】\n用户ID：{id}</code>`,
        parse_mode: "HTML",
        reply_markup: cancelKeyboard
      });
    }
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🚀 新增：从预览页点击"重新配置"后进入编辑第一步
  if (data === "SETTINGS_STARTMSG_EDIT") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_STARTMSG_CONTENT');
    const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
    const promptMsgId = editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `🚀 <b>重新设置 /start 启动消息</b>\n\n👉 <b>第一步：请发送 /start 消息的正文内容</b>\n\n*(支持图文/视频。可使用 <code>{user}</code> 占位符替换为用户昵称，<code>{id}</code> 占位符替换为用户ID)*\n\n例如：<code>👋Hi, {user}-【欢迎光临】\n用户ID：{id}</code>`, cancelKeyboard);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('startmsg_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, promptMsgId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：保存欢迎消息自动删除时间并完成配置
          if (data.startsWith("WELCOME_DEL_TIME_")) {
            const seconds = parseInt(data.replace("WELCOME_DEL_TIME_", ""));
            const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_welcome_' + userId);
            if (!draftStr) {
              answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 任务已过期，请重新配置", true);
              return;
            }
            
                        let wlDraft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
            wlDraft.autoDeleteTime = seconds * 1000; // 0 = 跳过倒计时，仅替换上一条
            wlDraft.enabled = true; // 🟢 配置完成后默认开启欢迎功能
            
            // 🟢 核心修复：移除对 lastMsgIds 的嵌套读取与合并，彻底分离静态配置与动态防刷屏状态
            // 防止主备数据库时段切换时，因局部空数据覆盖导致欢迎消息文案被清空
            writeToFirebase('system_config/welcome_msg', wlDraft);
            PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_welcome_' + userId);
            PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
            PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('welcome_prompt_msg_id_' + userId); // 🟢 养成好习惯，清理垃圾缓存
    
    const backKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
        editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `✅ <b>欢迎消息已配置成功！</b>\n\n🔌 功能：已开启\n⏱️ 倒计时自删：${seconds > 0 ? (seconds/60 + ' 分钟') : '未设置（仅替换上一条）'}\n\n可在「欢迎消息设置」里随时开关；关闭后新人进群将不再发送。`, backKeyboard, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 修复：点开在岗面板先预览当前已配置内容（文本/图片/视频/按钮链接），支持点击复制
  if (data === "SETTINGS_SB_START") {
    const currentBoard = readFromFirebase('system_config/static_board');

    if (currentBoard) {
      // ✅ 已有配置 → 展示预览面板，内容全部用 <code> 包裹，点击可直接复制
      let previewText = `📡 <b>【在岗面板】当前配置预览</b>\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n`;

      // 媒体类型
      if (currentBoard.mediaId) {
        const mediaLabel = currentBoard.mediaType === 'photo' ? '🖼️ 图片' : '🎬 视频';
        previewText += `📎 <b>媒体类型：</b>${mediaLabel}\n`;
        previewText += `🆔 <b>文件ID（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(currentBoard.mediaId)}</code>\n\n`;
      } else {
        previewText += `📎 <b>媒体类型：</b>纯文字（无图/视频）\n\n`;
      }

      // 正文内容（<code> 标签 = 点击即复制）
      previewText += `📝 <b>正文内容（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(currentBoard.text || '（未填写）')}</code>\n\n`;

      // 按钮链接配置
      if (currentBoard.buttonsRaw) {
        previewText += `🔘 <b>按钮/链接配置（点击复制）：</b>\n<code>${escapeHtml(currentBoard.buttonsRaw)}</code>\n\n`;
      } else {
        previewText += `🔘 <b>按钮/链接配置：</b>（当前无按钮）\n\n`;
      }

      previewText += `<i>💡 点击上方蓝色内容块可直接一键复制对应内容</i>`;

      const previewKeyboard = {
        inline_keyboard: [
          [{ text: "✏️ 重新配置面板内容", callback_data: "SETTINGS_SB_EDIT" }],
          [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
        ]
      };

      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, previewText, previewKeyboard, "HTML");
    } else {
      // ⚠️ 尚未配置 → 直接进入设置第一步
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_SB_CONTENT');
      // 🟢 新增：记录面板初始提示的 ID，供后续输入环节直接抹除
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('sb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, messageId);
      const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `📡 <b>设置自定义【在岗面板】</b>\n\n⚠️ 当前尚未配置任何面板内容。\n\n👉 <b>第一步：请发送面板的正文内容</b>\n(支持发送纯文字，也支持发送图片/视频并在底部填写文字说明)`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
    }

    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：点击"重新配置面板内容"后，进入正式编辑流程（原 SETTINGS_SB_START 的旧逻辑）
  if (data === "SETTINGS_SB_EDIT") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_SB_CONTENT');
    const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
    // 🟢 核心修复：如果是带图预览，editMessageText 会改为发新文字，需接住最新 message_id 并记录
    const promptMsgId = editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `📡 <b>设置自定义【在岗面板】</b>\n\n👉 <b>第一步：请发送面板的正文内容</b>\n(支持发送纯文字，也支持发送图片/视频并在底部填写文字说明)`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('sb_prompt_msg_id_' + userId, promptMsgId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：处理设置频道 ID
  if (data === "SETTINGS_CHANNEL") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_ADMIN_CHANNEL');
    const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
    const currentChannel = readFromFirebase('system_config/publish_channel') || "-1003979223568 (默认)";
    
    // 🟢 修复：改用底层 API 确保 HTML 格式正确渲染，使 ID 显示为“纯ID: ”格式并支持点击复制
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId,
      message_id: messageId,
      text: `📢 <b>设置车评发布频道 ID</b>\n\n当前频道 纯ID: <code>${currentChannel}</code>\n\n👉 请在下方直接回复新的频道纯数字 ID（需带 -100 前缀）：\n\n<i>* 设置后，所有审核通过的车评及资料会自动发布到新频道。\n* 回复【恢复默认】可清除自定义设置。</i>`,
      parse_mode: "HTML",
      reply_markup: cancelKeyboard
    });
    
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
    // 🟢 老师群内打卡触发词设置
  if (data === "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW") {
    const k = getCheckInKeywordMap();
    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [
      [{ text: "✏️ 修改三个触发词", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW_EDIT" }],
      [{ text: "🔄 恢复默认（打卡/下班/休息）", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW_RESET" }],
      [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
    ]};
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `📝 <b>打卡触发词设置</b>\n\n` +
      `老师在群内发送对应词语即可切换状态：\n\n` +
      `🟢 在岗：<code>${escapeHtml(k.online)}</code>\n` +
      `🔴 下班：<code>${escapeHtml(k.offline)}</code>\n` +
      `🟡 休息：<code>${escapeHtml(k.leave)}</code>\n\n` +
      `<i>三个词必须互不相同，建议使用简短中文。</i>`,
      cancelKb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  if (data === "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW_EDIT") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_CHECKIN_KEYWORDS');
    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW" }]] };
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `✏️ <b>修改打卡触发词</b>\n\n请按以下格式发送三行（或用 | 分隔）：\n\n` +
      `<code>在岗词\n下班词\n休息词</code>\n\n` +
      `或：<code>在岗词 | 下班词 | 休息词</code>\n\n` +
      `示例：\n<code>上线\n收工\n暂离</code>\n\n` +
      `当前：\n` +
      (() => { const k = getCheckInKeywordMap(); return `🟢 ${escapeHtml(k.online)} / 🔴 ${escapeHtml(k.offline)} / 🟡 ${escapeHtml(k.leave)}`; })(),
      cancelKb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  if (data === "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW_RESET") {
    writeToFirebase('system_config/checkin_keywords', { online: '打卡', offline: '下班', leave: '休息' });
    invalidateCheckInKeywordCache();
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "✅ 已恢复默认：打卡 / 下班 / 休息", true);
    // 回到展示页
    const k = getCheckInKeywordMap();
    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [
      [{ text: "✏️ 修改三个触发词", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW_EDIT" }],
      [{ text: "🔄 恢复默认（打卡/下班/休息）", callback_data: "SETTINGS_CHECKIN_KW_RESET" }],
      [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
    ]};
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `📝 <b>打卡触发词设置</b>\n\n` +
      `老师在群内发送对应词语即可切换状态：\n\n` +
      `🟢 在岗：<code>${escapeHtml(k.online)}</code>\n` +
      `🔴 下班：<code>${escapeHtml(k.offline)}</code>\n` +
      `🟡 休息：<code>${escapeHtml(k.leave)}</code>\n\n` +
      `<i>三个词必须互不相同，建议使用简短中文。</i>`,
      cancelKb, "HTML");
    return;
  }
    // 🟢 /menu 启动提示语设置
  if (data === "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT") {
    // 🟢 取消编辑时顺带清掉引导消息 ID，避免残留
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('menu_prompt_edit_msg_id_' + userId);
    // 若还在「等待输入」状态，一并清除，防止取消后仍拦截后续消息
    try {
      const st = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('state_' + userId);
      if (st === 'WAITING_FOR_MENU_PROMPT') {
        PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
      }
    } catch (e) {}

    const cur = getMenuPromptText();
    const kb = { inline_keyboard: [
      [{ text: "✏️ 修改提示语", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT_EDIT" }],
      [{ text: "🔄 恢复默认", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT_RESET" }],
      [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
    ]};
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `📋 <b>/menu 启动提示语</b>\n\n` +
      `用户发送 /menu 时，与底部键盘一起发出的说明文字：\n\n` +
      `<code>${escapeHtml(cur)}</code>\n\n` +
      `<i>支持多行；留空无效，将继续使用默认文案。</i>`,
      kb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  if (data === "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT_EDIT") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_MENU_PROMPT');
    // 🟢 记录当前引导消息 ID，保存后可原地改回设置面板，避免残留
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('menu_prompt_edit_msg_id_' + userId, String(messageId));
    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT" }]] };
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `✏️ <b>修改 /menu 提示语</b>\n\n请直接发送新的提示文字（可多行）。\n\n` +
      `当前：\n<code>${escapeHtml(getMenuPromptText())}</code>\n\n` +
      `默认示例：\n<code>${escapeHtml(getDefaultMenuPromptText())}</code>`,
      cancelKb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  if (data === "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT_RESET") {
    writeToFirebase('system_config/menu_prompt_text', null);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "✅ 已恢复默认 /menu 提示语", true);
    const cur = getMenuPromptText();
    const kb = { inline_keyboard: [
      [{ text: "✏️ 修改提示语", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT_EDIT" }],
      [{ text: "🔄 恢复默认", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_PROMPT_RESET" }],
      [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
    ]};
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `📋 <b>/menu 启动提示语</b>\n\n` +
      `用户发送 /menu 时，与底部键盘一起发出的说明文字：\n\n` +
      `<code>${escapeHtml(cur)}</code>\n\n` +
      `<i>支持多行；留空无效，将继续使用默认文案。</i>`,
      kb, "HTML");
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 标签榜（群内关键词呼出列表）自动删除时间设置
  if (data === "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL") {
    const cur = parseInt(readFromFirebase('system_config/status_board_auto_delete_minutes'), 10) || 0;
    const curText = cur <= 0 ? '关闭（不自动删）' : (cur >= 60 && cur % 60 === 0 ? (cur / 60) + ' 小时' : cur + ' 分钟');
    const kb = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: "关闭", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_0" }, { text: "10分钟", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_10" }],
        [{ text: "30分钟", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_30" }, { text: "1小时", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_60" }],
        [{ text: "2小时", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_120" }, { text: "3小时", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_180" }],
        [{ text: "6小时", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_360" }],
        [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
      ]
    };
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `⏱ <b>标签榜自动删除</b>\n\n当前：<b>${curText}</b>\n\n群内发送分类标签呼出的「在岗/下班/休息」列表，将在设定时间后自动删除（含触发关键词消息）。\n\n可选范围：<b>10 分钟～6 小时</b>，或关闭。`,
      kb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  if (data.startsWith("SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_")) {
    const mins = parseInt(data.replace("SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_", ""), 10);
    const safeMins = isNaN(mins) || mins < 0 ? 0 : Math.min(360, mins === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(10, mins));
    writeToFirebase('system_config/status_board_auto_delete_minutes', safeMins);
    const curText = safeMins <= 0 ? '关闭（不自动删）' : (safeMins >= 60 && safeMins % 60 === 0 ? (safeMins / 60) + ' 小时' : safeMins + ' 分钟');
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "✅ 已设为 " + curText, true);
    // 刷新当前设置页
    const kb = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: "关闭", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_0" }, { text: "10分钟", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_10" }],
        [{ text: "30分钟", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_30" }, { text: "1小时", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_60" }],
        [{ text: "2小时", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_120" }, { text: "3小时", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_180" }],
        [{ text: "6小时", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSB_AUTODEL_360" }],
        [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
      ]
    };
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId,
      `⏱ <b>标签榜自动删除</b>\n\n当前：<b>${curText}</b>\n\n群内发送分类标签呼出的「在岗/下班/休息」列表，将在设定时间后自动删除（含触发关键词消息）。\n\n可选范围：<b>10 分钟～6 小时</b>，或关闭。`,
      kb, "HTML");
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 新增：处理点击设置分类标签
  if (data === "SETTINGS_TAGS") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_ADMIN_TAGS');
    const cancelKeyboard = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
    const currentTags = readFromFirebase('system_config/category_tags') || "未设置";
    // 🟢 修复：去除了示例标签两端的 <code> 标签
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `🏷️ <b>设置全局分类关键词</b>\n\n当前模板：\n<code>${currentTags}</code>\n\n👉 请在下方直接回复新的分类关键词（多个请用空格间隔，不需要带#）：\n\n示例：新人 御姐 大胸 无套\n\n<i>* 设置后，群员直接发送关键词即可呼出对应榜单。\n* 回复【清空】可删除标签功能。</i>`, cancelKeyboard, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：分级频道配置面板入口
  if (data === "SETTINGS_TIER_CHANNEL") {
    const tierChs = readFromFirebase('system_config/tier_channels') || {};
    const tierInfo =
      `🏆 A级频道：<code>${tierChs.A || '未配置'}</code>\n` +
      `⭐ B级频道：<code>${tierChs.B || '未配置'}</code>\n` +
      `📋 C级频道：<code>${tierChs.C || '未配置'}</code>`;
    const tierKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: "🏆 配置A级频道", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TIER_A" }, { text: "⭐ 配置B级频道", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TIER_B" }],
        [{ text: "📋 配置C级频道", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TIER_C" }],
        [{ text: "🔙 返回面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
      ]
    };
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, parse_mode: "HTML",
      reply_markup: tierKeyboard,
      text: `🏆 <b>频道分级配置</b>\n\n${tierInfo}\n\n` +
            `<b>📋 升降级规则：</b>\n` +
            `• 🔼 <b>升级</b>：C频道累积 <b>3篇好评</b> → 升至B频道；默认/B频道累积 <b>3篇好评</b> → 升至A频道\n` +
            `• 🔽 <b>降级</b>：A或B频道 <b>30天内无新车评</b> → 自动直降至C频道（每小时扫描）\n\n` +
            `<i>提示：三个频道均为可选配置，只配置A频道即可启用升级功能。</i>`
    });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  if (data === "SETTINGS_TIER_A" || data === "SETTINGS_TIER_B" || data === "SETTINGS_TIER_C") {
    const tierKey = data.replace("SETTINGS_TIER_", "");
    const tierNameMap3 = { A: '🏆 A级（高级）', B: '⭐ B级（标准）', C: '📋 C级（初级）' };
    const currentTierCh = readFromFirebase(`system_config/tier_channels/${tierKey}`) || '未配置';
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, `WAITING_FOR_ADMIN_TIER_${tierKey}`);
    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TIER_CHANNEL" }]] };
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, parse_mode: "HTML",
      reply_markup: cancelKb,
      text: `${tierNameMap3[tierKey]} <b>频道设置</b>\n\n当前ID：<code>${currentTierCh}</code>\n\n👉 请直接回复该频道的纯数字ID（带 -100 前缀）：\n<i>回复【清空】可取消此级别配置</i>`
    });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 修复：评分直推频道/群组配置入口（同时支持频道和群组）
  if (data === "SETTINGS_SCORE_PUSH") {
    const currentPush = readFromFirebase('system_config/score_push_channel') || '未配置';
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_ADMIN_SCORE_PUSH');
    const cancelKb2 = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消并返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, parse_mode: "HTML",
      reply_markup: cancelKb2,
      text: `📡 <b>评分直推配置（频道 / 群组）</b>\n\n当前ID：<code>${currentPush}</code>\n\n` +
            `<b>📌 功能说明：</b>\n` +
            `每条通过审核的车评将<b>额外</b>直接推送至指定目标，\n` +
            `并附带完整互动按钮（收藏/关注/写车评/老师回复）。\n\n` +
            `<b>📋 支持类型：</b>\n` +
            `• 📢 <b>频道</b>：需将机器人设为管理员，ID 如 <code>-1001234567890</code>\n` +
            `• 👥 <b>群组</b>：将机器人加入群组即可，ID 如 <code>-1234567890</code>\n\n` +
            `👉 请直接回复目标的纯数字ID：\n<i>回复【清空】可关闭此功能</i>`
    });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：处理点击绑定老师按钮，显示列表（带分页/搜索）并呼出原位编辑提示
  if (data.startsWith("SETTINGS_BIND_")) {
    // 拦截并处理搜索触发
    if (data === "SETTINGS_BIND_SEARCH_START") {
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_BIND_SEARCH');
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('prompt_msg_id_' + userId, messageId);
      const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消搜索", callback_data: "SETTINGS_BIND_0" }]] };
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
        chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: cancelKb,
        text: `🔍 <b>搜索绑定记录</b>\n\n👉 请在下方直接回复您要搜索的<b>老师名字</b>或<b>TG_ID</b>：`
      });
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
      return;
    }
    // 拦截并处理清除搜索
    if (data === "SETTINGS_BIND_SEARCH_CLEAR") {
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('bind_kw_' + userId);
      callbackQuery.data = "SETTINGS_BIND_0";
      return handleCallbackQuery(callbackQuery); // 递归调用，原位平滑刷新
    }

    const page = parseInt(data.replace("SETTINGS_BIND_", "")) || 0;
    // 依然设置等待输入状态，保证用户随时可以打字绑定新老师
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_ADMIN_BIND');
    // 🟢 新增：记录面板的消息ID，供后续绑定成功时自动删除以保持屏幕干净
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('prompt_msg_id_' + userId, messageId);
    // 读取当前是否有搜索关键词
    const keyword = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('bind_kw_' + userId) || "";

    // 读取目前数据库中所有的绑定记录，并与 teachers 交叉校验，剔除幽灵/脱节绑定
    const allAuths = readFromFirebase('teacher_auth') || {};
    const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
    let authList = [];
    const ghostAuthKeys = []; // 待清理的无效 teacher_auth 节点

    for (let tgId in allAuths) {
      const tName = allAuths[tgId];
      // 脏数据：值不是合法老师名字符串
      if (!tName || typeof tName !== 'string') {
        ghostAuthKeys.push(tgId);
        continue;
      }
      const tData = allTeachers[tName];
      // 幽灵：老师主节点已不存在（频道删帖/删老师后残留）
      if (!tData || typeof tData !== 'object') {
        ghostAuthKeys.push(tgId);
        continue;
      }
      // 脱节：老师身上挂的 tg_id / username 都对不上当前 key（历史双绑或半解绑残留）
      const boundId = tData.tg_id != null ? String(tData.tg_id) : '';
      const boundUser = tData.username ? String(tData.username) : '';
      if (boundId !== String(tgId) && boundUser !== String(tgId)) {
        ghostAuthKeys.push(tgId);
        continue;
      }
      // 关键词过滤：名字或 TG_ID / 用户名
      if (!keyword || tName.toLowerCase().includes(keyword.toLowerCase()) || String(tgId).toLowerCase().includes(keyword.toLowerCase())) {
        authList.push({ tgId: tgId, teacherName: tName });
      }
    }

    // 异步清理幽灵绑定，避免下次再出现；不阻塞面板渲染
    if (ghostAuthKeys.length > 0) {
      try {
        const ghostPatch = {};
        ghostAuthKeys.forEach(k => { ghostPatch[`teacher_auth/${k}`] = null; });
        updateFirebase('', ghostPatch);
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    // 分页处理：每页 10 位
    const pageSize = 10;
    const totalPages = Math.ceil(authList.length / pageSize) || 1;
    const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 0), totalPages - 1);
    const startIndex = safePage * pageSize;
    const displayAuths = authList.slice(startIndex, startIndex + pageSize);

    let text = `🔗 <b>绑定老师权限管理 (第 ${safePage + 1}/${totalPages} 页)</b>\n`;
    if (keyword) {
      text += `🔍 当前搜索：<code>${escapeHtml(keyword)}</code> (找到 ${authList.length} 条)\n\n`;
    } else {
      text += `共 ${authList.length} 位已绑定老师。\n\n`;
    }
    text += `👉 <b>【新增绑定】</b>：请直接在下方回复：\n<code>老师名字 真实TG_ID</code>\n(示例: 苍老师 123456789或苍老师 @XXX)\n\n👇 <b>【解绑/删除】</b>：点击下方对应按钮撤销权限：`;

    let keyboard = [];
    displayAuths.forEach((auth) => {
      keyboard.push([{ text: `❌ 解绑: ${auth.teacherName} (${auth.tgId})`, callback_data: `SETTINGS_UNBIND_${auth.tgId}_${safePage}` }]);
    });

    // 挂载翻页控制按钮
    let navRow = [];
    if (safePage > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `SETTINGS_BIND_${safePage - 1}` });
    if (safePage < totalPages - 1 && authList.length > 0) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `SETTINGS_BIND_${safePage + 1}` });
    if (navRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(navRow);
    
    // 挂载搜索控制按钮
    if (keyword) {
      keyboard.push([{ text: "✖️ 清除搜索结果", callback_data: "SETTINGS_BIND_SEARCH_CLEAR" }]);
    } else {
      keyboard.push([{ text: "🔍 搜索绑定记录", callback_data: "SETTINGS_BIND_SEARCH_START" }]);
    }
    keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回面板主页", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]);

    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: text,
      parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: keyboard }
    });

    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：处理解除老师绑定（提速：先秒回 callback + 一次 PATCH 批量清绑定）
  if (data.startsWith("SETTINGS_UNBIND_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    // 兼容 username 内含下划线：最后一段是页码，中间拼回完整标识
    const page = parts[parts.length - 1];
    const targetTgId = parts.slice(2, -1).join("_");

    // 🟢 提速 1：立刻结束按钮转圈，用户马上看到结果（后续写库/刷新在后台完成）
    // 注意：后面 SETTINGS_BIND_ 里会再调一次 answerCallbackQuery，第二次会被 TG 忽略，无影响
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `✅ 已解绑 ${targetTgId}`, false);

    const teacherName = readFromFirebase('teacher_auth/' + targetTgId);
    if (teacherName) {
      // 🟢 提速 2：读一次老师节点，拿到可能同时存在的 tg_id / username
      const tData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`) || {};

      // 🟢 提速 3：一次 multi-path PATCH 清掉所有相关节点（原先 3 次串行 DELETE）
      const patch = {};
      patch[`teacher_auth/${targetTgId}`] = null;
      patch[`teachers/${teacherName}/tg_id`] = null;
      patch[`teachers/${teacherName}/username`] = null;
      // 若老师身上还挂着另一套标识（例如既绑过数字 ID 又绑过用户名），一并清掉 auth
      if (tData.tg_id && String(tData.tg_id) !== String(targetTgId)) {
        patch[`teacher_auth/${tData.tg_id}`] = null;
      }
      if (tData.username && String(tData.username) !== String(targetTgId)) {
        patch[`teacher_auth/${tData.username}`] = null;
      }
      updateFirebase('', patch);
    } else {
      // 记录已不存在时仍删掉该 key，避免幽灵按钮
      deleteFirebase('teacher_auth/' + targetTgId);
    }

    // 解绑完成后，伪装回调刷新当前页（继承搜索与分页）
    callbackQuery.data = `SETTINGS_BIND_${page}`;
    return handleCallbackQuery(callbackQuery);
  }

  // 🟢 新增：处理车评管理页面 (限制展示长度并附带翻页功能)
  if (data.startsWith("SETTINGS_MANAGE_REVIEWS_")) {
    const page = parseInt(data.replace("SETTINGS_MANAGE_REVIEWS_", ""));
    const allReviews = readFromFirebase('reviews');
    let reviewList = [];
    
    if (allReviews) {
      for (let key in allReviews) {
        reviewList.push({ id: key, ...allReviews[key] });
      }
    }
    
    // 按时间倒序排序
    reviewList.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);

    if (reviewList.length === 0) {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, "📭 目前数据库没有任何车评记录。", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] });
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
      return;
    }

    const pageSize = 5; // 每页展示 5 条，防止按钮超出限制
    const totalPages = Math.ceil(reviewList.length / pageSize);
    const startIndex = page * pageSize;
    const displayReviews = reviewList.slice(startIndex, startIndex + pageSize);

    let keyboard = [];
    let text = `🗑️ <b>管理车评库 (第 ${page + 1}/${totalPages} 页)</b>\n共计 ${reviewList.length} 条记录。点击下方对应按钮进行删除操作：\n\n`;

    displayReviews.forEach((rev) => {
      const statusIcon = rev.status === 'approved' ? '✅' : (rev.status === 'rejected' ? '❌' : '⏳');
      text += `🆔 <code>${rev.id}</code>\n👤 老师: ${escapeHtml(rev.teacher) || '未知'} [${statusIcon}]\n---\n`;
      // 🟢 修改：将直接删除按钮改为“🔍 查看详情”，引导进入预览界面，防止误删
      keyboard.push([{ text: `🔍 查看详情: ${rev.teacher || '未知'} (${rev.id.slice(-4)})`, callback_data: `SETTINGS_VIEW_REVIEW_${rev.id}_${page}` }]);
    });

    // 动态挂载翻页控制按钮
    let navRow = [];
    if (page > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `SETTINGS_MANAGE_REVIEWS_${page - 1}` });
    if (page < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `SETTINGS_MANAGE_REVIEWS_${page + 1}` });
    if (navRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(navRow);
    
    // 🟢 将补发按钮加在返回主页的上方
    keyboard.push([{ text: "🔄 ID补发车评", callback_data: "SETTINGS_FORCE_SYNC_START" }]);
    keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回面板主页", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]);

    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, text, { inline_keyboard: keyboard });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

// 🟢 新增：【管理员】手动控制老师在岗状态 - 列表翻页入口
  if (data.startsWith("SETTINGS_TSTATUS_")) {
    if (data === "SETTINGS_TSTATUS_SEARCH_START") {
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_TSTATUS_SEARCH');
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('prompt_msg_id_' + userId, messageId);
      const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消搜索", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSTATUS_0" }]] };
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
        chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: cancelKb,
        text: `🔍 <b>搜索老师状态</b>\n\n👉 请在下方直接回复您要搜索的<b>老师名字</b>：`
      });
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
      return;
    }
    if (data === "SETTINGS_TSTATUS_SEARCH_CLEAR") {
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('tstatus_kw_' + userId);
      callbackQuery.data = "SETTINGS_TSTATUS_0";
      return handleCallbackQuery(callbackQuery); // 递归调用，原位平滑刷新
    }

    const page = parseInt(data.replace("SETTINGS_TSTATUS_", "")) || 0;
    sendAdminTeacherStatusList(chatId, messageId, page);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：【管理员】应用老师状态修改（格式：ATSS.{encodedName}.{status}.{page}）
  // 用点号"."做分隔符是因为 base64WebSafe 编码中不含"."，不会产生歧义
  if (data.startsWith("ATSS.")) {
    const parts = data.slice(5).split(".");
    // 去掉前缀 "ATSS." 再按点切割
    const encodedName  = parts[0];
    // 编码后的老师名
    const targetStatus = parts[1]; // online | offline | leave
    const page         = parseInt(parts[2]) || 0;

    if (!encodedName || !["online", "offline", "leave"].includes(targetStatus)) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 解析失败，请重试。", true);
      return;
    }

    let teacherName = "";
    try {
      teacherName = decodeTeacherFromDeepLink(encodedName);
    } catch (e) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 老师名字解析失败，请重试。", true);
      return;
    }

    const statusMap  = { "online": "🟢 在岗", "offline": "🔴 下班", "leave": "🟡 休息" };
    const timeString = new Date().toLocaleString('zh-CN', { timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai' });

    // 管理员代打卡：更新 Firebase 老师状态
    updateFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`, {
      status: targetStatus,
      last_check_in: new Date().getTime()
    });

    // 记录管理员代打卡操作日志（与老师自己打卡的日志格式保持一致）
    writeToFirebase(`checkin_logs/${teacherName}/${new Date().getTime()}`, {
      status: targetStatus,
      status_text: statusMap[targetStatus],
      operated_by: `admin:${userId}`,   // 标注是管理员操作，便于日后审计
      timestamp: new Date().getTime(),
      time_string: timeString
    });

    // 🟢 关键修复：先响应按钮点击 + 刷新管理员列表面板，
    // 避免被下面耗时的"群内榜单刷新 + 关注者群发通知"拖慢甚至卡死，
    // 导致 Telegram 客户端一直转圈、看起来"点击无反应"
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `✅ ${teacherName} 已设为 ${statusMap[targetStatus]}`, true);
    sendAdminTeacherStatusList(chatId, messageId, page);

    // 🟢 关键修复：耗时的广播操作放到最后，并用 try/catch 包裹，
    // 即使群多、关注者多导致执行变慢，或中途抛出异常，也不会影响上面已经完成的打卡和按钮响应
    try {
      refreshAllTeacherStatusBoards();
      // 🟢 新增：同步刷新所有公网定时消息里的动态状态按钮
      syncScheduledMessagesStatus(teacherName);
      notifyTeacherFollowers(teacherName, statusMap[targetStatus]);
    } catch (err) {
      Logger.log("⚠️ 状态广播/通知失败: " + err);
    }
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：数据安全预览界面。展示详细车评数据供管理员核对，提供二次确认
  if (data.startsWith("SETTINGS_VIEW_REVIEW_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const revId = parts[3];
    const page = parts[4];
    
    // 从 Firebase 读取该条车评的完整结构
    const rev = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${revId}`);
    if (!rev) {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, "⚠️ 该车评数据不存在或已被提前删除。", { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回列表", callback_data: `SETTINGS_MANAGE_REVIEWS_${page}` }]] });
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
      return;
    }
    
    // 🟢 修复：匹配最新的全维度评分字段，抛弃旧的 rating/price 字段
    const mediaNote = rev.media ? (rev.media_type === 'photo' ? '🖼️ 附带图片' : '🎞️ 附带视频') : '无媒体文件';
    let detailText = `🔍 车评内容详细预览\n`;
    detailText += `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
    detailText += `🆔 车评编号: <code>${revId}</code>\n`;
    detailText += `👤 老师名字: #${escapeHtml(rev.teacher || '未填写')}\n`;
    detailText += `📷 人照评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.photos || '未填写')}\n`;
    detailText += `✨ 颜值评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.looks || '未填写')}\n`;
    detailText += `💃 身材评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.body || '未填写')}\n`;
    detailText += `💋 服务评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.service || '未填写')}\n`;
    detailText += `💓 态度评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.attitude || '未填写')}\n`;
    detailText += `🏡 环境评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.env || '未填写')}\n`;
    detailText += `💰 性价比评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.cp || '未填写')}\n`;
    detailText += `👤 留名用户: ${escapeHtml(rev.reviewer || '匿名')}\n`;
    detailText += `📝 过程描述:\n${escapeHtml(rev.process || '（无纯文字描述内容）')}\n`;
    detailText += `📎 附件状态: ${mediaNote}\n`;
    detailText += `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
    detailText += `⚠️ 您确定要将以上车评永久从数据库删除吗？此操作不可撤销！`;
    
    const previewKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [
          { text: "🔄 强制重新同步到频道评论区", callback_data: `MYREV_RESUB_${revId}_${page}` }
        ],
        [
          { text: "💥 确认永久删除", callback_data: `SETTINGS_DEL_REVIEW_${revId}_${page}`, style: "danger" },
          { text: "🔙 返回列表", callback_data: `SETTINGS_MANAGE_REVIEWS_${page}`, style: "primary" }
        ]
      ]
    };
    
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, detailText, previewKeyboard, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：执行指定车评的删除，并在原位提供返回入口
  if (data.startsWith("SETTINGS_DEL_REVIEW_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const revId = parts[3];
    const page = parts[4];
    
    // 🟢 修复：删除前先获取老师信息，并在删除后同步扣减该老师的全维度评分
    const rev = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${revId}`);

    // 🟢 核心修复：同步删除评论区里这条车评的实际消息，避免数据库删了但频道评论区仍残留
    if (rev && rev.group_chat_id && rev.group_msg_id) {
      try {
        // 🛠️ 修复：强制转换数据类型
        sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: String(rev.group_chat_id), message_id: Number(rev.group_msg_id) });
      } catch (e) {
        // 如果消息已被手动删除或机器人无权限，捕获错误以防脚本中断
      }
    }

    // 🟢 核心新增：如果该车评当初是匿名提交扣过积分，被管理员删除时自动退还给原作者
    refundAnonPoints(rev);
    
    // 从 Firebase 彻底删除该节点
    deleteFirebase(`reviews/${revId}`);

    // 如果删掉的是已生效的车评，重新计算并刷新频道面板
    if (rev && rev.status === 'approved' && rev.teacher) {
      recalculateAndSyncTeacherScore(rev.teacher);
    }
    
    const redirectKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: "🔙 返回车评列表", callback_data: `SETTINGS_MANAGE_REVIEWS_${page}` }],
        [{ text: "🔙 返回面板主页", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
      ]
    };
    
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `✅ 订单编号 <code>${revId}</code> 的车评已从数据库中永久删除。`, redirectKeyboard);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已删除");
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【报告榜】多周期无缝切换及翻页回调
  if (data.startsWith("RB_PERIOD_")) {
    const period = data.replace("RB_PERIOD_", "");
    sendReportBoard(chatId, messageId, period, 0);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  if (data.startsWith("RB_PAGE_")) {
    const parts = data.replace("RB_PAGE_", "").split("_");
    sendReportBoard(chatId, messageId, parts[0], parseInt(parts[1]));
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【公开榜】多周期无缝切换及翻页回调
  if (data.startsWith("PB_PERIOD_")) {
    const period = data.replace("PB_PERIOD_", "");
    sendPublicBoard(chatId, messageId, period, 0);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  if (data.startsWith("PB_PAGE_")) {
    const parts = data.replace("PB_PAGE_", "").split("_");
    sendPublicBoard(chatId, messageId, parts[0], parseInt(parts[1]));
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【新人榜】多周期无缝切换及翻页回调
  if (data.startsWith("NB_PERIOD_")) {
    const period = data.replace("NB_PERIOD_", "");
    sendNewcomerBoard(chatId, messageId, period, 0);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  if (data.startsWith("NB_PAGE_")) {
    const parts = data.replace("NB_PAGE_", "").split("_");
    sendNewcomerBoard(chatId, messageId, parts[0], parseInt(parts[1]));
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【📚开课】在岗老师列表翻页
  if (data.startsWith("OK_LIST_")) {
    const page = parseInt(data.replace("OK_LIST_", ""));
    sendOpenClassList(chatId, userId, messageId, page, null);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【查车评】搜索结果翻页
  if (data.startsWith("SREV_PAGE_")) {
    const page = parseInt(data.replace("SREV_PAGE_", ""));
    sendSearchReviewList(chatId, userId, messageId, page, null);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【查车评】查看某条车评的完整详情
  if (data.startsWith("SREV_VIEW_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const revId = parts[2];
    const fromPage = parseInt(parts[3]) || 0;
    const srev = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${revId}`);
    if (!srev || srev.status !== 'approved') {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 该车评不存在或已被撤回。", true);
      return;
    }
    const mediaNote = srev.media ? (srev.media_type === 'photo' ? '🖼️ 附带图片' : '🎞️ 附带视频') : '无附件';
    const srevDetail = `🚘 <b>车评详情</b>\n` +
                       `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n` +
                       `【老师】：${escapeHtml(srev.teacher) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `【人照】：${escapeHtml(srev.photos) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `【颜值】：${escapeHtml(srev.looks) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `【身材】：${escapeHtml(srev.body) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `【服务】：${escapeHtml(srev.service) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `【态度】：${escapeHtml(srev.attitude) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `【环境】：${escapeHtml(srev.env) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `【性价比】：${escapeHtml(srev.cp) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `【留名】：${escapeHtml(srev.reviewer) || '匿名'}\n` +
                       `【附件】：${mediaNote}\n` +
                       `【过程】：${srev.process && srev.process.length > 500 ? '\n<blockquote expandable>' + escapeHtml(srev.process) + '</blockquote>' : escapeHtml(srev.process) || '未填'}\n` +
                       `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━`;
    const srevBackKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 返回搜索列表", callback_data: `SREV_PAGE_${fromPage}` }]] };
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: srevDetail, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: srevBackKb });
    // 🟢 如含媒体文件，额外单独发送一条媒体消息（文字消息无法内嵌图/视频）
    if (srev.media) {
      const mp = { chat_id: chatId, caption: `📎 <b>${escapeHtml(srev.teacher)}</b> 的车评附件`, parse_mode: "HTML" };
      if (srev.media_type === 'photo') { mp.photo = srev.media; sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", mp); }
      else if (srev.media_type === 'video') { mp.video = srev.media; sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", mp); }
    }
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【我的车评】面板翻页
  if (data.startsWith("MYREV_PAGE_")) {
    const page = parseInt(data.replace("MYREV_PAGE_", ""));
    sendMyReviewsList(chatId, userId, messageId, page);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【我的车评】点击预览详情
  if (data.startsWith("MYREV_PV_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const revId = parts[2];
    const page = parts[3];

    const rev = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${revId}`);
    if (!rev) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 该车评数据不存在或已被删除。", true);
      sendMyReviewsList(chatId, userId, messageId, page);
      return;
    }

    // 强隔离鉴权：只允许原作者查看详细内容
    if (String(rev.userId) !== String(userId)) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "🚷 您无权查看他人的车评数据！", true);
      return;
    }

    const statusIcon = rev.status === 'approved' ? '✅已发布' : (rev.status === 'rejected' ? '❌已拒绝' : '⏳审核中');
    const mediaNote = rev.media ? (rev.media_type === 'photo' ? '🖼️ 附带图片' : '🎞️ 附带视频') : '无媒体文件';

    let pvText = `🔍 车评详情预览 (订单: <code>${revId}</code>)\n`;
    pvText += `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
    pvText += `当前状态: ${statusIcon}\n`;
    pvText += `目标老师: ${escapeHtml(rev.teacher) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `人照评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.photos) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `颜值评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.looks) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `身材评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.body) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `服务评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.service) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `态度评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.attitude) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `环境评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.env) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `性价比评分: ${escapeHtml(rev.cp) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `留名用户: ${escapeHtml(rev.reviewer) || '匿名'}\n`;
    pvText += `过程描述:\n${rev.process && rev.process.length > 500 ? '<blockquote expandable>' + escapeHtml(rev.process) + '</blockquote>' : escapeHtml(rev.process) || '未填'}\n`;
    pvText += `附件状态: ${mediaNote}\n`;
    pvText += `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━`;

    const pvKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [
          { text: rev.delete_pending ? "⏳ 删除审核中..." : "💥 申请删除此车评", callback_data: `MYREV_DEL_${revId}_${page}` },
          { text: "🔙 返回列表", callback_data: `MYREV_PAGE_${page}` }
        ]
      ]
    };

    // 手动组装 Payload 确保 parse_mode 正确解析 HTML 标签
    const payload = { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: pvText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: pvKeyboard };
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", payload);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已成功载入预览");
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 核心修改：处理【我的车评】删除请求（改为向管理员提交删除申请）
  if (data.startsWith("MYREV_DEL_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const revId = parts[2];
    const page = parseInt(parts[3]);

    const rev = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${revId}`);
    // 二次鉴权：拦截恶意构造回调数据去删除他人车评的行为
    if (rev && String(rev.userId) === String(userId)) {
       if (rev.delete_pending) {
         answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 您的删除申请正在审核中，请耐心等待！", true);
         return;
       }
       
       // 标记该车评正在申请删除
       writeToFirebase(`reviews/${revId}/delete_pending`, true);
       
       // 通知所有管理员审核删除请求
       const adminText = `⚠️ <b>【车评删除申请】</b>\n` +
                         `用户ID: <code>${userId}</code> 申请删除其发布的车评\n` +
                         `订单编号: <code>${revId}</code>\n` +
                         `目标老师: ${escapeHtml(rev.teacher) || '未填'}\n` +
                         `当前状态: ${rev.status === 'approved' ? '已发布' : (rev.status === 'rejected' ? '已拒绝' : '审核中')}\n` +
                         `-------------------\n` +
                         `【过程描述】:\n${escapeHtml(rev.process) || '未填'}\n`;
                         
       const adminKeyboard = {
         inline_keyboard: [
           [
             { text: "✅ 批准删除", callback_data: `ADMIN_APVDEL_${userId}_${revId}`, style: "success" },
             { text: "❌ 拒绝并需回复理由", callback_data: `ADMIN_REJDEL_${userId}_${revId}`, style: "danger" }
           ]
         ]
       };

       ADMIN_USER_IDS.forEach(adminId => {
         sendMessage(Number(adminId), adminText, adminKeyboard, "HTML");
       });
       
       answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "✅ 删除申请已提交！需等待管理员审核。", true);
    } else {
       answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "❌ 删除申请失败：数据不存在或您无权操作！", true);
    }

    // 强制自动原位刷新当前列表
    sendMyReviewsList(chatId, userId, messageId, page);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增路由：处理【我的车评】强制重新同步请求
  if (data.startsWith("MYREV_RESUB_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const revId = parts[2];
    const page = parseInt(parts[3]);

    const reviewData = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${revId}`);

    // 🟢 核心修复：获取管理员权限标识
    const isAdmin = ADMIN_USER_IDS.map(String).includes(String(userId));

    // 二次鉴权与状态验证 (管理员可绕过归属权校验)
    if (!reviewData || (!isAdmin && String(reviewData.userId) !== String(userId)) || reviewData.status !== 'approved') {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "❌ 操作失败：数据不存在、您无权操作，或该车评尚未通过审核！", true);
      return;
    }

    // 尝试先删除可能存在的旧评论区消息（防重复发送堆积）
    if (reviewData.group_chat_id && reviewData.group_msg_id) {
      try { 
        // 🛠️ 修复：强制转换数据类型
        sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: String(reviewData.group_chat_id), message_id: Number(reviewData.group_msg_id) });
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    // 重新组装发布到群组的文案 (完全复用管理员通过时的排版)
    const mediaNote = reviewData.media ? (reviewData.media_type === 'photo' ? '🖼️ 附带图片' : '🎞️ 附带视频') : '无媒体文件';
    const publishTime = new Date(reviewData.timestamp).toLocaleString('zh-CN', {timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai'});
    
    // 动态提取老师当前的打分数据进行拼接
    const teacherData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${reviewData.teacher}`) || {};
    const tAvg = teacherData.average_score || "0.00";
    let ratingLabel = "";
    if (parseFloat(tAvg) >= 8.00) ratingLabel = " 🌟<b>[好评]</b>";
    else if (parseFloat(tAvg) >= 5.00) ratingLabel = " ⭐<b>[中评]</b>";
    else if (parseFloat(tAvg) > 0) ratingLabel = " 💔<b>[差评]</b>";

    const rawScorePanel = `📊 <b>【全维度评分】: ${tAvg > 0 ? tAvg + ' 分' : '暂无'}</b>${ratingLabel} (基于 ${teacherData.total_reviews || 0} 份真实车评)\n` +
                          `👤 最新留名: ${escapeHtml(reviewData.reviewer) || '暂无'}\n` +
                          `-------------------\n`;

    const safeProcess = reviewData.process && reviewData.process.length > 500 ? '\n<blockquote expandable>' + escapeHtml(reviewData.process) + '</blockquote>' : escapeHtml(reviewData.process) || '未填';
    
    const publishText = `📢 <b>【车评重新同步补发】</b>\n⏱ <b>原发布时间:</b> ${publishTime}\n\n` +
                        `【老师】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.teacher) || '未填'}\n` +
                        rawScorePanel +
                        `【人照】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.photos) || '未填'}\n` +
                        `【颜值】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.looks) || '未填'}\n` +
                        `【身材】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.body) || '未填'}\n` +
                        `【服务】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.service) || '未填'}\n` +
                        `【态度】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.attitude) || '未填'}\n` +
                        `【环境】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.env) || '未填'}\n` +
                        `【性价比】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.cp) || '未填'}\n` +
                        `【留名】：${escapeHtml(reviewData.reviewer) || '匿名'}\n` +
                        `【过程】：${safeProcess}\n` +
                        `【上传】：${mediaNote}\n`;

    // 重新发布到频道或群组
    // 🟢 核心修复 3：补发机制同步兼容 A/B/C 频道体系
    if (PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID || TIER_CHANNELS.A || TIER_CHANNELS.B || TIER_CHANNELS.C) {
      let targetChatId = PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID;
      let replyToMessageId = null;
      let teacherTgId = null;

      if (teacherData) {
                // 🟢 优先读取群组的 ID 和群组转发消息的 ID，以实现在评论区群内引用回复 (适用于所有已关联群组的频道)
                if (teacherData.group_id && teacherData.group_message_id) {
                  targetChatId = teacherData.group_id;
                  replyToMessageId = teacherData.group_message_id;
                } else {
                  // 🟢 修复：通过 getChat 查找频道留言群组，确保补发也出现在留言区
                  const _fallbackChId2 = teacherData.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID;
                  try {
                    const _gcRes2 = sendTelegramMessage("getChat", { chat_id: _fallbackChId2 });
                    const _gcData2 = JSON.parse(_gcRes2.getContentText());
                    if (_gcData2.ok && _gcData2.result && _gcData2.result.linked_chat_id) {
                      const _linkedChatId2 = _gcData2.result.linked_chat_id;
                      // 🟢 修复：将发现的留言群组 ID 写入 Firebase，后续补发可直接复用
                      let _freshData = teacherData;
                      if (!teacherData.group_id) {
                        writeToFirebase(`teachers/${reviewData.teacher}/group_id`, _linkedChatId2);
                        // 🟢 修复：再等一轮，给 Webhook 抓取 group_message_id 更多时间
                        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
                          Utilities.sleep(1500);
                          _freshData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${reviewData.teacher}`) || {};
                          if (_freshData.group_message_id) break;
                        }
                      }
                      targetChatId = _linkedChatId2;
                      replyToMessageId = _freshData.group_message_id || null; // 🟢 修复：捕获到则精准引用
                    } else {
                      targetChatId = _fallbackChId2;
                      replyToMessageId = teacherData.message_id;
                    }
                  } catch (_gcErr2) {
                    targetChatId = _fallbackChId2;
                    replyToMessageId = teacherData.message_id;
                  }
                }
        if (teacherData.username) teacherTgId = `https://t.me/${teacherData.username}`;
        else if (teacherData.tg_id) teacherTgId = `tg://user?id=${teacherData.tg_id}`;
      }

      const contactUrl = teacherTgId ? teacherTgId : `${BOT_USERNAME_URL}?start=contact`;
      const publishKeyboard = {
        inline_keyboard: [
          [{ text: `直连${reviewData.teacher}`, url: contactUrl }],
          [{ text: "⭐️ 收藏老师", callback_data: `FAV_${encodeTeacherForDeepLink(reviewData.teacher)}` }, { text: "💖 关注动态", callback_data: `FL_${encodeTeacherForDeepLink(reviewData.teacher)}` }],
          // 🟢 核心修复：改为 url 深度链接跳转，点击后瞬间拉起私聊并进入打分面板
          [{ text: `💬 老师回复`, url: `${BOT_USERNAME_URL}?start=reply_${revId}` }]
        ]
      };

      let publishSendResult = null;
      const payload = { chat_id: targetChatId, text: publishText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: publishKeyboard };
      if (replyToMessageId) {
        payload.reply_to_message_id = replyToMessageId;
        payload.allow_sending_without_reply = true; // 🟢 防止补发时留言区原贴失效导致报错
      }

      if (reviewData.media) {
        payload.caption = publishText;
        delete payload.text; // 避免 text 与 caption 冲突
        if (reviewData.media_type === 'photo') { 
          payload.photo = reviewData.media;
          publishSendResult = sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload); 
        } else if (reviewData.media_type === 'video') { 
          payload.video = reviewData.media;
          publishSendResult = sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload); 
        }
      } else {
        publishSendResult = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
      }

      // 覆盖保存最新的评论区消息 ID，保证后续删除操作仍然精准生效
      try {
        if (publishSendResult) {
          const resObj = JSON.parse(publishSendResult.getContentText());
          if (resObj.ok && resObj.result) {
            updateFirebase(`reviews/${revId}`, {
              group_chat_id: String(resObj.result.chat.id),
              group_msg_id: resObj.result.message_id
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {}
    }
    
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "✅ 已强制重新同步！请前往频道/群组评论区查看。", true);
    return;
  }

  if (data === "CLOSE") {
    // 🟢 新增：若管理员是在回复投诉/建议途中取消，则释放认领锁，允许其他管理员接手
    const closingState = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('state_' + userId);
    if (closingState && closingState.startsWith('WAITING_FOR_FBREPLY_')) {
      const closingRaw = closingState.replace('WAITING_FOR_FBREPLY_', '');
      const closingParts = closingRaw.split('_');
      const closingFeedbackId = closingParts.length >= 2 ? closingParts.slice(1).join('_') : null;
      if (closingFeedbackId) {
        // 重置为未认领状态，让其他管理员可以接手
        updateFirebase(`feedback_queue/${closingFeedbackId}`, {
          handled: false,
          replied: false,
          handlerName: null,
          handlerId: null
        });
      }
    }

    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, "✖️ 已关闭。", null);
    // 清理缓存状态
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_' + userId);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
    // 🟢 修复：完全关闭面板时，自动清除搜索关键词缓存
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('bind_kw_' + userId);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('tstatus_kw_' + userId);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('tstatus_kw_' + chatId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：处理“取消输入并返回”按钮，原位无缝恢复打分大面板
    if (data === "CANCEL_INPUT") {
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('prompt_msg_id_' + userId);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_' + userId);
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, "ℹ️ 车评填写功能已关闭。", null);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "车评功能已关闭");
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：处理老师打卡状态切换的回调数据
  if (data.startsWith("TSTATUS_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_"); 
    const targetStatus = parts[1];
    const authorizedUserId = parts[2];
    
    // 防止其他用户越权点击群组/历史消息里的按钮
    if (String(userId) !== String(authorizedUserId)) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 您只能操作属于您自己的打卡面板！");
      return;
    }
    
    // 🟢 修复：关闭按钮绑定 owner，无需查 Firebase，直接关闭消息
    if (targetStatus === "close") {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, "✖️ 开课控制台已关闭。如需重新开始请发送 /start", null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已关闭");
      return;
    }

    const teacherName = readFromFirebase('teacher_auth/' + userId);
    if (!teacherName) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "❌ 未找到您的老师权限记录。");
      return;
    }
    
    // 🟢 修复 1：使用 PATCH 方法一次性提交，并在打卡时自动抓取真实的老师 TG_ID 和 用户名
    const teacherUsername = callbackQuery.from.username || "";
    updateFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`, {
      status: targetStatus,
      last_check_in: new Date().getTime(),
      tg_id: userId,
      username: teacherUsername
    });
    
    const statusMap = {
      "online": "🟢 在岗",
      "offline": "🔴 下班",
      "leave": "🟡 休息"
    };
    const timeString = new Date().toLocaleString('zh-CN', {timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai'});
    
    // 🟢 修复 2：动态重构键盘状态，实现原位编辑。当前状态对应按钮会高亮为 ✅
    const updatedKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [
          { text: targetStatus === "online" ?
         "✅ 在岗" : "🟢 在岗", callback_data: `TSTATUS_online_${userId}` },
          { text: targetStatus === "offline" ?
          "✅ 下班" : "🔴 下班", callback_data: `TSTATUS_offline_${userId}` }
        ],
        [
          { text: targetStatus === "leave" ? "✅ 休息" : "🟡 休息", callback_data: `TSTATUS_leave_${userId}` },
          { text: "✖️ 关闭", callback_data: `TSTATUS_close_${userId}` }
        ]
      ]
    };
    
    // 🟢 修复 3：将 null 替换为 updatedKeyboard，打卡后保留面板供下次直接操作
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, `✅ <b>打卡状态更新成功！</b>\n\n老师姓名：<b>${escapeHtml(teacherName)}</b>\n当前状态：<b>${statusMap[targetStatus]}</b>\n更新时间：${timeString}`, updatedKeyboard);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "打卡成功");

    // 🟢 新增：详细记录每一次打卡历史（按时间戳追加，不覆盖旧记录），便于后续审计/统计在岗率
    // 同时这也保证了 teachers/{name}.status 与本次打卡严格同步，群内在岗榜读取的就是这里写入的最新状态
    writeToFirebase(`checkin_logs/${teacherName}/${new Date().getTime()}`, {
      status: targetStatus,
      status_text: statusMap[targetStatus],
      tg_id: userId,
      username: teacherUsername,
      timestamp: new Date().getTime(),
      time_string: timeString
    });

    // 🟢 新增：打卡状态一变动，立刻把所有群里已经展示出来的【在岗榜】消息原位刷新一遍，做到实时同步
    refreshAllTeacherStatusBoards();
    // 🟢 新增：同步刷新所有公网定时消息里的动态状态按钮
    syncScheduledMessagesStatus(teacherName);
    // 🟢 新增：打卡状态变动时，异步通知所有关注该老师动态的用户
    notifyTeacherFollowers(teacherName, statusMap[targetStatus]);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：群内【小姐姐在线列表 / 技师在岗榜】翻页回调
  if (data.startsWith("TLIST_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    // 🟢 核心修复：兼容 STATIC 面板的翻页回调，防止 parseInt('STATIC') 变为 NaN 导致面板瘫痪
    const tagIndex = parts[1] === 'STATIC' ? 'STATIC' : parseInt(parts[1]);
    const page = parseInt(parts[2]);
    sendTeacherStatusBoard(chatId, tagIndex, page, messageId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：⭐️ 收藏老师按钮回调（点击切换收藏/取消收藏）
  if (data.startsWith("FAV_")) {
    const encodedTeacher = data.replace("FAV_", "");
    let teacherName = "";
    try { teacherName = decodeTeacherFromDeepLink(encodedTeacher); } catch (e) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 解析失败，请重试。", true); return;
    }
    const favPath = `user_profiles/${userId}/favorites/${teacherName}`;
    if (readFromFirebase(favPath)) {
      deleteFirebase(favPath);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `❌ 已取消收藏：${teacherName}`, false);
    } else {
      writeToFirebase(favPath, new Date().getTime());
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `⭐️ 已收藏！可在【👤个人中心】查看`, false);
    }
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：💖 关注动态按钮回调（点击切换关注/取消关注）
  if (data.startsWith("FL_")) {
    const encodedTeacher = data.replace("FL_", "");
    let teacherName = "";
    try { teacherName = decodeTeacherFromDeepLink(encodedTeacher); } catch (e) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 解析失败，请重试。", true); return;
    }
    const flPath = `user_profiles/${userId}/follows/${teacherName}`;
    if (readFromFirebase(flPath)) {
      deleteFirebase(flPath);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `❌ 已取消关注：${teacherName}`, false);
    } else {
      writeToFirebase(flPath, new Date().getTime());
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `💖 已关注！老师打卡时将收到通知`, false);
    }
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：个人中心 - 我的收藏列表
  if (data === "CENTER_FAV") {
    const profile = readFromFirebase(`user_profiles/${userId}`) || {};
    const favorites = profile.favorites ? Object.keys(profile.favorites) : [];
    if (favorites.length === 0) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⭐️ 您还没有收藏任何老师", true); return;
    }
    let rows = [];
    let deletedCount = 0;
    favorites.forEach(name => {
      const td = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${name}`) || {};
      if (!td.message_id) {
        // 资料已被删除：自动同步移除 Firebase 中的收藏记录
        deleteFirebase(`user_profiles/${userId}/favorites/${name}`);
        deletedCount++;
        return;
      }
      const avg = td.average_score || '暂无';
      // 🟢 修复：使用老师自己的 channel_id，兼容分级频道
      const targetChId = String(td.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID).replace(/^-100/, '');
      const url = `https://t.me/c/${targetChId}/${td.message_id}`;
      const encoded = encodeTeacherForDeepLink(name);
      rows.push({ name, avg, url, encoded });
    });
    if (rows.length === 0) {
      const tip = deletedCount > 0
        ? `⭐️ 您收藏的 ${deletedCount} 位老师资料均已被删除，列表已自动清空`
        : "⭐️ 您还没有收藏任何老师";
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, tip, true); return;
    }
    let text = `⭐️ <b>我的收藏列表</b>（共 ${rows.length} 位）\n`;
    if (deletedCount > 0) text += `⚠️ 已自动移除 ${deletedCount} 位资料已删除老师\n`;
    text += `\n`;
    let keyboard = [];
    rows.forEach(({ name, avg, url, encoded }) => {
      text += `⭐️ ${escapeHtml(name)}（综合评分: ${avg}）\n`;
      keyboard.push([
        { text: `👁️ 查看: ${name}`, url: url },
        { text: `🗑 取消收藏`, callback_data: `FAV_${encoded}` }
      ]);
    });
    keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回个人中心", callback_data: "CENTER_BACK" }]);
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId,
      text: text, parse_mode: "HTML",
      reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: keyboard }
    });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：个人中心 - 我的关注列表（含实时在岗状态）
  if (data === "CENTER_FL") {
    const profile = readFromFirebase(`user_profiles/${userId}`) || {};
    const follows = profile.follows ? Object.keys(profile.follows) : [];
    if (follows.length === 0) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "💖 您还没有关注任何老师的动态", true); return;
    }
    let rows = [];
    let deletedCount = 0;
    follows.forEach(name => {
      const td = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${name}`) || {};
      if (!td.message_id) {
        // 资料已被删除：自动同步移除 Firebase 中的关注记录
        deleteFirebase(`user_profiles/${userId}/follows/${name}`);
        deletedCount++;
        return;
      }
      const statusEmoji = td.status === 'online' ? '🟢' : (td.status === 'leave' ? '🟡' : '🔴');
