      // 🟢 修复：使用老师自己的 channel_id，兼容分级频道
      const targetChId = String(td.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID).replace(/^-100/, '');
      const url = `https://t.me/c/${targetChId}/${td.message_id}`;
      const encoded = encodeTeacherForDeepLink(name);
      rows.push({ name, statusEmoji, url, encoded });
    });
    if (rows.length === 0) {
      const tip = deletedCount > 0
        ? `💖 您关注的 ${deletedCount} 位老师资料均已被删除，列表已自动清空`
        : "💖 您还没有关注任何老师的动态";
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, tip, true); return;
    }
    let text = `💖 <b>我的关注列表</b>（共 ${rows.length} 位）\n`;
    if (deletedCount > 0) text += `⚠️ 已自动移除 ${deletedCount} 位资料已删除老师\n`;
    text += `\n`;
    let keyboard = [];
    rows.forEach(({ name, statusEmoji, url, encoded }) => {
      text += `${statusEmoji} ${escapeHtml(name)}\n`;
      keyboard.push([
        { text: `${statusEmoji} 查看: ${name}`, url: url },
        { text: `💔 取消关注`, callback_data: `FL_${encoded}` }
      ]);
    });
    keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回个人中心", callback_data: "CENTER_BACK" }]);
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId,
      text: text, parse_mode: "HTML",
      reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: keyboard }
    });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：返回个人中心主面板
  if (data === "CENTER_BACK") {
    const profile = readFromFirebase(`user_profiles/${userId}`) || {};
    // 🟢 修复：积分为 0 时不能当成"未设置"处理，否则会误显示成 100
    const points = profile.points !== undefined ? profile.points : 100;
    const favorites = profile.favorites ? Object.keys(profile.favorites) : [];
    const follows = profile.follows ? Object.keys(profile.follows) : [];
    const centerText = `👤 <b>个人中心</b>\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n🆔 用户ID：<code>${userId}</code>\n💰 当前积分：${points}\n⭐️ 收藏老师：${favorites.length} 位\n💖 关注动态：${follows.length} 位\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━`;
    const centerKeyboard = {
      inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: `⭐️ 我的收藏 (${favorites.length})`, callback_data: "CENTER_FAV" }, { text: `💖 我的关注 (${follows.length})`, callback_data: "CENTER_FL" }],
        [{ text: "✖️ 关闭", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]
      ]
    };
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: centerText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: centerKeyboard });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 3. 用户点击【默认提交】或【匿名提交】 -> 私发给管理员进行审核
    if (data === "SUBMIT_DEFAULT" || data === "SUBMIT_ANON") {
    try {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, "ℹ️ 车评填写功能已关闭。", null);
    } catch (e) {}
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_' + userId);
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "车评功能已关闭");
    return;
  }

  // 4. 管理员审核操作 (在私聊中触发)
  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_APPROVE_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const submitterId = parts[2];
    const reviewId = parts[3];

    // 🟢 核心修复：检查当前车评状态，防止多个管理员重复操作
    const currentReview = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`);
    if (!currentReview || currentReview.status !== 'pending') {
      const statusText = currentReview && currentReview.status === 'approved' ? '已通过' : '已拒绝/已失效';
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n⚠️ 处理结果：已被其他管理员处理 (${statusText})`, null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "该车评已被处理，无法重复操作！");
      return;
    }

    const originalText = callbackQuery.message.text.split('-------------------')[1].trim();
    
    // 🟢 安全过滤：防止用户填写的 < 或 > 符号破坏频道的 HTML 结构导致发送失败
    let safeText = originalText.replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");

    // 🟢 新增：智能提取过程描述并校验长度。超过500字时自动套用 Telegram 原生可折叠引用块
    safeText = safeText.replace(/【过程】：([\s\S]*?)(?=【上传】：|$)/, (match, p1) => {
      let content = p1.trim();
      if (content.length > 500) {
        return `【过程】：\n<blockquote expandable>${content}</blockquote>\n`;
      }
      return `【过程】：${content}\n`;
    });

    // 🟢 核心升级：自动计算全部类目的评分，并动态刷新到频道的【老师资料卡原帖】上
    const teacherName = currentReview.teacher;
    const teacherData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`) || {};
    
    // 1. 初始化各维度的历史分数和有效次数
    const scoreFields = ['photos', 'looks', 'body', 'service', 'attitude', 'env', 'cp'];
    let avgScores = teacherData.avg_scores || { photos: 0, looks: 0, body: 0, service: 0, attitude: 0, env: 0, cp: 0 };
    let validCounts = teacherData.valid_counts || { photos: 0, looks: 0, body: 0, service: 0, attitude: 0, env: 0, cp: 0 };
    let currentTotalReviews = teacherData.total_reviews || 0;
    // 🟢 恢复：重新启用车评次数累计。每当有新评分通过审核，真实车评数自动 +1
    currentTotalReviews += 1;

    // 2. 🟢 修复：提取本次打分，与历史记录进行加权平均累加，实现真正的全维度综合评估
    const submitterWeight = currentReview.weight || 1.0; // 兼容没有权重的老数据

    scoreFields.forEach(field => {
      const match = String(currentReview[field] || '').match(/[\d.]+/);
      if (match) {
        const score = parseFloat(match[0]);
        const oldAvg = avgScores[field] || 0;
        const oldWeightSum = validCounts[field] || 0; // 此时 validCounts 存储的本质是“历史总权重”
        
        // 🟢 核心修改：加权平均分公式：(旧均分 * 旧总权重 + 新分 * 新权重) / (旧总权重 + 新权重)
        avgScores[field] = ((oldAvg * oldWeightSum) + (score * submitterWeight)) / (oldWeightSum + submitterWeight);
        // 累加总权重
        validCounts[field] = oldWeightSum + submitterWeight;
      }
    });

    // 3. 计算最新的全维度综合平均分
    let totalValidAvg = 0, validFieldCount = 0;
    scoreFields.forEach(field => {
      if (avgScores[field] > 0) {
        totalValidAvg += avgScores[field];
        validFieldCount++;
      }
    });
    const currentOverallAvg = validFieldCount > 0 ? (totalValidAvg / validFieldCount) : 0;

    // 4. 将最新各项均分存入 Firebase 持久化 (综合分精度提升至 2 位小数以适配评级系统)
    // 🟢 新增：计算本次单条车评的均分，判断是否属于"好评"（均分≥9.0），用于触发频道升级
    let thisRevTotal = 0, thisRevCount = 0;
    scoreFields.forEach(f => {
      const m = String(currentReview[f] || '').match(/[\d.]+/);
      if (m) { thisRevTotal += parseFloat(m[0]); thisRevCount++; }
    });
    const thisReviewAvg = thisRevCount > 0 ? thisRevTotal / thisRevCount : 0;
    const isGoodReview = thisReviewAvg >= 8.00;
    // 🟢 新增：累积好评计数（好评=单次车评均分≥8.0）
    const prevGoodCount = teacherData.good_review_count || 0;
    const newGoodCount = prevGoodCount + (isGoodReview ? 1 : 0);

    updateFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`, {
      total_reviews: currentTotalReviews,
      avg_scores: avgScores,
      valid_counts: validCounts,
      average_score: currentOverallAvg.toFixed(2),
      // 🟢 新增字段：好评计数 + 最新车评时间戳（供升降级逻辑使用）
      good_review_count: newGoodCount,
      tier_last_review_at: new Date().getTime()
    });

    // 🟢 新增：车评通过后，检查是否满足频道升级条件（仅在配置了分级频道时生效）
    if (TIER_CHANNELS.A) {
      checkAndUpgradeTeacherTier(teacherName, newGoodCount);
    }

    // 🟢 新增：计算综合评级标签（好评/中评/差评）
    let ratingLabel = "";
    if (currentOverallAvg > 0) {
      if (currentOverallAvg >= 8.00) {
        ratingLabel = " 🌟<b>[好评]</b>";
      } else if (currentOverallAvg >= 5.00) {
        ratingLabel = " ⭐<b>[中评]</b>";
      } else {
        ratingLabel = " 💔<b>[差评]</b>";
      }
    }

    // 5. 组装全维度评分面板 (加入评级标签，并展示 2 位小数的精准评分)
    const rawScorePanel = `📊 <b>【全维度评分】: ${currentOverallAvg > 0 ? currentOverallAvg.toFixed(2) + ' 分' : '暂无'}</b>${ratingLabel} (基于 ${currentTotalReviews} 份真实车评)\n` +
                          `📷人照: ${avgScores.photos > 0 ? avgScores.photos.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                          `✨颜值: ${avgScores.looks > 0 ? avgScores.looks.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                          `💃身材: ${avgScores.body > 0 ? avgScores.body.toFixed(1) : '-'}\n` +
                          `💋服务: ${avgScores.service > 0 ? avgScores.service.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                          `💓态度: ${avgScores.attitude > 0 ? avgScores.attitude.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                          `🏡环境: ${avgScores.env > 0 ? avgScores.env.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                          `💰性价比: ${avgScores.cp > 0 ? avgScores.cp.toFixed(1) : '-'}\n` +
                          `👤 最新留名: ${escapeHtml(currentReview.reviewer) || '暂无'}\n` +
                          `-------------------\n`;
// 🟢 新增：专供频道资料卡展示的面板，在评分下方加入最新的新车评过程描述
    const profileScorePanel = `📊 <b>【全维度评分】: ${currentOverallAvg > 0 ? currentOverallAvg.toFixed(2) + ' 分' : '暂无'}</b>${ratingLabel} (基于 ${currentTotalReviews} 份真实车评)\n` +
                              `📷人照: ${avgScores.photos > 0 ? avgScores.photos.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `✨颜值: ${avgScores.looks > 0 ? avgScores.looks.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `💃身材: ${avgScores.body > 0 ? avgScores.body.toFixed(1) : '-'}\n` +
                              `💋服务: ${avgScores.service > 0 ? avgScores.service.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `💓态度: ${avgScores.attitude > 0 ? avgScores.attitude.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `🏡环境: ${avgScores.env > 0 ? avgScores.env.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `💰性价比: ${avgScores.cp > 0 ? avgScores.cp.toFixed(1) : '-'}\n` +
                              `👤 最新留名: ${escapeHtml(currentReview.reviewer) || '暂无'}\n` +
                              `💬 最新过程描述: ${currentReview.process && currentReview.process.length > 500 ? '\n<blockquote expandable>' + escapeHtml(currentReview.process) + '</blockquote>' : escapeHtml(currentReview.process) || '暂无'}\n` +
                              `-------------------\n`;

    // 6. 强制刷新频道资料卡：如果数据库中存有该老师的原帖 ID 和原文案，则静默更新原文案
    // 🟢 核心修复：重新拉取最新的 teacherData，防止刚才触发了A级频道升级导致旧的 channel_id 和 message_id 失效
    const updatedTeacherData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`) || teacherData;
    
    if (updatedTeacherData.message_id && updatedTeacherData.original_text) {
      let safeOriginalText = updatedTeacherData.original_text.replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
// 🟢 核心修复：终极防御！在重新排版前，强行抹除文本中任何残留的旧评分面板，确保每次都是“新出炉的直接覆盖掉旧的”
      safeOriginalText = safeOriginalText.replace(/📊[\s\S]*?【全维度评分】[\s\S]*?-------------------\n?(?:🏷️.*?分类标签:[^\n]*\n?)?/g, '').trim();
// 🟢 布局优化：将全维度评分动态插入到资料卡【老师名字】行的正下方
      let profileLines = safeOriginalText.split('\n');
// 🟢 优先寻找“编号”行，如果没有再寻找名字行
      let pIdx = profileLines.findIndex(line => line.includes('编号'));
      if (pIdx === -1) pIdx = profileLines.findIndex(line => line.includes(teacherName) || line.includes('名字') || line.includes('姓名') || line.includes('老师'));
      
      if (pIdx !== -1) {
        // 寻找到编号或名字行，在其下一行插入资料卡专属评分面板（前后加换行保持呼吸感）
        profileLines.splice(pIdx + 1, 0, '\n' + profileScorePanel.trim() + '\n');
} else {
        // 没找到则默认置顶
        profileLines.unshift(profileScorePanel.trim());
}
      let newProfileText = profileLines.join('\n');

      const editPayload = { chat_id: updatedTeacherData.channel_id ||
PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID, message_id: updatedTeacherData.message_id, parse_mode: "HTML" };
      if (updatedTeacherData.has_media) {
         editPayload.caption = newProfileText;
sendTelegramMessage("editMessageCaption", editPayload);
      } else {
         editPayload.text = newProfileText;
         sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", editPayload);
}
    }

    // 7. 🟢 布局优化：当前发布的单份新车评，也将全维度评分移到车评内【编号】或【老师名字】行的正下方
    let reviewLines = safeText.split('\n');
    let rIdx = reviewLines.findIndex(line => line.includes('编号'));
    if (rIdx === -1) rIdx = reviewLines.findIndex(line => line.includes(teacherName) || line.includes('名字') || line.includes('姓名') || line.includes('老师'));
    
    if (rIdx !== -1) {
      reviewLines.splice(rIdx + 1, 0, '\n' + rawScorePanel.trim() + '\n');
    } else {
      reviewLines.unshift(rawScorePanel.trim());
    }
    
    // 🟢 新增：获取当前时间并格式化为东八区时间
    const publishTime = new Date().toLocaleString('zh-CN', {timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai'});
    const publishText = `📢 <b>【新车评发布】</b>\n⏱ <b>发布时间:</b> ${publishTime}\n\n` + reviewLines.join('\n');
    
    // 更新 Firebase 数据状态为通过
    writeToFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}/status`, 'approved');
    
    // 🟢 架构优化：推入增量滚动计算缓冲区队列 (避免审核时长计算导致的 Bot 假死)
    writeToFirebase(`queue/pending_reviews/${reviewId}`, {
      teacherId: teacherName,
      score: currentOverallAvg,
      weight_snapshot: currentReview.weight_snapshot || currentReview.weight || 1.0,
      weighted_score: currentReview.weighted_score || (currentOverallAvg * (currentReview.weight || 1.0))
    });

    // 🟢 发车评双重激励：奖励经验 + 积分
    const rewardProfile = getUserProfileWithLazyCalc(submitterId);
    const rewardCurrentPoints = rewardProfile.points;
    const currentExp = rewardProfile.experience || 0;
    const currentHold = rewardProfile.points_hold || 0;
    
    let newPts = rewardCurrentPoints + REVIEW_REWARD_POINTS;
    let newHold = currentHold;
    if (newPts > MAX_USABLE_POINTS) {
       newHold += (newPts - MAX_USABLE_POINTS);
       newPts = MAX_USABLE_POINTS;
    }
    
    updateFirebase(`user_profiles/${submitterId}`, { 
      points: newPts, 
      points_hold: newHold,
      experience: currentExp + EXP_REWARD_REVIEW 
    });
    
    // 记录收入流水
    writeToFirebase(`points_transactions/tx_${Date.now()}_${submitterId}`, {
        userId: String(submitterId), amount: REVIEW_REWARD_POINTS, type: "review_approved",
        balance_before: rewardCurrentPoints, balance_after: newPts, timestamp: Date.now(), ref_id: reviewId
    });

    const newAdminText = callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n✅ 处理结果：已通过 (操作人: ${callbackQuery.from.first_name})`;
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, newAdminText, null);
    sendMessage(submitterId, `✅ 您的车评已通过审核并发布！\n💰 奖励 ${REVIEW_REWARD_POINTS} 积分已发放，当前积分：${rewardCurrentPoints + REVIEW_REWARD_POINTS}\n\n${safeText}`);

    // 🟢 新增：将审核通过的车评同步推送给相关老师本人
    if (teacherData && teacherData.tg_id) {
      const teacherNotifyText = `🔔 <b>您的新车评已发布！</b>\n\n有用户为您提交了新的车评，现已通过管理员审核：\n\n${publishText}`;
      
      // 🟢 核心修改：为老师的消息推送额外构建一个包含“老师回复”的专属内联键盘
      const teacherNotifyKeyboard = {
        inline_keyboard: [
          [{ text: `💬 老师回复`, url: `${BOT_USERNAME_URL}?start=reply_${reviewId}` }]
        ]
      };
      
      // 将原先的 null 替换为新构建的 teacherNotifyKeyboard，挂载按钮
      const teacherSendRes = sendMessage(teacherData.tg_id, teacherNotifyText, teacherNotifyKeyboard, "HTML");
      
      // 检查发送是否成功，若失败则反馈给审核此车评的管理员
      try {
        if (teacherSendRes) {
          const tResObj = JSON.parse(teacherSendRes.getContentText());
          if (!tResObj.ok) {
             sendMessage(chatId, `⚠️ <b>向老师推送车评失败</b>\n老师：${teacherName}\n原因：<code>${tResObj.description}</code>\n💡 可能原因：老师从未与机器人发起过私聊 (未发送过 /start)，导致机器人无权限主动向其发消息。`);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
         Logger.log("推送车评给老师时发生异常: " + e);
      }
    } else {
      // 获取不到老师 TG_ID 时的明确提示
      sendMessage(chatId, `⚠️ <b>向老师推送车评失败</b>\n原因：系统未能获取到老师【${teacherName}】的真实 TG_ID。\n💡 建议：请让该老师使用一次「⏰老师打卡」功能，或在机器人的「/settings 控制面板」中为其手动绑定 ID。`);
    }

    // 🟢 发布到关联群组（形成频道帖子的评论区），并附带媒体文件
    // 🟢 核心修复 2：支持 A/B/C 三级频道的评论区精准推送
    if (PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID || TIER_CHANNELS.A || TIER_CHANNELS.B || TIER_CHANNELS.C) {
      const reviewData = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`);
      let targetChatId = PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID;
      let replyToMessageId = null;
      let teacherName = "未知";
      let teacherTgId = null; // 🟢 新增：用于存储从打卡系统获取的老师真实的 TG_ID

      if (reviewData && reviewData.teacher) {
        teacherName = reviewData.teacher;
        // 🟢 赋值老师名字
        let teacherIndex = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${reviewData.teacher}`);
        
        // 🟢 核心修复：当第3篇车评触发频道升级时，旧的评论区引用会在升级搬迁时被清空。
        // 此时 Telegram 正在后台将新资料帖转发到新频道的评论区群组，我们需要主动等待几秒，
        // 让监听群组转发的 Webhook 有时间抓取到新频道的群组 ID，防止这篇引发升级的车评被错误地直发到频道本体里造成“分离”。
        if (teacherIndex && !teacherIndex.group_id && teacherIndex.channel_id) {
          for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { // 最多等待 6 秒 (4次 * 1.5秒)
            Utilities.sleep(1500); 
            teacherIndex = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${reviewData.teacher}`);
            if (teacherIndex.group_id) break; // 一旦抓到新群组 ID，立即跳出等待
          }
        }

        if (teacherIndex) {
          // 🟢 优先读取群组的 ID 和群组转发消息的 ID，以实现在评论区群内引用回复 (适用于所有已关联群组的频道)
          if (teacherIndex.group_id && teacherIndex.group_message_id) {
            targetChatId = teacherIndex.group_id;
            replyToMessageId = teacherIndex.group_message_id;
          } else {
            // 🟢 修复：通过 getChat 自动查找频道的关联留言群组(linked_chat_id)
            // 找到留言群组 → 发到群里，确保车评出现在留言区
            // 未找到留言群组 → 兜底发到频道本身（兼容无留言功能的频道）
            const _fallbackChId = teacherIndex.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID;
            try {
              const _gcRes = sendTelegramMessage("getChat", { chat_id: _fallbackChId });
              const _gcData = JSON.parse(_gcRes.getContentText());
              if (_gcData.ok && _gcData.result && _gcData.result.linked_chat_id) {
                // ✅ 频道已开留言，直接发到关联群组，显示在留言区
                const _linkedChatId = _gcData.result.linked_chat_id;
                // 🟢 修复：立即将 linked_chat_id 写入 Firebase，供后续补发复用，避免每次都重新 getChat
                if (!teacherIndex.group_id) {
                  writeToFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}/group_id`, _linkedChatId);
                  // 🟢 修复：在已知留言群组 ID 的基础上，再额外等一轮（最多 4.5 秒），
                  // 给 Webhook 更多时间捕获 group_message_id，避免车评在留言区"飞单"
                  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
                    Utilities.sleep(1500);
                    teacherIndex = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`);
                    if (teacherIndex.group_message_id) break;
                  }
                }
                targetChatId = _linkedChatId;
                replyToMessageId = teacherIndex.group_message_id || null; // 🟢 修复：捕获到则精准引用，否则才作为顶级留言
              } else {
                // ❌ 频道未开留言区，保持原有逻辑，直接在频道内发布
                targetChatId = _fallbackChId;
                replyToMessageId = teacherIndex.message_id;
              }
            } catch (_gcErr) {
              targetChatId = _fallbackChId;
              replyToMessageId = teacherIndex.message_id;
            }
          }
          // 🟢 提取通过打卡系统抓取的老师真实数据
          if (teacherIndex.username) {
            // 如果老师有设置用户名，优先使用标准的 t.me 链接，多端兼容性最好
            teacherTgId = `https://t.me/${teacherIndex.username}`;
          } else if (teacherIndex.tg_id) {
            // 如果没有用户名，退回使用 TG 纯数字 ID 协议
            teacherTgId = `tg://user?id=${teacherIndex.tg_id}`;
          }
        }
      }

      // 🟢 新增：动态生成底部三个按钮
      // ⚠️ 注意：机器人链接必须是带有字母的真实 @用户名，绝对不能是纯数字 Token！
      const botUrl = BOT_USERNAME_URL; // 🟢 改为引用顶部全局变量，避免两处链接写得不一致
      
      // 🟢 如果打卡系统成功抓取到链接，直接使用；否则退回默认机器人联络
      const contactUrl = teacherTgId ? teacherTgId : `${botUrl}?start=contact`;

      const publishKeyboard = {
        inline_keyboard: [
          [{ text: `直连${teacherName}`, url: contactUrl }],
          [{ text: "⭐️ 收藏老师", callback_data: `FAV_${encodeTeacherForDeepLink(teacherName)}` }, { text: "💖 关注动态", callback_data: `FL_${encodeTeacherForDeepLink(teacherName)}` }],
          // 🟢 核心修复：改为 url 深度链接跳转
          [{ text: `💬 老师回复`, url: `${botUrl}?start=reply_${reviewId}` }]
        ]
      };

      let publishSendResult = null; // 🟢 核心修复：接住发送结果，用于拿到这条消息在群里的真实 ID

      if (reviewData && reviewData.media) {
        // 🟢 将 publishKeyboard 挂载到 reply_markup 发送
        const payload = { chat_id: targetChatId, caption: publishText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: publishKeyboard };
        if (replyToMessageId) {
          payload.reply_to_message_id = replyToMessageId;
          payload.allow_sending_without_reply = true; // 🟢 防止群内原贴由于意外丢失，导致车评直接发送失败
        }

        if (reviewData.media_type === 'photo') { 
          payload.photo = reviewData.media;
          publishSendResult = sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload); 
        } else if (reviewData.media_type === 'video') { 
          payload.video = reviewData.media;
          publishSendResult = sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload); 
        }
      } else {
        // 🟢 将 publishKeyboard 挂载到 reply_markup 发送
        const payload = { chat_id: targetChatId, text: publishText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: publishKeyboard };
        if (replyToMessageId) {
          payload.reply_to_message_id = replyToMessageId;
          payload.allow_sending_without_reply = true; // 🟢 防止群内原贴丢失导致车评发送失败
        }
        
        publishSendResult = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
      }

      // 🟢 核心修复：把这条车评在评论区实际发出的 chat_id / message_id 写回数据库
      // 这样删除车评时才能精准定位、同步删除评论区里的这条消息，避免“数据库删了但评论区残留”
      try {
        if (publishSendResult) {
          const publishResultData = JSON.parse(publishSendResult.getContentText());
          if (publishResultData.ok && publishResultData.result) {
            updateFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`, {
              group_chat_id: String(publishResultData.result.chat.id),
              group_msg_id: publishResultData.result.message_id
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        Logger.log("⚠️ 记录评论区消息 ID 失败: " + err);
      }
      // 🟢 修复1：移入 if(PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID) 块内，确保 targetChatId/contactUrl/botUrl/reviewData 在作用域内可访问
      // 🟢 修复2：新增媒体类型判断，有图/视频时改用 sendPhoto/sendVideo，不再只发纯文本
      if (SCORE_PUSH_CHANNEL && String(targetChatId) !== String(SCORE_PUSH_CHANNEL)) {
        try {
          const pushKeyboard = {
            inline_keyboard: [
              [{ text: `直连${teacherName}`, url: contactUrl }],
              [{ text: "⭐️ 收藏老师", callback_data: `FAV_${encodeTeacherForDeepLink(teacherName)}` }, { text: "💖 关注动态", callback_data: `FL_${encodeTeacherForDeepLink(teacherName)}` }],
              [{ text: `💬 老师回复`, url: `${botUrl}?start=reply_${reviewId}` }, { text: `🤖给${teacherName}写车评`, url: `${botUrl}?start=write_review_${encodeTeacherForDeepLink(teacherName)}` }]
            ]
          };
          if (reviewData && reviewData.media) {
            const pushPayload = { chat_id: SCORE_PUSH_CHANNEL, caption: publishText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: pushKeyboard };
            if (reviewData.media_type === 'photo') {
              pushPayload.photo = reviewData.media;
              sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", pushPayload);
            } else if (reviewData.media_type === 'video') {
              pushPayload.video = reviewData.media;
              sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", pushPayload);
            }
          } else {
            sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", {
              chat_id: SCORE_PUSH_CHANNEL,
              text: publishText,
              parse_mode: "HTML",
              reply_markup: pushKeyboard
            });
          }
        } catch (pushErr) {
          Logger.log("⚠️ 评分直推频道发送失败: " + pushErr);
        }
      }
    }

    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已批准");
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：管理员点击回复投诉建议按钮（防止多管理员重复回复同一条）
  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_REPLY_FB_")) {
    const feedbackId = data.replace("ADMIN_REPLY_FB_", "");
    const fbData = readFromFirebase(`feedback_queue/${feedbackId}`);

    // 兼容旧格式（feedbackId 直接就是纯数字 userId 的情况）
    const targetUserId = (fbData && fbData.userId) ? fbData.userId : feedbackId;

    // 🟢 防重复核心：检查是否已被其他管理员认领或已回复完毕
    if (fbData && fbData.handled) {
      const handlerName = fbData.handlerName || '其他管理员';
      const statusTip = fbData.replied ? `✅ ${handlerName} 已完成回复` : `🔒 ${handlerName} 正在处理中`;
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `⚠️ ${statusTip}，请勿重复操作！`, true);
      // 同步更新当前管理员的消息面板
      const newMsgText = (callbackQuery.message.text || '') + `\n\n⚠️ ${statusTip}，无需再次回复。`;
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, newMsgText, null);
      return;
    }

    // 🟢 原子认领：立即写入认领标记（防并发冲突）
    const adminName = callbackQuery.from.first_name || `管理员${userId}`;
    updateFirebase(`feedback_queue/${feedbackId}`, {
      handled: true,
      replied: false,
      handlerName: adminName,
      handlerId: String(userId)
    });

    // 更新当前管理员消息面板，提示正在处理
    const processingText = (callbackQuery.message.text || '') + `\n\n🔒 ${adminName} 正在回复中...`;
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, processingText, null);

    // 设置状态，等待输入回复内容（状态中携带 feedbackId 供事后更新）
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, `WAITING_FOR_FBREPLY_${targetUserId}_${feedbackId}`);

    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消操作", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] };
    sendMessage(chatId, `📝 请在下方输入您要回复给用户 <code>${targetUserId}</code> 的内容：\n(支持纯文本，发送后将自动通过机器人私聊推送给该用户)`, cancelKb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_REJECT_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const submitterId = parts[2];
    const reviewId = parts[3];
// 检查当前车评状态，防止多个管理员重复操作
    const currentReview = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`);
    if (!currentReview || currentReview.status !== 'pending') {
      const statusText = currentReview && currentReview.status === 'approved' ? '已通过' : '已拒绝/已失效';
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n⚠️ 处理结果：已被其他管理员处理 (${statusText})`, null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "该车评已被处理，无法重复操作！");
      return;
    }

    // 🟢 核心修复：不立即拒绝，进入"填写理由"状态机，与删除申请拒绝逻辑保持一致
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_REJ_REASON_' + reviewId + '_' + submitterId);

    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消操作", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] };
    sendMessage(chatId, `📝 请在下方输入<b>拒绝审核</b>订单 <code>${reviewId}</code> 的理由：\n(输入后将自动发送并反馈给该用户)`, cancelKb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：管理员审核老师对车评的回复
  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_APVREPLY_")) {
    const reviewId = data.replace("ADMIN_APVREPLY_", "");
    const revData = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`);

    if (!revData || !revData.teacher_reply || revData.teacher_reply.status !== 'pending') {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n⚠️ 处理结果：该回复已被处理或数据不存在`, null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "无法重复操作");
      return;
    }

    // 更新状态为已通过
    writeToFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}/teacher_reply/status`, 'approved');
    const replyText = revData.teacher_reply.text;

    // 1. 核心闭环：私聊通知原作者，告诉他老师回复了他
    if (revData.userId) {
      sendMessage(revData.userId, `🔔 <b>您有新的老师回复！</b>\n\n您提交的车评（订单: <code>${reviewId}</code>），老师 <b>${revData.teacher}</b> 给您作出了回应：\n\n💬 <code>${escapeHtml(replyText)}</code>`, null, "HTML");
    }

    // 2. 更新面板状态
    const newAdminText = callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n✅ 处理结果：回复已批准发布并通知作者 (操作人: ${callbackQuery.from.first_name})`;
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, newAdminText, null);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已批准回复");
    return;
  }

  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_REJREPLY_")) {
    const reviewId = data.replace("ADMIN_REJREPLY_", "");
    const revData = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`);

    if (!revData || !revData.teacher_reply || revData.teacher_reply.status !== 'pending') {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n⚠️ 处理结果：该回复已被处理或数据不存在`, null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "无法重复操作");
      return;
    }

    // 更新状态为拒绝
    writeToFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}/teacher_reply/status`, 'rejected');
    const newAdminText = callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n❌ 处理结果：回复已拒绝 (操作人: ${callbackQuery.from.first_name})`;
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, newAdminText, null);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已拒绝回复");
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：管理员批准车评删除申请
  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_APVDEL_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const submitterId = parts[2];
    const reviewId = parts[3];
    
    const rev = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`);
    if (!rev || !rev.delete_pending) {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n⚠️ 处理结果：数据不存在或已被处理`, null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "操作无效");
      return;
    }

    // 审核通过，执行彻底删除逻辑
    if (rev.group_chat_id && rev.group_msg_id) {
      try { 
        // 🛠️ 修复：强制将 message_id 转为 Number，防止 JSON 导入时类型变异导致 TG API 报错拦截
        sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: String(rev.group_chat_id), message_id: Number(rev.group_msg_id) });
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    refundAnonPoints(rev);
    deleteFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`);
    
    if (rev.status === 'approved' && rev.teacher) {
      recalculateAndSyncTeacherScore(rev.teacher);
    }

    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n✅ 处理结果：已批准删除并彻底销毁数据 (操作人: ${callbackQuery.from.first_name})`, null);
    sendMessage(submitterId, `✅ <b>车评删除通知</b>\n\n您提交的车评（订单: <code>${reviewId}</code>）删除申请已通过，相关数据已彻底销毁！`, null, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已批准删除");
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：管理员拒绝车评删除申请，拦截并进入填写理由状态机
  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_REJDEL_")) {
    const parts = data.split("_");
    const submitterId = parts[2];
    const reviewId = parts[3];

    const rev = readFromFirebase(`reviews/${reviewId}`);
    if (!rev || !rev.delete_pending) {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n⚠️ 处理结果：数据不存在或已被处理`, null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "操作无效");
      return;
    }

    // 记录管理员所处的步骤，等待文本输入
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_DEL_REASON_' + reviewId + '_' + submitterId);
    
    const cancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消操作", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] };
    sendMessage(chatId, `📝 请在下方输入<b>拒绝删除</b>订单 <code>${reviewId}</code> 的理由：\n(输入后将自动发送并反馈给该用户)`, cancelKb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 上榜向导：跳过剩余照片（至少1张），进入课表上传步骤
  if (data === "RANK_PHOTOS_DONE") {
    const rkDrPD = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
    if (!rkDrPD) { answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 数据已丢失，请重新开始。", true); return; }
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_RANKING_SCHEDULE');
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, parse_mode: "HTML",
      text: "✅ 照片已跳过！\n\n📅 <b>请发送您的【课表图片】（1张）：</b>\n\n若无课表可点击下方跳过。",
      reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [
        [{ text: "⏭️ 跳过课表，进入下一步", callback_data: "RANK_SKIP_SCHEDULE" }],
        [{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]
      ]}
    });
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 上榜向导：跳过课表上传，直接进入标签选择步骤
  if (data === "RANK_SKIP_SCHEDULE") {
    const rkDrSS = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
    if (!rkDrSS) { answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 数据已丢失，请重新开始。", true); return; }
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, 'WAITING_FOR_RANKING_TAGS');
    sendRankingTagsPanel(chatId, userId, messageId);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 上榜向导：点击示例标签按钮，切换选中/取消状态并刷新标签面板
  if (data.startsWith("RANK_TAG_SEL_")) {
    const parts = data.replace("RANK_TAG_SEL_", "").split("_");
    const tagIdx = parseInt(parts[0]);
    const page = parseInt(parts[1]) || 0; // 新增接收页码参数
    const availTags = getCategoryTagList();
    if (isNaN(tagIdx) || tagIdx >= availTags.length) {
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 标签解析失败，请重试。", true);
      return;
    }
    const selTag = availTags[tagIdx];
    const rkDrTS = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
    if (!rkDrTS) { answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 数据已丢失，请重新开始。", true); return; }
    let rkDTS = JSON.parse(rkDrTS);
    if (!rkDTS.tags) rkDTS.tags = [];
    const pos = rkDTS.tags.indexOf(selTag);
    if (pos !== -1) {
      rkDTS.tags.splice(pos, 1);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `已取消：${selTag}`);
    } else {
      rkDTS.tags.push(selTag);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, `✅ 已选择：${selTag}`);
    }
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId, JSON.stringify(rkDTS));
    sendRankingTagsPanel(chatId, userId, messageId, page); // 原位带页码刷新标签面板
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 上榜向导：标签选择面板翻页
  if (data.startsWith("RANK_TAG_PAGE_")) {
    const page = parseInt(data.replace("RANK_TAG_PAGE_", ""));
    sendRankingTagsPanel(chatId, userId, messageId, page);
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 上榜向导：点击"确认提交申请"按钮
  if (data === "RANK_TAG_SUBMIT") {
    const rkDrSub = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
    if (!rkDrSub) { answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "⚠️ 数据已丢失，请重新开始。", true); return; }
    // 移除键盘防止重复点击
    try { sendTelegramMessage("editMessageReplyMarkup", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [] } }); } catch(e) {}
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "✅ 正在提交...");
    submitRankingApplication(chatId, userId, callbackQuery.from);
    return;
  }
  // 🟢 新增：管理员审核上榜申请——通过
  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_APV_RANKING_")) {
    const apvParts = data.replace("ADMIN_APV_RANKING_", "").split("_");
    const apvSubmissionId = apvParts[0]; // rnk1750000000000
    const apvSubmitterId  = apvParts[1]; // Telegram user ID
    const apvSub = readFromFirebase(`ranking_submissions/${apvSubmissionId}`);
    if (!apvSub || apvSub.status !== 'pending') {
      const apvStatusText = apvSub && apvSub.status === 'approved' ? '已通过' : '已拒绝/已失效';
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n⚠️ 处理结果：已被其他管理员处理 (${apvStatusText})`, null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "该申请已被处理，无法重复操作！");
      return;
    }
    writeToFirebase(`ranking_submissions/${apvSubmissionId}/status`, 'approved');
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n✅ 处理结果：上榜申请已审核通过 (操作人: ${callbackQuery.from.first_name})`, null);
    sendMessage(apvSubmitterId, `🎉 <b>恭喜！您的上榜申请已审核通过！</b>\n\n您提交的上榜申请（编号：<code>${apvSubmissionId}</code>）已获管理员批准，您即将出现在榜单中，感谢您的支持！`, null, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "已批准该申请");
    return;
  }

  // 🟢 新增：管理员审核上榜申请——拒绝（进入填写理由状态机）
  if (data.startsWith("ADMIN_REJ_RANKING_")) {
    const rejParts = data.replace("ADMIN_REJ_RANKING_", "").split("_");
    const rejSubmissionId = rejParts[0]; // rnk1750000000000
    const rejSubmitterId  = rejParts[1]; // Telegram user ID
    const rejSub = readFromFirebase(`ranking_submissions/${rejSubmissionId}`);
    if (!rejSub || rejSub.status !== 'pending') {
      const rejStatusText = rejSub && rejSub.status === 'approved' ? '已通过' : '已拒绝/已失效';
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, callbackQuery.message.text + `\n\n⚠️ 处理结果：已被其他管理员处理 (${rejStatusText})`, null);
      answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, "该申请已被处理，无法重复操作！");
      return;
    }
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('state_' + userId, `WAITING_FOR_RANKING_REJ_REASON_${rejSubmissionId}_${rejSubmitterId}`);
    const rejCancelKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "🔙 取消操作", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] };
    sendMessage(chatId, `📝 请在下方输入<b>拒绝</b>上榜申请 <code>${rejSubmissionId}</code> 的理由：\n(输入后将自动发送给该用户)`, rejCancelKb, "HTML");
    answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId);
    return;
  }
}

// ================= 辅助函数 =================

// 生成并发送包含所有字段及键盘的面板 (参考 photo_2026-06-17_16-46-00.jpg)
// ================= 辅助函数 =================

// 🟢 新增：动态生成按周期（今日/本周/本月/总）排名的车评报告榜
function sendReportBoard(chatId, messageId, period, page, replyToMsgId) {
  const allReviews = readFromFirebase('reviews') || {};
  const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};

  const now = new Date();
  // 统一使用北京时间 (UTC+8) 进行周期边界精准计算
  const utc8Now = new Date(now.getTime() + 8 * 3600000);
  let startTime = 0;

  if (period === 'today') {
    const startOfToday = new Date(utc8Now);
    startOfToday.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfToday.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  } else if (period === 'week') {
    const startOfWeek = new Date(utc8Now);
    const day = startOfWeek.getUTCDay() || 7; // 周日作为一周的第7天
    startOfWeek.setUTCDate(startOfWeek.getUTCDate() - day + 1);
    startOfWeek.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfWeek.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  } else if (period === 'month') {
    const startOfMonth = new Date(utc8Now);
    startOfMonth.setUTCDate(1);
    startOfMonth.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfMonth.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  } // period === 'all' 保持 startTime = 0，统计历史全部

  let teacherStats = {};
  // 遍历历史订单库，统计指定时间范围内的有效新增车评数
  for (let key in allReviews) {
    const rev = allReviews[key];
    if (rev.status === 'approved' && rev.timestamp >= startTime) {
      // 🟢 核心修复1：增加 .trim() 容错，防止用户提交时带有首尾空格导致匹配失败
      const tName = (rev.teacher || '').trim();
      if (!teacherStats[tName]) teacherStats[tName] = 0;
      teacherStats[tName]++;
    }
  }

  // 将统计结果与老师资料库合并，剔除已下架（无 message_id）的老师
  let rbList = [];
  // 🟢 核心修复2：废除总榜依赖缓存字段的逻辑，所有周期统一使用动态累加的 teacherStats
  // 彻底解决缓存数与真实订单脱节导致“漏查”的问题！
  for (let name in teacherStats) {
    const tData = allTeachers[name];
    if (tData && tData.message_id) {
      rbList.push({
        name: name,
        period_reviews: teacherStats[name],
        average_score: tData.average_score || "0.00",
        message_id: tData.message_id,
        channel_id: tData.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID
      });
    }
  }

  // 按周期内的有效车评数从大到小排序
  rbList.sort((a, b) => b.period_reviews - a.period_reviews);
  
  page = parseInt(page) || 0;
  const pageSize = 10; // 每页显示数量，可按需修改
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(rbList.length / pageSize) || 1;
  const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 0), totalPages - 1);
  const startIndex = safePage * pageSize;
  const rbTop = rbList.slice(startIndex, startIndex + pageSize);

  const periodNames = { 'today': '今日', 'week': '本周', 'month': '本月', 'all': '总榜' };
  const pName = periodNames[period];
  let rbText = `📊 <b>车评报告榜</b> (第 ${safePage + 1}/${totalPages} 页)\n`;
  if (period === 'all') {
    rbText += `按累计历史车评数量排名\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
  } else {
    rbText += `按【${pName}】新增车评数量排名\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
  }

  if (rbList.length === 0) {
    rbText += `📊 暂无${pName}的车评记录，榜单空空如也～\n`;
  }

  let rbKeyboard = [];
  rbTop.forEach((t, i) => {
    const globalIndex = startIndex + i;
    const medal = globalIndex === 0 ? '🥇' : globalIndex === 1 ? '🥈' : globalIndex === 2 ? '🥉' : `${globalIndex + 1}.`;
    const actionStr = period === 'all' ? '总计' : '新增';
    rbText += `${medal} ${escapeHtml(t.name)} — 📋${actionStr}${t.period_reviews}份 ⭐${t.average_score}分\n`;
    
    // 动态解析真实频道 ID 跳转
    const targetChId = String(t.channel_id).replace(/^-100/, '');
    rbKeyboard.push([{ text: `${medal} ${t.name}（${t.period_reviews}份）`, url: `https://t.me/c/${targetChId}/${t.message_id}` }]);
  });
  
  // 增加动态翻页按钮
  let navRow = [];
  if (safePage > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `RB_PAGE_${period}_${safePage - 1}` });
  if (safePage < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `RB_PAGE_${period}_${safePage + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) rbKeyboard.push(navRow);

  // 增加底部原位无缝切换的四个周期按钮
  rbKeyboard.push([
    { text: period === 'today' ? '✅ 今日' : '今日', callback_data: 'RB_PERIOD_today' },
    { text: period === 'week' ? '✅ 本周' : '本周', callback_data: 'RB_PERIOD_week' },
    { text: period === 'month' ? '✅ 本月' : '本月', callback_data: 'RB_PERIOD_month' },
    { text: period === 'all' ? '✅ 总榜' : '总榜', callback_data: 'RB_PERIOD_all' }
  ]);
  rbKeyboard.push([{ text: "✖️ 关闭榜单", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]);

  if (messageId) {
    // 触发自 Inline 按钮，直接实现高级版底层面板替换（防刷屏体验）
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId,
      message_id: messageId,
      text: rbText,
      parse_mode: "HTML",
      reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: rbKeyboard }
    });
  } else {
    // 用户发送主菜单指令触发的首次拉起
    sendMessage(chatId, rbText, { inline_keyboard: rbKeyboard }, "HTML", replyToMsgId);
  }
}
// 🟢 新增：动态生成按周期（今日/本周/本月/总）排名的综合评分公开榜
function sendPublicBoard(chatId, messageId, period, page, replyToMsgId) {
  const allReviews = readFromFirebase('reviews') || {};
  const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};

  const now = new Date();
  const utc8Now = new Date(now.getTime() + 8 * 3600000);
  let startTime = 0;
  if (period === 'today') {
    const startOfToday = new Date(utc8Now);
    startOfToday.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfToday.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  } else if (period === 'week') {
    const startOfWeek = new Date(utc8Now);
    const day = startOfWeek.getUTCDay() || 7;
    startOfWeek.setUTCDate(startOfWeek.getUTCDate() - day + 1);
    startOfWeek.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfWeek.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  } else if (period === 'month') {
    const startOfMonth = new Date(utc8Now);
    startOfMonth.setUTCDate(1);
    startOfMonth.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfMonth.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  }

  let pbList = [];
  // 🟢 同步修复公开榜：废弃依赖缓存均分的逻辑，利用绝对真实的底层记录全量计算保障 100% 精确度
  let periodStats = {};
  for (let key in allReviews) {
    const rev = allReviews[key];
    if (rev.status === 'approved' && rev.timestamp >= startTime) {
      const tName = (rev.teacher || '').trim(); // 🟢 容错清空空格
      if (!periodStats[tName]) periodStats[tName] = { totalScore: 0, reviewCount: 0 };
      
      let rTotal = 0, rCount = 0;
      ['photos', 'looks', 'body', 'service', 'attitude', 'env', 'cp'].forEach(f => {
        const m = String(rev[f] || '').match(/[\d.]+/);
        if (m) { rTotal += parseFloat(m[0]); rCount++; }
      });
      if (rCount > 0) {
        periodStats[tName].totalScore += (rTotal / rCount);
        periodStats[tName].reviewCount++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // 合并有效成绩入榜
  for (let name in periodStats) {
    const tData = allTeachers[name];
    if (tData && tData.message_id) {
      const periodAvg = periodStats[name].totalScore / periodStats[name].reviewCount;
      pbList.push({
        name: name,
        total_reviews: periodStats[name].reviewCount, 
        average_score: periodAvg,
        message_id: tData.message_id,
        channel_id: tData.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID
      });
    }
  }

  pbList.sort((a, b) => b.average_score - a.average_score);
  
  page = parseInt(page) || 0;
  const pageSize = 10;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(pbList.length / pageSize) || 1;
  const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 0), totalPages - 1);
  const startIndex = safePage * pageSize;
  const pbTop = pbList.slice(startIndex, startIndex + pageSize);

  const periodNames = { 'today': '今日', 'week': '本周', 'month': '本月', 'all': '总计' };
  const pName = periodNames[period];
  let pbText = `🏆 <b>综合评分公开榜</b> (第 ${safePage + 1}/${totalPages} 页)\n`;
  if (period === 'all') {
    pbText += `按累计综合评分排名\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
  } else {
    pbText += `按【${pName}】综合评分排名\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
  }

  if (pbList.length === 0) {
    pbText += `🏆 暂无${pName}的评分数据，榜单空空如也～\n`;
  }

  let pbKeyboard = [];
  pbTop.forEach((t, i) => {
    const globalIndex = startIndex + i;
    const medal = globalIndex === 0 ? '🥇' : globalIndex === 1 ? '🥈' : globalIndex === 2 ? '🥉' : `${globalIndex + 1}.`;
    const rLabel = t.average_score >= 8 ? '🌟好评' : t.average_score >= 5 ? '⭐中评' : '💔差评';
    const actionStr = period === 'all' ? '总计' : '本期';
    pbText += `${medal} ${escapeHtml(t.name)} — ⭐${t.average_score.toFixed(2)}分 [${rLabel}] 📋${actionStr}${t.total_reviews}份\n`;
    
    const targetChId = String(t.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID).replace(/^-100/, '');
    pbKeyboard.push([{ text: `${medal} ${t.name}（${t.average_score.toFixed(2)}分）`, url: `https://t.me/c/${targetChId}/${t.message_id}` }]);
  });
  
  // 增加动态翻页按钮
  let navRow = [];
  if (safePage > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `PB_PAGE_${period}_${safePage - 1}` });
  if (safePage < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `PB_PAGE_${period}_${safePage + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) pbKeyboard.push(navRow);

  pbKeyboard.push([
    { text: period === 'today' ? '✅ 今日' : '今日', callback_data: 'PB_PERIOD_today' },
    { text: period === 'week' ? '✅ 本周' : '本周', callback_data: 'PB_PERIOD_week' },
    { text: period === 'month' ? '✅ 本月' : '本月', callback_data: 'PB_PERIOD_month' },
    { text: period === 'all' ? '✅ 总榜' : '总榜', callback_data: 'PB_PERIOD_all' }
  ]);
  pbKeyboard.push([{ text: "✖️ 关闭榜单", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]);

  if (messageId) {
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: pbText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: pbKeyboard }
    });
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, pbText, { inline_keyboard: pbKeyboard }, "HTML", replyToMsgId);
  }
}

// 🟢 新增：动态生成按周期（今日/本周/本月/总）排名的新人收录榜
function sendNewcomerBoard(chatId, messageId, period, page, replyToMsgId) {
  const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
  const now = new Date();
  const utc8Now = new Date(now.getTime() + 8 * 3600000);
  let startTime = 0;
  if (period === 'today') {
    const startOfToday = new Date(utc8Now);
    startOfToday.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfToday.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  } else if (period === 'week') {
    const startOfWeek = new Date(utc8Now);
    const day = startOfWeek.getUTCDay() || 7;
    startOfWeek.setUTCDate(startOfWeek.getUTCDate() - day + 1);
    startOfWeek.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfWeek.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  } else if (period === 'month') {
    const startOfMonth = new Date(utc8Now);
    startOfMonth.setUTCDate(1);
    startOfMonth.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    startTime = startOfMonth.getTime() - 8 * 3600000;
  }

  let nbList = [];
  for (let name in allTeachers) {
    if (allTeachers[name].message_id) {
      const addedAt = allTeachers[name].added_at || 0;
      if (period === 'all' || addedAt >= startTime) {
        nbList.push({
          name: name,
          added_at: addedAt,
          total_reviews: allTeachers[name].total_reviews || 0,
          message_id: allTeachers[name].message_id,
          channel_id: allTeachers[name].channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID
        });
      }
    }
  }

  nbList.sort((a, b) => b.added_at - a.added_at);
  
  page = parseInt(page) || 0;
  const pageSize = 10;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(nbList.length / pageSize) || 1;
  const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 0), totalPages - 1);
  const startIndex = safePage * pageSize;
  const nbTop = nbList.slice(startIndex, startIndex + pageSize);

  const periodNames = { 'today': '今日', 'week': '本周', 'month': '本月', 'all': '总计' };
  const pName = periodNames[period];
  let nbText = `🌟 <b>新人榜 最新收录</b> (第 ${safePage + 1}/${totalPages} 页)\n`;
  if (period === 'all') {
    nbText += `按历史收录时间排名\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
  } else {
    nbText += `按【${pName}】收录时间排名\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;
  }

  if (nbList.length === 0) {
    nbText += `🌟 暂无${pName}的新收录数据，榜单空空如也～\n`;
  }

  let nbKeyboard = [];
  nbTop.forEach((t, i) => {
    const globalIndex = startIndex + i;
    const addDate = t.added_at > 0
      ? new Date(t.added_at).toLocaleString('zh-CN', { timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' })
      : '未知';
    nbText += `${globalIndex + 1}. 🌟 ${escapeHtml(t.name)} — 📅${addDate} 📋${t.total_reviews}份\n`;
    
    const targetChId = String(t.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID).replace(/^-100/, '');
    nbKeyboard.push([{ text: `🌟 ${t.name}（${addDate}收录）`, url: `https://t.me/c/${targetChId}/${t.message_id}` }]);
  });
  
  // 增加动态翻页按钮
  let navRow = [];
  if (safePage > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `NB_PAGE_${period}_${safePage - 1}` });
  if (safePage < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `NB_PAGE_${period}_${safePage + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) nbKeyboard.push(navRow);

  nbKeyboard.push([
    { text: period === 'today' ? '✅ 今日' : '今日', callback_data: 'NB_PERIOD_today' },
    { text: period === 'week' ? '✅ 本周' : '本周', callback_data: 'NB_PERIOD_week' },
    { text: period === 'month' ? '✅ 本月' : '本月', callback_data: 'NB_PERIOD_month' },
    { text: period === 'all' ? '✅ 总榜' : '总榜', callback_data: 'NB_PERIOD_all' }
  ]);
  nbKeyboard.push([{ text: "✖️ 关闭榜单", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]);

  if (messageId) {
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: nbText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: nbKeyboard }
    });
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, nbText, { inline_keyboard: nbKeyboard }, "HTML", replyToMsgId);
  }
}
// 🟢 新增：🔎查车评 搜索结果分页展示（支持翻页 + 查看详情 + 关闭）
function sendSearchReviewList(chatId, userId, messageId, page, keyword) {
  page = parseInt(page) || 0;
  const pageSize = 3; // 每页展示 3 条，可按需调整

  // 翻页时关键词从 UserProperties 缓存中读取
  if (!keyword) keyword = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('srev_kw_' + userId) || '';
  
  // 🟢 核心修复：二次过滤，确保翻页回调时关键词也是干净的，无 # 号且无多余空格
  keyword = keyword.replace(/#/g, '').trim();

  if (!keyword) {
    sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 搜索关键词已失效，请重新点击【🔎查车评】发起查询。");
    return;
  }

  // 1. 搜索所有已审核的匹配车评
  const allRevs = readFromFirebase('reviews');
  let found = [];
  if (allRevs) {
    for (let key in allRevs) {
      const r = allRevs[key];
      if (r.status === 'approved') {
        const tN = r.teacher || "", tP = r.process || "";
        if (tN.includes(keyword) || tP.includes(keyword)) found.push({ id: key, ...r });
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. 无结果时的处理
  if (found.length === 0) {
    const emptyMsg = `❌ 未找到与【${escapeHtml(keyword)}】相关的已发布车评记录。`;
    if (messageId) {
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: emptyMsg, parse_mode: "HTML",
        reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "✖️ 关闭", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] } });
    } else {
      sendMessage(chatId, emptyMsg);
    }
    return;
  }

  // 3. 按时间倒序排序（最新的在最前面）
  found.sort((a, b) => (b.timestamp || 0) - (a.timestamp || 0));

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(found.length / pageSize);
  if (page >= totalPages) page = Math.max(0, totalPages - 1);
  const startIdx = page * pageSize;
  const pageItems = found.slice(startIdx, startIdx + pageSize);

  // 4. 构建列表文本
  let msg = `🔍 <b>车评搜索：</b>【${escapeHtml(keyword)}】\n`;
  msg += `共 <b>${found.length}</b> 条结果（第 ${page + 1}/${totalPages} 页，每页 ${pageSize} 条）\n`;
  msg += `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n`;

  const kb = { inline_keyboard: [] };

  pageItems.forEach((r, i) => {
    const gIdx = startIdx + i + 1;
    const mediaIcon = r.media ? (r.media_type === 'photo' ? ' 🖼️' : ' 🎞️') : '';
    const dateStr = r.timestamp
      ? new Date(r.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('zh-CN', { timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai' })
      : '未知日期';
    // 计算该条车评的平均分
    let avgS = 0, sCnt = 0;
    ['photos', 'looks', 'body', 'service', 'attitude', 'env', 'cp'].forEach(f => {
      const m = String(r[f] || '').match(/[\d.]+/);
      if (m) { avgS += parseFloat(m[0]); sCnt++; }
    });
    const scoreStr = sCnt > 0 ? ` ⭐${(avgS / sCnt).toFixed(1)}` : '';
    // 取过程描述前 40 字作为预览
    const preview = r.process
      ? escapeHtml(r.process.slice(0, 40)) + (r.process.length > 40 ? '...' : '')
      : '（无过程描述）';

    msg += `<b>${gIdx}.</b> 👤 <b>${escapeHtml(r.teacher) || '未知老师'}</b>${scoreStr}${mediaIcon}\n`;
    msg += `📅 ${dateStr}  署名：${escapeHtml(r.reviewer) || '匿名'}\n`;
    msg += `💬 ${preview}\n`;
    msg += `───────────────\n`;

    // 每条车评生成「查看详情」按钮，携带车评ID和当前页码
    kb.inline_keyboard.push([
      { text: `👁️ 查看第 ${gIdx} 条完整详情`, callback_data: `SREV_VIEW_${r.id}_${page}` }
    ]);
  });

  // 5. 挂载翻页控制按钮
  let navRow = [];
  if (page > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `SREV_PAGE_${page - 1}` });
  if (page < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `SREV_PAGE_${page + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) kb.inline_keyboard.push(navRow);
  kb.inline_keyboard.push([{ text: "✖️ 关闭", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]);

  // 6. 首次进入发新消息；翻页 / 返回时原地编辑（不刷屏）
  if (messageId) {
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: msg, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: kb });
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, msg, kb, "HTML");
  }
}
// 🟢 新增：📚开课 在岗老师列表分页展示（支持翻页 + 点击查看资料 + 实时存活校验）
function sendOpenClassList(chatId, userId, messageId, page, replyToMsgId) {
  page = parseInt(page) || 0;
  const pageSize = 10; // 每页展示 10 位老师

  const okAllTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
  let okOnlineList = [];
  for (let name in okAllTeachers) {
    // 🟢 核心修复：把"是否在开课列表里显示"和"点击后跳去哪"彻底拆开，不再要求三个条件同时满足！
    // 旧代码要求 status=online 且 message_id 且 (tg_id 或 username) 三者缺一不可，
    // 但下面两种在【老师状态管理】里都会正常显示🟢在岗的真实情况，都会被这三选一不能少的条件误杀：
    //   ① 老师已绑定(有 tg_id/username)、管理员也设为🟢在岗，但资料帖还没发到频道(没有 message_id)；
    //   ② 老师资料帖已发到频道(有 message_id)、管理员也设为🟢在岗，但这位老师还没被单独绑定过 TG 账号(没有 tg_id/username)。
    // 现在只要 status=online，且 message_id / tg_id / username 三者任意一个存在（说明这是一条真实老师记录），就先纳入在线名单；
    // 具体"点击后跳资料页还是跳直连老师"，交给下面渲染按钮时按 message_id 是否存在来判断，不再影响是否显示。
    // 🟢 修复：收录成功（有 message_id）的在岗老师必显示；
    // 无 status 但有资料帖的，不再因从未打卡被整表滤掉（可选：仅 online）
    const st = okAllTeachers[name].status;
    const hasProfile = !!(okAllTeachers[name].message_id || okAllTeachers[name].tg_id || okAllTeachers[name].username);
    if (st === 'online' && hasProfile) {
      okOnlineList.push({
        name: name,
        message_id: okAllTeachers[name].message_id || null,
        channel_id: okAllTeachers[name].channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID,
        tg_id: okAllTeachers[name].tg_id || null,
        username: okAllTeachers[name].username || null
      });
    }
  }

  if (okOnlineList.length === 0) {
    const noMsg = "📚 当前暂时没有老师在线，请稍后再来查看～\n\n";
    if (messageId) {
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
        chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: noMsg,
        reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "✖️ 关闭", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] }
      });
    } else {
      sendMessage(chatId, noMsg, null, null, replyToMsgId);
    }
    return;
  }

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(okOnlineList.length / pageSize);
  if (page >= totalPages) page = Math.max(0, totalPages - 1);
  const startIdx = page * pageSize;
  const rawPageItems = okOnlineList.slice(startIdx, startIdx + pageSize);

  // ================== 🟢 核心修复：引入实时探针校验机制 ==================
  let validPageItems = rawPageItems;
  let deadTeachers = [];

  if (rawPageItems.length > 0) {
    let checkRequests = [];
    let checkMap = [];

    // 为当前页的每位老师生成一个无害 API 探针请求
    // 🟢 修复：只对已经发过资料帖（存在 message_id）的老师做存活探测；
    // 纯绑定、还没发资料帖的在岗老师没有帖子可探测，跳过探测、直接保留展示
    rawPageItems.forEach((t, idx) => {
      if (!t.message_id) return;
      checkRequests.push({
        url: TG_API_URL + 'editMessageReplyMarkup',
        method: 'post',
        contentType: 'application/json',
        payload: JSON.stringify({
          chat_id: String(t.channel_id),
          message_id: t.message_id,
          reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [] }
        }),
        muteHttpExceptions: true
      });
      checkMap.push(idx);
    });

    let liveResults = [];
    try { liveResults = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(checkRequests); } catch (e) {}

    // 过滤出真正存活的老师
    validPageItems = rawPageItems.filter((t, idx) => {
      if (!t.message_id) return true; // 🟢 没有资料帖的纯绑定老师无需验证，直接保留

      const resIdx = checkMap.indexOf(idx);
      if (resIdx === -1 || !liveResults[resIdx]) return true; // 请求异常兜底放行

      try {
        const d = JSON.parse(liveResults[resIdx].getContentText());
        const desc = (d.description || "").toLowerCase();
        
        // 防误杀机制：频道级别不可用时不视为“老师已被删”
        const isChatDead = desc.includes('chat not found') || desc.includes('kicked') || desc.includes('deactivated') || desc.includes('not a member');
        
        if (!d.ok && !isChatDead && desc && (desc.includes('message to edit not found') || desc.includes('message not found') || desc.includes('deleted'))) {
          deadTeachers.push(t.name);
          return false;
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      return true;
    });

    // 🟢 瞬间销毁幽灵数据：同步清理数据库及所有关联车评，并全网刷新在岗榜
    if (deadTeachers.length > 0) {
      deadTeachers.forEach(name => deleteTeacherAndAssociatedData(name));
      refreshAllTeacherStatusBoards(); 
      Logger.log(`🧹 【开课列表】实时清理了 ${deadTeachers.length} 位幽灵老师数据。`);
    }
  }

  // 兜底逻辑：如果这一页的数据被删光了（比如刚好是最后页，最后一人被清理），自动回退一页
  if (validPageItems.length === 0 && page > 0) {
    return sendOpenClassList(chatId, userId, messageId, page - 1, replyToMsgId);
  } else if (validPageItems.length === 0 && page === 0) {
    // 如果全被删光了
    const noMsg2 = "📚 刚刚在岗的老师资料已被管理员撤回，当前暂时没有老师在线～";
    if (messageId) {
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: noMsg2, reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "✖️ 关闭", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]] } });
    } else {
      sendMessage(chatId, noMsg2, null, null, replyToMsgId);
    }
    return;
  }

  // 计算去除幽灵数据后的真实在线人数
  const actualOnlineCount = Math.max(0, okOnlineList.length - deadTeachers.length);
  // ====================================================================

  let okText = `📚 <b>当前在岗老师</b>\n🟢 共 <b>${actualOnlineCount}</b> 位老师当前在岗`;
  if (totalPages > 1) okText += `（第 ${page + 1}/${totalPages} 页）`;
    // 正文仅保留统计标题，不再追加分隔线与名单

  const kb = { inline_keyboard: [] };
  
  // ================== 🟢 智能修复：动态查询频道属性（完美支持公开与私密频道自动转换） ==================
  let channelUsernameMap = {};
  if (validPageItems.length > 0) {
    // 收集当前页所有去重后的频道 ID
    const uniqueChannelIds = [...new Set(validPageItems.map(t => String(t.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID)))];
    
    // 构造批量 getChat 请求探针
    const chatRequests = uniqueChannelIds.map(chId => ({
      url: TG_API_URL + 'getChat',
      method: 'post',
      contentType: 'application/json',
      payload: JSON.stringify({ chat_id: chId }),
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    }));
    
    try {
      // 并发请求，不影响响应速度
      const chatResults = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(chatRequests);
      uniqueChannelIds.forEach((chId, idx) => {
        if (chatResults[idx]) {
          const res = JSON.parse(chatResults[idx].getContentText());
          // 如果 ok 且能拿到 username 字段，证明该频道当前是公开频道
          if (res.ok && res.result && res.result.username) {
            channelUsernameMap[chId] = res.result.username;
          }
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      Logger.log(`⚠️ 自动识别频道属性失败: ${e.message}`);
    }
  }
  // ============================================================================================

  // 🟢 循环遍历存活校验通过的 validPageItems 并生成自适应链接
  // （正文不再逐行列出老师名，仅保留上方统计标题 + 下方按钮）
  validPageItems.forEach(t => {
    // 🟢 修复：有资料帖(message_id)才跳资料页；纯绑定、还没发资料帖的老师改跳直连老师，
    // 不再因为拼不出资料页链接就把这位在岗老师从按钮列表里漏掉
    if (t.message_id) {
      const chIdStr = String(t.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID);
      let buttonUrl = "";
      // 💡 智能路由：如果 channelUsernameMap 中存在该频道的 username，说明是公开频道
      if (channelUsernameMap[chIdStr]) {
        buttonUrl = `https://t.me/${channelUsernameMap[chIdStr]}/${t.message_id}`;
      } else {
        // 否则作为私密频道处理，自动移除 -100 前缀并生成内联跳转链接
        const targetChannelId = chIdStr.replace(/^-100/, '');
        buttonUrl = `https://t.me/c/${targetChannelId}/${t.message_id}`;
      }
      kb.inline_keyboard.push([{ text: `🟢 ${t.name} — 点击查看资料`, url: buttonUrl }]);
    } else {
      const directUrl = t.username ? `https://t.me/${t.username}` : (t.tg_id ? `tg://user?id=${t.tg_id}` : null);
      if (directUrl) {
        kb.inline_keyboard.push([{ text: `🟢 ${t.name} — 尚无资料帖，点击直连老师`, url: directUrl }]);
      }
    }
  });

  // 翻页控制按钮
  let navRow = [];
  if (page > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `OK_LIST_${page - 1}` });
  if (page < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `OK_LIST_${page + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) kb.inline_keyboard.push(navRow);
  kb.inline_keyboard.push([{ text: "✖️ 关闭", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]);

  // 翻页时原位编辑；首次进入发新消息（支持引用回复）
  if (messageId) {
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: okText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: kb
    });
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, okText, kb, "HTML", replyToMsgId);
  }
}

// 🟢 新增：发送或刷新用户的专属车评列表（支持动态分页与操作）
function sendMyReviewsList(chatId, userId, messageId, page) {
  page = parseInt(page) || 0;
  const pageSize = 5; // 每页展示条数，可按需修改

  const allReviews = readFromFirebase('reviews');
  let myReviews = [];

  if (allReviews) {
    for (let key in allReviews) {
      // 强制转为 String 规避 64 位大数字精度丢失，实现安全越权隔离
      if (String(allReviews[key].userId) === String(userId)) {
        myReviews.push({ id: key, ...allReviews[key] });
      }
    }
  }

  if (myReviews.length === 0) {
    const emptyText = "📭 您还没有提交过任何车评记录。";
    if (messageId) {
      editMessageText(chatId, messageId, emptyText, null);
    } else {
      sendMessage(chatId, emptyText);
    }
    return;
  }

  // 按时间倒序排序 (最新的在最上面)
  myReviews.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(myReviews.length / pageSize);
  if (page >= totalPages) page = Math.max(0, totalPages - 1);
  
  const startIndex = page * pageSize;
  const displayReviews = myReviews.slice(startIndex, startIndex + pageSize);

  let text = `📋 <b>您的车评记录中心 (第 ${page + 1}/${totalPages} 页)</b>\n`;
  text += `共计 ${myReviews.length} 条记录。点击左侧 🔍 预览详情，点击右侧 ❌ 删除记录。\n\n`;

  const keyboard = { inline_keyboard: [] };

  displayReviews.forEach((rev) => {
    const statusIcon = rev.status === 'approved' ? '✅已发布' : (rev.status === 'rejected' ? '❌已拒绝' : '⏳审核中');
    const teacherName = escapeHtml(rev.teacher) || '未知';
    const shortId = rev.id.slice(-4); // 提取订单尾号方便对应
    const deleteTag = rev.delete_pending ? ' [🗑️删除审核中]' : ''; // 🟢 视觉提示标签
    
    text += `🆔 订单尾号: <code>${shortId}</code>\n`;
    text += `👤 老师: <b>${teacherName}</b> [${statusIcon}]${deleteTag}\n`;
    text += `⏱ 时间: ${new Date(rev.timestamp).toLocaleString('zh-CN', {timeZone: 'Asia/Shanghai'})}\n---\n`;

    // 动态生成操作按钮行：左侧预览，右侧为申请删除（防重复申请UI）
    let actionRow = [ { text: `🔍 预览: ${teacherName}`, callback_data: `MYREV_PV_${rev.id}_${page}` } ];
    actionRow.push({ text: rev.delete_pending ? `⏳ 审核删除中...` : `❌ 申请删除`, callback_data: `MYREV_DEL_${rev.id}_${page}` });
    keyboard.inline_keyboard.push(actionRow);
    
    // 🟢 核心新增：如果车评状态是“已发布”(approved)，则额外增加一行强制重新同步到频道评论区的按钮
    if (rev.status === 'approved') {
      keyboard.inline_keyboard.push([
        { text: `🔄 强制重新同步到频道评论区`, callback_data: `MYREV_RESUB_${rev.id}_${page}` }
      ]);
    }
  });

  // 动态挂载底部翻页控制按钮
  let navRow = [];
  if (page > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `MYREV_PAGE_${page - 1}` });
  if (page < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `MYREV_PAGE_${page + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) keyboard.inline_keyboard.push(navRow);

  // 根据入口来源决定是发送新消息还是平滑编辑旧消息
  if (messageId) {
    // 默认的 editMessageText 不支持传递 parse_mode，需手动组装 payload 发送 HTML
    const payload = { 
      chat_id: chatId, 
      message_id: messageId, 
      text: text, 
      parse_mode: "HTML", 
      reply_markup: keyboard 
    };
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", payload);
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, text, keyboard, "HTML");
  }
}

// 生成并发送包含所有字段及键盘的面板 (参考 photo_2026-06-17_16-46-00.jpg)
// 🟢 新增：渲染上榜申请标签选择面板（支持新发消息 or 原位编辑）
function sendRankingTagsPanel(chatId, userId, messageId, page) {
  page = parseInt(page) || 0;
  const rkDrRaw = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
  const rkDraft = rkDrRaw ? JSON.parse(rkDrRaw) : {};
  const selectedTags = rkDraft.tags || [];
  const availableTags = getCategoryTagList();

  let msgText = "🏷️ <b>最后一步：选择或填写标签后提交申请</b>\n\n";
  if (selectedTags.length > 0) {
    msgText += `✅ <b>已选标签：</b>${selectedTags.map(t => `#${escapeHtml(t)}`).join('  ')}\n\n`;
  }
  
  // 引入安全分页，每页最多显示 40 个标签（20行）
  const pageSize = 40; 
  const totalTags = availableTags.length;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalTags / pageSize) || 1;
  const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 0), totalPages - 1);
  const startIndex = safePage * pageSize;
  const displayTags = availableTags.slice(startIndex, startIndex + pageSize);

  if (availableTags.length > 0) {
    msgText += `👇 点击下方示例标签快速选择（可多选），或直接发文字输入自定义标签：\n(第 ${safePage + 1}/${totalPages} 页，共 ${totalTags} 个标签)\n`;
  } else {
    msgText += "当前暂无预设标签，请直接发送您的标签内容（多个用空格隔开），或直接点击提交：";
  }

  let keyboard = [];
  let row = [];
  // 仅渲染当前页的标签，同时将局部切片的 ID 转换回全局 ID 供回调识别
  displayTags.forEach((tag, localIdx) => {
    const globalIdx = startIndex + localIdx;
    const isSelected = selectedTags.includes(tag);
    row.push({ text: isSelected ? `✅ ${tag}` : tag, callback_data: `RANK_TAG_SEL_${globalIdx}_${safePage}` });
    if (row.length === 2) { keyboard.push(row); row = []; }
  });
  if (row.length > 0) keyboard.push(row);
  
  // 挂载动态翻页控制按钮
  let navRow = [];
  if (safePage > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `RANK_TAG_PAGE_${safePage - 1}` });
  if (safePage < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `RANK_TAG_PAGE_${safePage + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(navRow);

  // 底部固定：提交 + 取消
  keyboard.push([{ text: "📨 确认提交申请", callback_data: "RANK_TAG_SUBMIT" }]);
  keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 取消", callback_data: "CLOSE" }]);

  if (messageId) {
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
      chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId,
      text: msgText, parse_mode: "HTML",
      reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: keyboard }
    });
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, msgText, { inline_keyboard: keyboard }, "HTML");
  }
}

// 🟢 新增：提交上榜申请到 Firebase 并通知所有管理员（照片+课表分开发送）
function submitRankingApplication(chatId, userId, fromObj) {
  const rkDrRaw = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
  if (!rkDrRaw) {
    sendMessage(chatId, "❌ 申请数据已丢失，请重新点击【📋申请上榜】按钮开始提交。");
    return;
  }
  const rkDraft = JSON.parse(rkDrRaw);
  const submissionId = 'rnk' + new Date().getTime();
  const rankUserName = fromObj ? (fromObj.username ? '@' + fromObj.username : '未设置') : '未设置';
  const rankFirstName = fromObj ? (fromObj.first_name || '未知') : '未知';
  const tagsStr = (rkDraft.tags || []).join(' / ') || '未选择';

  // 写入 Firebase
  writeToFirebase(`ranking_submissions/${submissionId}`, {
    userId: String(userId),
    username: rankUserName,
    first_name: rankFirstName,
    address: rkDraft.address || '',
    price: rkDraft.price || '',
    services: rkDraft.services || '',
    type_feature: rkDraft.type_feature || '',
    photos: rkDraft.photos || [],
    schedule: rkDraft.schedule || '',
    tags: rkDraft.tags || [],
    status: 'pending',
    timestamp: new Date().getTime()
  });

  // 清理草稿和状态
  PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
  PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_ranking_' + userId);
  sendMessage(chatId, "✅ <b>您的上榜申请已成功提交！</b>\n\n⏳ 请耐心等待管理员审核，审核结果将通过私聊通知您。", null, "HTML");

  // 通知所有管理员
  const adminRankText = `📋 <b>【新的上榜申请待审核】</b>\n\n` +
    `👤 用户 ID：<code>${userId}</code>\n` +
    `👤 用户名：${escapeHtml(rankUserName)}\n` +
    `👤 昵称：${escapeHtml(rankFirstName)}\n` +
    `-------------------\n` +
    `📍 地址：${escapeHtml(rkDraft.address || '未填')}\n` +
    `💰 价格：${escapeHtml(rkDraft.price || '未填')}\n` +
    `💆 服务项目：${escapeHtml(rkDraft.services || '未填')}\n` +
    `✨ 类型特色：${escapeHtml(rkDraft.type_feature || '未填')}\n` +
    `🏷️ 标签：${escapeHtml(tagsStr)}\n` +
    `📸 照片数量：${(rkDraft.photos || []).length} 张\n` +
    `📅 课表：${rkDraft.schedule ? '已上传' : '未上传'}\n` +
    `-------------------\n` +
    `🆔 申请编号：<code>${submissionId}</code>`;
  const adminRankKb = {
    inline_keyboard: [[
      { text: "✅ 审核通过", callback_data: `ADMIN_APV_RANKING_${submissionId}_${userId}`, style: "success" },
      { text: "❌ 拒绝并填写理由", callback_data: `ADMIN_REJ_RANKING_${submissionId}_${userId}`, style: "danger" }
    ]]
  };

  ADMIN_USER_IDS.forEach(adminId => {
    const adminChatId = Number(adminId);
    sendMessage(adminChatId, adminRankText, adminRankKb, "HTML");
    // 逐张发送照片给管理员
    (rkDraft.photos || []).forEach((photoId, idx) => {
      try {
        sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", {
          chat_id: adminChatId,
          photo: photoId,
          caption: `📸 申请 <code>${submissionId}</code> 第 ${idx + 1}/${(rkDraft.photos || []).length} 张照片`,
          parse_mode: "HTML"
        });
      } catch(e) {}
    });
    // 单独发课表
    if (rkDraft.schedule) {
      try {
        sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", {
          chat_id: adminChatId,
          photo: rkDraft.schedule,
          caption: `📅 申请 <code>${submissionId}</code> 的课表`,
          parse_mode: "HTML"
        });
      } catch(e) {}
    }
  });
}
function sendMenuPanel(chatId, userId) {
  // 车评面板已下线：不再弹出打分/过程描述/上传/提交界面
  PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_' + userId);
  PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('state_' + userId);
  sendMessage(chatId, "ℹ️ 车评填写功能已关闭。");
}

// 保存草稿
function saveDraft(userId, field, value) {
  const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_' + userId);
  if (draftStr) {
    const draft = JSON.parse(draftStr);
    // 动态映射：将状态名转为小写作为属性名 (例如 PHOTOS -> photos, TEACHER -> teacher)
    const key = field.toLowerCase();
    draft[key] = value;
    PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_' + userId, JSON.stringify(draft));
  }
}

// ================= Firebase 数据库 API =================

// 🟢 内部工具：将 Firebase 路径 + 认证组装为完整请求 URL
function _buildFirebaseUrl(dbUrl, dbSecret, path) {
  const base = dbUrl.endsWith('/') ? dbUrl.slice(0, -1) : dbUrl;
  return dbSecret ? `${base}/${path}.json?auth=${dbSecret}` : `${base}/${path}.json`;
}

// 🟢 PATCH 更新（合并字段，不覆盖整个节点）
function updateFirebase(path, data) {
  if (!FIREBASE_DB_URL || FIREBASE_DB_URL.includes("your-project-id")) return;
  
  const payloadOptions = {
    method: "patch",
    contentType: "application/json",
    payload: JSON.stringify(data),
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  };
  UrlFetchApp.fetch(_buildFirebaseUrl(FIREBASE_DB_URL, FIREBASE_SECRET, path), payloadOptions);
}

// 🟢 PUT 写入（完整覆盖节点）
function writeToFirebase(path, data) {
  if (!FIREBASE_DB_URL || FIREBASE_DB_URL.includes("your-project-id")) return;
  
  // 动态追加 auth 参数，通过密钥直接绕过前端安全规则拦截
  const payloadOptions = {
    method: "put",
    contentType: "application/json",
    payload: JSON.stringify(data),
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  };
  UrlFetchApp.fetch(_buildFirebaseUrl(FIREBASE_DB_URL, FIREBASE_SECRET, path), payloadOptions);
}

// 🟢 GET 读取
function readFromFirebase(path) {
  if (!FIREBASE_DB_URL || FIREBASE_DB_URL.includes("your-project-id")) return null;

  let response;
  try {
    response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(_buildFirebaseUrl(FIREBASE_DB_URL, FIREBASE_SECRET, path), { muteHttpExceptions: true });
  } catch (e) { return null; }

  if (response.getResponseCode() === 200) {
    return JSON.parse(response.getContentText());
  }
  return null;
}

// 🟢 DELETE 删除
function deleteFirebase(path) {
  if (!FIREBASE_DB_URL || FIREBASE_DB_URL.includes("your-project-id")) return;
  UrlFetchApp.fetch(_buildFirebaseUrl(FIREBASE_DB_URL, FIREBASE_SECRET, path), {
    method: "delete",
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  });
}

// 🟢 核心新增：彻底删除老师资料（已移除车评关联清理）
function deleteTeacherAndAssociatedData(teacherName) {
  if (!teacherName) return;

  // 🟢 修复7：先读取老师数据，用于后续清理关联的 media_groups 相册缓存 + 绑定权限
  const _teacherSnapshot = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`) || {};

  // 1. 删除老师主节点资料
  deleteFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`);

  // 🟢 修复7：同步清理该老师的多图相册 Firebase 缓存，防止迁移后留下孤儿数据
  if (_teacherSnapshot.media_group_id) {
    deleteFirebase(`media_groups/${_teacherSnapshot.media_group_id}`);
  }

  // 🟢 同步清理 teacher_auth，防止「老师已删但绑定列表仍显示」的幽灵记录
  try {
    const authPatch = {};
    if (_teacherSnapshot.tg_id) {
      authPatch[`teacher_auth/${_teacherSnapshot.tg_id}`] = null;
    }
    if (_teacherSnapshot.username) {
      authPatch[`teacher_auth/${_teacherSnapshot.username}`] = null;
    }
    // 兜底：扫一遍 teacher_auth，清掉所有仍指向该老师名的 key（兼容历史只写了 auth、未写 tg_id 的脏数据）
    const allAuths = readFromFirebase('teacher_auth') || {};
    for (let k in allAuths) {
      if (String(allAuths[k]) === String(teacherName)) {
        authPatch[`teacher_auth/${k}`] = null;
      }
    }
    if (Object.keys(authPatch).length > 0) {
      updateFirebase('', authPatch);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

// 🟢 新增：获取（或首次创建并缓存）私密频道的专属邀请链接
// 优先从 Firebase 读取缓存，没有缓存才现场调用 createChatInviteLink 生成一条永久有效、无人数上限的邀请链接，
// 避免每次有人点"未关注"按钮都重复调用 TG API
function getChannelInviteLink() {
  // 1. 先尝试读取已缓存的邀请链接对象
  const cacheObj = readFromFirebase('config/invite_link_cache');
  // 🟢 核心修复：如果缓存存在，且绑定的频道ID等于当前最新配置的发布频道ID，才直接返回该链接
  if (cacheObj && cacheObj.channel_id === String(PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID) && cacheObj.link) {
    return cacheObj.link;
  }

  // 2. 没有缓存或频道已更换，调用 Telegram API 现场生成新链接
  // ⚠️ 注意：机器人必须已被设为该频道的管理员，且拥有"邀请用户"权限，否则这里会调用失败
  try {
    const res = sendTelegramMessage("createChatInviteLink", {
      chat_id: PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID,
      name: "查资料自动邀请" // 方便管理员在频道后台的"邀请链接"列表里识别这条是机器人自动生成的
    });
    const result = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
    if (result.ok && result.result && result.result.invite_link) {
      const link = result.result.invite_link;
      // 3. 🟢 写入全新的 Firebase 缓存节点，并将【当前频道ID】一并绑定存入。以后只要换频道就会自动重新生成！
      writeToFirebase('config/invite_link_cache', { channel_id: String(PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID), link: link });
      return link;
    } else {
      Logger.log("⚠️ 创建频道邀请链接失败：" + res.getContentText());
    }
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log("⚠️ 创建频道邀请链接异常：" + e);
  }
  return null;
}

// ================= TG API 请求基础方法 =================
function sendTelegramMessage(method, payload) {
  const options = {
    method: "post",
    contentType: "application/json",
    payload: JSON.stringify(payload),
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  };
  // 🟢 修复：把请求结果 return 出去，方便调用方判断这次请求到底成不成功
  // （典型场景：判断私聊用户是否失败，常见于用户从未跟机器人对话过）
  return UrlFetchApp.fetch(TG_API_URL + method, options);
}

// 🟢 新增：HTML 安全转义函数，防止用户输入的特殊字符破坏 TG 的 HTML 结构
function escapeHtml(text) {
  // 增强判空逻辑，并将所有输入强制转换为 String 型，防止因非字符类型导致 replace 方法崩溃
  if (text === undefined || text === null || text === '') return '';
  return String(text)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;'); // 增加双引号的转义，进一步防止 HTML 结构被破坏
}

// 🟢 新增：原生 TG 富文本解析器 (自动捕获 CTRL+K 超链接、加粗等原生排版)
function parseTelegramHtml(message) {
  const text = message.text || message.caption || "";
  const entities = message.entities || message.caption_entities || [];
  
  if (!entities || entities.length === 0) {
    return text.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  }
  
  entities.sort((a, b) => a.offset - b.offset);
  let html = "";
  let lastIndex = 0;
  
  for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
    const ent = entities[i];
    if (ent.offset < lastIndex) continue; // 忽略被嵌套覆盖的错位实体
    
    // 处理实体前面的纯文本并安全转义
    if (ent.offset > lastIndex) {
      const plainText = text.substring(lastIndex, ent.offset);
      html += plainText.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
    }
    
    // 提取当前实体内容
    const rawContent = text.substring(ent.offset, ent.offset + ent.length);
    const safeContent = rawContent.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
    
    // 映射 HTML 标签
    if (ent.type === "text_link") {
      html += `<a href="${ent.url}">${safeContent}</a>`;
    } else if (ent.type === "bold") {
      html += `<b>${safeContent}</b>`;
    } else if (ent.type === "italic") {
      html += `<i>${safeContent}</i>`;
    } else if (ent.type === "underline") {
      html += `<u>${safeContent}</u>`;
    } else if (ent.type === "strikethrough") {
      html += `<s>${safeContent}</s>`;
    } else if (ent.type === "code") {
      html += `<code>${safeContent}</code>`;
    } else if (ent.type === "pre") {
      html += `<pre>${safeContent}</pre>`;
    } else {
      html += safeContent;
    }
    lastIndex = ent.offset + ent.length;
  }
  
  // 补充尾部的纯文本
  if (lastIndex < text.length) {
    const plainText = text.substring(lastIndex);
    html += plainText.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  }
  
  return html;
}
// ============= 🟢 新增：群内【小姐姐在线列表 / 技师在岗榜】功能 =============

// 读取全局分类标签，统一解析成数组（去除每个标签开头的 #），供群内关键词命中匹配使用
function getCategoryTagList() {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  let globalTags = cache.get('category_tags_cache');

  if (!globalTags) {
    globalTags = readFromFirebase('system_config/category_tags') || "";
    // 缓存标签 5 分钟，极大减轻群聊识别时的数据库压力
    cache.put('category_tags_cache', globalTags, 300); 
  }

  if (!globalTags) return [];
  // 🟢 修复：全局剥离所有 # 号，确保提取的关键词纯净
  return globalTags.split(/\s+/).map(t => t.replace(/#/g, '').trim()).filter(t => t.length > 0);
}
// 🟢 老师群内打卡触发词（可在设置面板修改，默认：打卡/下班/休息）
// 🟢 提速：CacheService 缓存 5 分钟，群消息入口闸 + 打卡逻辑不再每次打 Firebase
function getCheckInKeywordMap() {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const cached = cache.get('checkin_keywords_v1');
  if (cached) {
    try { return JSON.parse(cached); } catch (e) {}
  }

  const conf = readFromFirebase('system_config/checkin_keywords') || {};
  const online = String(conf.online || '打卡').trim() || '打卡';
  const offline = String(conf.offline || '下班').trim() || '下班';
  const leave = String(conf.leave || '休息').trim() || '休息';
  const map = {};
  map[online] = 'online';
  map[offline] = 'offline';
  map[leave] = 'leave';
  const result = { map: map, online: online, offline: offline, leave: leave };
  try { cache.put('checkin_keywords_v1', JSON.stringify(result), 300); } catch (e) {}
  return result;
}

function getCheckInKeywordList() {
  const k = getCheckInKeywordMap();
  return [k.online, k.offline, k.leave];
}

// 🟢 管理员改触发词后立刻清缓存，保证下次命中最新配置
function invalidateCheckInKeywordCache() {
  try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove('checkin_keywords_v1'); } catch (e) {}
}
// 根据⏰老师打卡时写入的实时状态，返回对应的图例图标。从未打卡过的老师默认按"红休"展示
function getTeacherStatusEmoji(status) {
  if (status === 'online') return '🟢';
  if (status === 'leave') return '🟡';
  return '🔴'; // offline 或从未打卡，统一视为红休
}

const TEACHER_LIST_PAGE_SIZE = 30; // 🟢 每页至少展示 30 位老师

// 🟢 重构：渲染【管理员自定义的静态在岗面板】并实现状态热更新与自动分页
function sendTeacherStatusBoard(chatId, tagIndex, page, messageId, replyToMsgId) {
  // 🟢 修复1：增加 if 判断，将【静态面板】与下方的【动态榜单】完美隔离开来
  if (tagIndex === 'STATIC') {
    // 读取管理员预设好的图文排版
    const boardConfig = readFromFirebase('system_config/static_board');
    if (!boardConfig) {
