      if (!messageId) sendMessage(chatId, "⚠️ 管理员尚未配置在岗面板。\n请联系机器人在私聊中发送 /settings → [📡设置在岗面板] 进行快速初始化配置。");
      return;
    }

    let payload = { chat_id: chatId, parse_mode: "HTML" };
    let keyboard = [];
    let safePage = 0;
    
    // 🟢 极限优化：提前一次性读取全部老师状态库，避免在下面的按钮循环中频繁触发数据库网络请求导致卡顿
    const allTeachersData = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};

    // 1. 动态装载管理员配置的 Inline 按钮，解析 STATUS 同步变量并执行切片分页
    if (boardConfig.buttonsRaw) {
      let allButtons = [];
      // 将所有输入的按钮压平放入数组池
      boardConfig.buttonsRaw.split('\n').forEach(row => {
        row.split('|').forEach(btn => {
          let parts = btn.split('-');
          if (parts.length >= 2) {
            let urlObj = "";
            let styleObj = null;
            let statusBind = null;
         
            let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
            
            let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
            if (lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                statusBind = lastP.substring(7).trim();
                urlParts.pop();
           
                lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
            }
            if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
                styleObj = lastP.toLowerCase();
                urlParts.pop();
                lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                if (!statusBind && lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                    statusBind = lastP.substring(7).trim();
                    urlParts.pop();
                }
            }
            urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();
            if (!urlObj.startsWith('http') && !urlObj.startsWith('tg://')) urlObj = 'https://' + urlObj;
            
            let btnText = parts[0].trim();
            // 如果绑定了 STATUS，自动附加 🟢🔴🟡 图标后缀
            if (statusBind) {
               // 🟢 修复：直接从已缓存的总库中读取状态，耗时从几秒降至 0 毫秒
               const tData = allTeachersData[statusBind] || {};
               let sText = "🔴下班";
               if (tData.status === 'online') sText = "🟢在岗";
               else if (tData.status === 'leave') sText = "🟡休息";
               btnText += ` [${sText}]`;
            }
            
            let btnDef = { text: btnText, url: urlObj };
            if (styleObj) btnDef.style = styleObj;
            allButtons.push(btnDef); // 全部装载到一维数组池中
          }
        });
      });

      // 🟢 核心修复：内联按钮自动切片，强制一页 30 个，并且强制以 3 个一行的整齐网格排列
      const PAGE_SIZE = 30;
      const totalPages = Math.ceil(allButtons.length / PAGE_SIZE) || 1;
      safePage = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(page) || 0, 0), totalPages - 1);
      const startIndex = safePage * PAGE_SIZE;
      const pageButtons = allButtons.slice(startIndex, startIndex + PAGE_SIZE);

      let btnRow = [];
      pageButtons.forEach(btn => {
          btnRow.push(btn);
          // 积满 3 个就换行，确保 UI 整洁度极佳
          if (btnRow.length === 3) {
              keyboard.push(btnRow);
              btnRow = [];
          }
      });
      // 将不足 3 个的剩余尾巴也推进行列中
      if (btnRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(btnRow);

      // 挂载动态翻页控制按钮
      let navRow = [];
      if (safePage > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `TLIST_STATIC_${safePage - 1}` });
      if (safePage < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `TLIST_STATIC_${safePage + 1}` });
      if (navRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(navRow);
    }

    if (keyboard.length > 0) payload.reply_markup = { inline_keyboard: keyboard };
    
    // 2. 如果是老师打卡后触发的“刷新”动作，仅秒速替换底层按钮状态，防止重新发图造成刷屏
    if (messageId) {
       try {
         sendTelegramMessage("editMessageReplyMarkup", {
           chat_id: chatId,
           message_id: messageId,
           reply_markup: payload.reply_markup || { inline_keyboard: [] }
         });
         // 🟢 修复：翻页时要同步更新雷达缓存里的页码，不然下次别处打卡会导致该面板瞬间强制跳回第 0 页
         writeToFirebase(`status_boards/${chatId}`, { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, tag_index: 'STATIC', page: safePage });
       } catch(e) {}
       return; // 更新完毕，立即返回
    }

    // 3. 全新触发时：发布完整图/视频/文本，并留底待后续状态监控
    if (replyToMsgId) payload.reply_to_message_id = replyToMsgId;
    let sendRes;
    
    if (boardConfig.mediaId) {
       payload.caption = boardConfig.text || "";
       if (boardConfig.mediaType === 'photo') {
           payload.photo = boardConfig.mediaId;
           sendRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload);
       } else {
           payload.video = boardConfig.mediaId;
           sendRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload);
       }
    } else {
       payload.text = boardConfig.text || "📡 <b>在岗面板</b>";
       sendRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
    }

    // 获取发送成功的 message_id 并打入监控雷达，确保它像动态版一样随时同步变红变绿
    try {
       const resData = JSON.parse(sendRes.getContentText());
       if (resData.ok && resData.result && resData.result.message_id) {
         writeToFirebase(`status_boards/${chatId}`, { chat_id: chatId, message_id: resData.result.message_id, tag_index: 'STATIC', page: safePage });
       }
    } catch(e){}
    
    return; // 🟢 修复2：静态面板逻辑执行完后立刻返回，防止逻辑向下穿透
  }

  // --- 下方为原有的【动态标签榜单】逻辑 ---
  const categoryTagList = getCategoryTagList();
  const tagName = categoryTagList[tagIndex];
  if (!tagName) return;

  const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
  const teacherList = Object.keys(allTeachers)
      .filter(name => {
        // 频道帖子已删除（message_id 为空）的老师直接从列表剔除，不显示任何按钮
        if (!allTeachers[name].message_id) return false;
        
        // 🟢 修复：优先用 search_text（纯文本）；否则把 original_text 去 HTML 再匹配。
        // 解决「地址标签：大朗」等关键词因 HTML/转义导致 includes 失败、列表缺人的问题。
        const rawSearch = String(
          allTeachers[name].search_text ||
          String(allTeachers[name].original_text || "").replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
        ).toLowerCase();
        const tName = String(name).toLowerCase();
        const searchTag = String(tagName).toLowerCase().trim();
        if (!searchTag) return false;
        
        return rawSearch.includes(searchTag) || tName.includes(searchTag);
      })
    .map(name => ({
      name: name,
      status: allTeachers[name].status, // online / offline / leave / undefined(从未打卡)
      added_at: allTeachers[name].added_at || 0,
      message_id: allTeachers[name].message_id,
      // 🟢 核心修复 1：提取该老师专属的真实所在频道 ID，而不是用全局默认 ID
      channel_id: allTeachers[name].channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID 
    }))
    // 🟢 在岗(🟢) 排最前，休息(🟡)/下班(🔴) 排最后；同状态内仍按收录时间
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const rank = (s) => (s === 'online' ? 0 : 1); // 0=绿在前，1=黄/红在后
      const ra = rank(a.status);
      const rb = rank(b.status);
      if (ra !== rb) return ra - rb;
      return a.added_at - b.added_at;
    });

  const totalCount = teacherList.length;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalCount / TEACHER_LIST_PAGE_SIZE) || 1;
  const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 0), totalPages - 1);
  const startIndex = safePage * TEACHER_LIST_PAGE_SIZE;
  const displayTeachers = teacherList.slice(startIndex, startIndex + TEACHER_LIST_PAGE_SIZE);

  // 🟢 核心提速：把"渲染文案+按钮"抽成内部小函数，乐观秒回和探测后纠正复用同一套渲染逻辑，避免重复维护两份
  function buildBoardPayload(teachersToShow, deletedCountSoFar) {
    const validTotalCount = Math.max(0, totalCount - deletedCountSoFar);
    const safeTagName = escapeHtml(tagName);
          // 🟢 读取自动删除配置，用于文案提示
    let _autoMin = parseInt(readFromFirebase('system_config/status_board_auto_delete_minutes'), 10);
    if (isNaN(_autoMin) || _autoMin < 0) _autoMin = 0;
    if (_autoMin > 0 && _autoMin < 10) _autoMin = 10;
    if (_autoMin > 360) _autoMin = 360;
    let _delHint = "";
    if (_autoMin > 0) {
      _delHint = _autoMin >= 60
        ? `\n\n<i>* 本列表将在 ${(_autoMin / 60) % 1 === 0 ? (_autoMin / 60) + ' 小时' : _autoMin + ' 分钟'}后自动删除</i>`
        : `\n\n<i>* 本列表将在 ${_autoMin} 分钟后自动删除</i>`;
    }

        const boardText = validTotalCount === 0
      ? `❌ 暂未找到与【${safeTagName}】相关的老师资料。\n\n<i>* 本提示与触发关键词将在 ${_autoMin > 0 ? (_autoMin + ' 分钟') : '10 秒'}后自动删除</i>`
      : `为您找到 <b>${validTotalCount}</b> 位${safeTagName}的老师，请点击下面按钮查看。\n` +
        `🔴红=下班 🟢绿=在岗 🟡黄=休息\n` +
        `(第 ${safePage + 1}/${totalPages} 页，共 ${validTotalCount} 位)` + _delHint;

    // 👇 ⚠️ 如果你的频道是【公开频道】，请在这里配置【频道数字ID】到【频道公开用户名】的映射。
    // 用户名不要带 @。如果是私密频道，不填即可，系统会自动回退使用私密链接。
    const PUBLIC_CHANNEL_MAP = {
      "-1004435951158": "kmcangjingge", // 👈 请替换为你真实的公开用户名B频道（如 kmbailongmen）
      "-1004377016840": "bailongmen1", // A频道
      "-1003735695395": "kmwenyuange" // C频道
    };

    let boardKeyboard = [];
    let boardRow = [];

    teachersToShow.forEach((t) => {
      const emoji = getTeacherStatusEmoji(t.status);
      const chIdStr = String(t.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID);

      let buttonUrl = "";
      if (PUBLIC_CHANNEL_MAP[chIdStr]) {
        // 公开频道：使用 https://t.me/用户名/消息ID 格式（未加群的用户也能直接查看）
        buttonUrl = `https://t.me/${PUBLIC_CHANNEL_MAP[chIdStr]}/${t.message_id}`;
      } else {
        // 私密频道：使用默认的内部专属链接（必须加群才能查看）
        const targetChannelId = chIdStr.replace(/^-100/, '');
        buttonUrl = `https://t.me/c/${targetChannelId}/${t.message_id}`;
      }

            boardRow.push({ text: `${emoji} ${t.name}`, url: buttonUrl });
      if (boardRow.length === 2) {
        boardKeyboard.push(boardRow);
        boardRow = [];
      }
    });

    if (boardRow.length > 0) boardKeyboard.push(boardRow);

    // 翻页控制按钮
    let navRow = [];
    if (safePage > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `TLIST_${tagIndex}_${safePage - 1}` });
    if (safePage < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `TLIST_${tagIndex}_${safePage + 1}` });
    if (navRow.length > 0) boardKeyboard.push(navRow);

    return { text: boardText, keyboard: boardKeyboard };
  }

  // 🟢 核心提速 1/2：先用数据库现成缓存"乐观秒回"，完全不等待频道存活探测，恢复毫秒级响应
  const optimisticBoard = buildBoardPayload(displayTeachers, 0);
  const payload = {
    chat_id: chatId,
    text: optimisticBoard.text,
    parse_mode: "HTML",
    reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: optimisticBoard.keyboard }
  };

   let sentMessageId = messageId;

  if (messageId) {
    payload.message_id = messageId;
    sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", payload);
    // 翻页/刷新时同步更新追踪记录的页码
    writeToFirebase(`status_boards/${chatId}`, { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, tag_index: tagIndex, page: safePage });
  } else {
    if (replyToMsgId) {
      payload.reply_to_message_id = replyToMsgId;
    }
    const sendResult = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
    try {
      const resultData = JSON.parse(sendResult.getContentText());
      if (resultData.ok && resultData.result && resultData.result.message_id) {
        sentMessageId = resultData.result.message_id;
        writeToFirebase(`status_boards/${chatId}`, {
          chat_id: chatId,
          message_id: sentMessageId,
          tag_index: tagIndex,
          page: safePage
        });

                // 🟢 标签榜自动删除：读取设置（分钟），范围 10～360；0 或不设 = 关闭（空结果仍默认 10 秒清理）
        let autoDelMin = parseInt(readFromFirebase('system_config/status_board_auto_delete_minutes'), 10);
        if (isNaN(autoDelMin) || autoDelMin < 0) autoDelMin = 0;
        if (autoDelMin > 0 && autoDelMin < 10) autoDelMin = 10;
        if (autoDelMin > 360) autoDelMin = 360;

        if (autoDelMin > 0) {
          const expireAt = new Date().getTime() + autoDelMin * 60 * 1000;
          const queueId = `tsb_${new Date().getTime()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
          writeToFirebase(`delete_queue/${queueId}`, {
            chat_id: String(chatId),
            bot_msg_id: sentMessageId || null,
            user_msg_id: replyToMsgId || null,
            expire_at: expireAt
          });
        } else if (totalCount === 0) {
          // 未开启自动删除时：空结果仍 10 秒后清理，避免刷屏
          const expireAt = new Date().getTime() + 10 * 1000;
          const queueId = `tsb_empty_${new Date().getTime()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
          writeToFirebase(`delete_queue/${queueId}`, {
            chat_id: String(chatId),
            bot_msg_id: sentMessageId || null,
            user_msg_id: replyToMsgId || null,
            expire_at: expireAt
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      // 解析失败静默忽略
    }
  }

  // 🟢 核心提速 2/2：秒回已经发出去了，用户完全无感知，现在才做实时探测校验（不再卡在回复前面）
  // 叠加 CacheService 短期"存活缓存"，同一位老师 120 秒内被重复搜到直接免检查，不再消耗 UrlFetch 配额
  const ALIVE_CACHE_TTL = 120; // 秒。存活缓存有效期，与 cleanupDeletedTeachers 的 2 分钟周期保持一致的新鲜度上限
  const aliveCache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const probeTargets = displayTeachers.filter(t => t.message_id && !aliveCache.get(`tsb_alive_${t.channel_id}_${t.message_id}`));

  if (probeTargets.length > 0) {
    const probeBatch = probeTargets.map(t => ({
      url: TG_API_URL + 'editMessageReplyMarkup',
      method: 'post',
      contentType: 'application/json',
      payload: JSON.stringify({
        chat_id: String(t.channel_id),
        message_id: t.message_id,
        reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [] }
      }),
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    }));

    try {
      const probeResults = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(probeBatch);
      const deletedNames = [];

      probeTargets.forEach((t, idx) => {
        try {
          const d = JSON.parse(probeResults[idx].getContentText());
          const desc = (d.description || "").toLowerCase();
          // 🟢 与 cleanupDeletedTeachers 保持一致：区分"帖子被删"与"整个频道/权限失效"，避免误杀
          const isChatDead = desc.includes('chat not found') || desc.includes('kicked') || desc.includes('deactivated') || desc.includes('not a member');
          const isDeleted = !d.ok && !isChatDead && desc && (desc.includes('message to edit not found') || desc.includes('message not found') || desc.includes('deleted'));
          if (isDeleted) {
            deletedNames.push(t.name);
          } else {
            // 🟢 确认存活，写入短期缓存，2分钟内同一位老师再被搜到直接免检查
            aliveCache.put(`tsb_alive_${t.channel_id}_${t.message_id}`, "1", ALIVE_CACHE_TTL);
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      });

      if (deletedNames.length > 0) {
        // 🟢 核心新增：探测到有已删老师混在刚才的秒回列表里，立刻用纠正后的数据把那条消息原位更新掉
        const correctedTeachers = displayTeachers.filter(t => !deletedNames.includes(t.name));
        const correctedBoard = buildBoardPayload(correctedTeachers, deletedNames.length);

        if (sentMessageId) {
          try {
            sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
              chat_id: chatId,
              message_id: sentMessageId,
              text: correctedBoard.text,
              parse_mode: "HTML",
              reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: correctedBoard.keyboard }
            });
          } catch (e) {}
        }

        // 🟢 清理 Firebase 失效数据（含关联车评），避免下次搜索命中同一批已删老师重复探测
        deletedNames.forEach(name => {
          try { deleteTeacherAndAssociatedData(name); } catch (e) {}
        });
        Logger.log(`🗑️ 标签榜探测后纠正了 ${deletedNames.length} 位频道已删老师：${deletedNames.join('、')}`);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // 探测异常时静默忽略，秒回的消息已经发出去了，不影响用户体验
      Logger.log('sendTeacherStatusBoard 实时校验异常: ' + e);
    }
  }
}

// 🟢 新增：老师打卡状态发生变动后，自动把所有群里已展示过的【在岗榜】消息原位刷新一遍，做到实时同步
function refreshAllTeacherStatusBoards() {
  const boards = readFromFirebase('status_boards');
  if (!boards) return;
  for (let chatKey in boards) {
    const board = boards[chatKey];
    if (!board || !board.chat_id || !board.message_id || board.tag_index === undefined) continue;
    try {
      sendTeacherStatusBoard(board.chat_id, board.tag_index, board.page || 0, board.message_id);
    } catch (err) {
      // 单条刷新失败（例如消息已被群管理员手动删除）不应影响其他群的刷新
    }
  }
}
// 🟢 新增：后台定时清理已被频道删除的老师帖子，并自动刷新群内所有已呼出的在岗榜
// 工作原理与老师打卡完全一致：检测到变化 → 清除 Firebase 失效数据 → 调用 refreshAllTeacherStatusBoards
// ⚙️ 设置方式：GAS 编辑器左侧「触发器」→「新增触发器」→ 选择此函数 → 时间驱动 → 每 5 分钟
// ================= 🟢 新增：频道分级升降级系统 =================

/**
 * 升级检查：车评通过审核后调用，判断是否满足 B→A 升级条件
 * 规则：在B频道（或默认频道）累积3篇好评（单次均分≥8.0）→ 自动升至A频道
 */
function checkAndUpgradeTeacherTier(teacherName, goodReviewCount) {
  const teacherData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`);
  if (!teacherData || !teacherData.message_id) return;

  const currentTier = teacherData.channel_tier || '';
  if (currentTier === 'A') return; // 已是最高级，无需升级

  // 🟢 核心修改：支持 C升B，以及 默认/B升A
  if (goodReviewCount >= 3) {
    if (currentTier === 'C' && TIER_CHANNELS.B) {
      moveTeacherToTierChannel(teacherName, teacherData, 'B');
    } else if ((currentTier === 'B' || currentTier === '') && TIER_CHANNELS.A) {
      moveTeacherToTierChannel(teacherName, teacherData, 'A');
    }
  }
}

/**
 * 将老师帖子从当前频道搬迁到目标分级频道
 * - 纯文字帖：sendMessage 发到目标频道
 * - 媒体帖：copyMessage 复制到目标频道再编辑 caption
 * - 完成后删除旧帖，更新 Firebase，通知管理员
 */
function moveTeacherToTierChannel(teacherName, teacherData, newTier) {
  const targetChannelId = TIER_CHANNELS[newTier];
  if (!targetChannelId) return;

  const sourceChannelId = String(teacherData.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID);
  if (sourceChannelId === String(targetChannelId)) {
    // 已在目标频道，仅更新分级标记，无需移帖
    updateFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`, { channel_tier: newTier, tier_changed_at: new Date().getTime() });
    return;
  }

  try {
    // 1. 组装含分级标识的最新评分面板
    const avgScores = teacherData.avg_scores || {};
    const avgOverall = parseFloat(teacherData.average_score) || 0;
    const totalReviews = teacherData.total_reviews || 0;
    let ratingLabel = '';
    if (avgOverall >= 8) ratingLabel = ' 🌟<b>[好评]</b>';
    else if (avgOverall >= 5) ratingLabel = ' ⭐<b>[中评]</b>';
    else if (avgOverall > 0) ratingLabel = ' 💔<b>[差评]</b>';

    const tierBadgeMap = { A: '🏆 <b>[已升级至A级频道]</b>', B: '⭐ <b>[已迁至B级频道]</b>', C: '📋 <b>[已迁至C级频道]</b>' };
    const tierBadge = tierBadgeMap[newTier] || '';

    const profileScorePanel =
      `📊 <b>【全维度评分】: ${avgOverall > 0 ? avgOverall.toFixed(2) + ' 分' : '暂无'}</b>${ratingLabel} (基于 ${totalReviews} 份真实车评)\n` +
      `📷人照: ${avgScores.photos > 0 ? avgScores.photos.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ✨颜值: ${avgScores.looks > 0 ? avgScores.looks.toFixed(1) : '-'} | 💃身材: ${avgScores.body > 0 ? avgScores.body.toFixed(1) : '-'}\n` +
      `💋服务: ${avgScores.service > 0 ? avgScores.service.toFixed(1) : '-'} | 💓态度: ${avgScores.attitude > 0 ? avgScores.attitude.toFixed(1) : '-'} | 🏡环境: ${avgScores.env > 0 ? avgScores.env.toFixed(1) : '-'} | 💰性价比: ${avgScores.cp > 0 ? avgScores.cp.toFixed(1) : '-'}\n` +
      `${tierBadge}\n` +
      `-------------------\n`;

    let safeText = (teacherData.original_text || teacherName).replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
    safeText = safeText.replace(/📊[\s\S]*?【全维度评分】[\s\S]*?-------------------\n?/g, '').trim();
    let profileLines = safeText.split('\n');
    // 🟢 优先寻找“编号”行，如果没有再寻找名字行
    let pIdx = profileLines.findIndex(l => l.includes('编号'));
    if (pIdx === -1) pIdx = profileLines.findIndex(l => l.includes(teacherName) || l.includes('名字') || l.includes('姓名') || l.includes('老师'));
    
    if (pIdx !== -1) {
      profileLines.splice(pIdx + 1, 0, '\n' + profileScorePanel.trim() + '\n');
    } else {
      profileLines.unshift(profileScorePanel.trim());
    }
    const newText = profileLines.join('\n');

    // 2. 在目标频道创建新帖
    let newMsgId = null;
    if (teacherData.has_media) {
      let migratedSuccessfully = false;

      // 🟢 核心修复 (相册支持)：读取记录的相册组，使用 copyMessages 批量无损迁移整个图集
      if (teacherData.media_group_id) {
        const mgObj = readFromFirebase(`media_groups/${teacherData.media_group_id}`);
        if (mgObj) {
          const mgIds = Object.keys(mgObj).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b);
          if (mgIds.length > 0) {
            const copyRes = sendTelegramMessage("copyMessages", {
              chat_id: targetChannelId,
              from_chat_id: sourceChannelId,
              message_ids: mgIds
            });
            
            try {
              const copyData = JSON.parse(copyRes.getContentText());
              if (copyData.ok && copyData.result && copyData.result.length > 0) {
                migratedSuccessfully = true;
                
                // 找出含有文案的那张主图的最新 message_id 并更新文案
                const textMsgIndex = mgIds.indexOf(Number(teacherData.message_id));
                if (textMsgIndex !== -1 && copyData.result[textMsgIndex]) {
                  newMsgId = copyData.result[textMsgIndex].message_id;
                  sendTelegramMessage("editMessageCaption", { chat_id: targetChannelId, message_id: newMsgId, caption: newText, parse_mode: "HTML" });
                } else {
                  newMsgId = copyData.result[0].message_id; // 兜底
                }

                // 批量物理销毁原频道的整组废弃图集，彻底避免遗留孤儿照片
                sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessages", { chat_id: sourceChannelId, message_ids: mgIds });
              }
            } catch (e) {
              Logger.log("批量迁移图集异常: " + e);
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // 兜底方案：针对以前没有记录过相册ID的历史老数据，平滑降级保留原作者的链接方案
      if (!migratedSuccessfully) {
        const sourceLink = `https://t.me/c/${String(sourceChannelId).replace(/^-100/, '')}/${teacherData.message_id}`;
        const textWithMediaLink = newText + `\n🔗 <a href="${sourceLink}">点击查看老师原图/视频附件</a>`;
        
        const sendRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", { 
          chat_id: targetChannelId, 
          text: textWithMediaLink, 
          parse_mode: "HTML",
          disable_web_page_preview: true 
        });
        const sendData = JSON.parse(sendRes.getContentText());
        if (sendData.ok) {
          newMsgId = sendData.result.message_id;
          sendTelegramMessage("editMessageCaption", { chat_id: sourceChannelId, message_id: teacherData.message_id, caption: newText, parse_mode: "HTML" });
        } else {
          Logger.log('迁移兜底方案失败: ' + (sendData.description || '未知错误'));
          return;
        }
      }
    } else {
      // 纯文字帖子：直接在目标频道发新消息
      const sendRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", { chat_id: targetChannelId, text: newText, parse_mode: "HTML" });
      const sendData = JSON.parse(sendRes.getContentText());
      if (!sendData.ok) { Logger.log('moveTeacherToTierChannel sendMessage失败: ' + sendData.description); return; }
      newMsgId = sendData.result.message_id;

      // 3. 纯文本帖不存在断裂问题，正常删除原频道旧帖
      try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: sourceChannelId, message_id: teacherData.message_id }); } catch(e) {}
    }

    // 4. 更新 Firebase 分级状态
    updateFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`, {
      channel_id: String(targetChannelId),
      message_id: newMsgId,
      channel_tier: newTier,
      tier_changed_at: new Date().getTime(),
      good_review_count: 0,   // 🟢 循环机制核心：频道升降级后，将当前积累的好评数清空重置，重新开始计算！
      group_id: null,         // 🟢 修复：清除旧频道评论区引用，防止升级后新车评仍发到原群组
      group_message_id: null  // 🟢 修复：同上，确保后续车评直接发到升级后的分级频道并引用新帖
    });

    // 5. 通知管理员 + 原频道
    const tierNameMap = { A: 'A级（高级频道）', B: 'B级（标准频道）', C: 'C级（初级频道）' };
    const newLink = `https://t.me/c/${String(targetChannelId).replace(/^-100/, '')}/${newMsgId}`;
    const tierNotifText =
      `📊 <b>频道分级变动通知</b>\n\n老师 <b>${escapeHtml(teacherName)}</b> 已自动迁移至 ${tierNameMap[newTier] || newTier}\n` +
      `📈 综合评分：${avgOverall > 0 ? avgOverall.toFixed(2) + ' 分' : '暂无'} | 📋 总车评：${totalReviews} 份\n` +
      `🔗 新帖：<a href="${newLink}">点击查看</a>`;

    // 5a. 通知管理员（私聊）
    ADMIN_USER_IDS.forEach(adminId => {
      try {
        sendMessage(Number(adminId), tierNotifText, null, "HTML");
      } catch(e) {}
    });

    // 5b. 在原频道发公告，方便频道用户知道老师去向
    try {
      sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", {
        chat_id: sourceChannelId,
        text: tierNotifText,
        parse_mode: "HTML",
        disable_web_page_preview: true
      });
    } catch(e) {
      Logger.log('原频道发送迁移通知失败: ' + e);
    }
    Logger.log(`📊 老师 [${teacherName}] 已迁移至 ${newTier} 级频道 (新 msg_id: ${newMsgId})`);
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log('moveTeacherToTierChannel 异常: ' + e);
  }
}

/**
 * 降级检查：由 cleanupDeletedTeachers 定时调用（每小时执行一次）
 * 规则：A或B频道超过30天无新车评 → 自动降一级
 */
function checkAndDowngradeTeacherTiers() {
  if (!TIER_CHANNELS.A && !TIER_CHANNELS.B && !TIER_CHANNELS.C) return; // 未配置分级频道则跳过

  // 🟢 速率限制：每小时最多执行一次，避免每分钟重复扫描所有老师
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const lastCheck = parseInt(props.getProperty('last_tier_downgrade_check')) || 0;
  const now = new Date().getTime();
  if (now - lastCheck < 60 * 60 * 1000) return; // 未满1小时，跳过
  props.setProperty('last_tier_downgrade_check', String(now));

  const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
  const ONE_MONTH = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 30天毫秒数

  for (let name in allTeachers) {
    const t = allTeachers[name];
    // 只处理已分级（A或B）且有帖子的老师
    if (!t.message_id || !t.channel_tier || t.channel_tier === 'C') continue;

    const lastReview = t.tier_last_review_at || t.added_at || 0;
    if ((now - lastReview) <= ONE_MONTH) continue; // 30天内有车评，不降级

    const currentTier = t.channel_tier;
        let newTier = null;
        
        // 🟢 核心修改：A或B频道 30天内无新车评 → 自动直接降至 C频道
        if ((currentTier === 'A' || currentTier === 'B') && TIER_CHANNELS.C) {
          newTier = 'C';
        } else if (currentTier === 'A' && TIER_CHANNELS.B && !TIER_CHANNELS.C) {
          // 兜底兼容：如果管理员没配置C频道，但配置了B频道，则A频道降级到B频道
          newTier = 'B';
        }

        if (newTier) {
          const latestData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${name}`);
          if (latestData) {
            moveTeacherToTierChannel(name, latestData, newTier);
            Logger.log(`📉 老师 [${name}] 超30天无车评，从 ${currentTier} 直接降至 ${newTier} 级`);
          }
        }
  }
}

function cleanupDeletedTeachers() {
  // 🟢 核心修复：满足系统的 2 分钟缓冲执行要求。
  // 利用时间戳拦截，当前分钟数为奇数时直接退出，完美实现 2 分钟精准执行周期
  if (new Date().getMinutes() % 2 !== 0) return;

  // 🟢 加载动态配置（频道 ID 等）
  initGlobalConfig();

    // 🟢 核心修复 1：将榜单发布队列前置！
  // 避免当系统中没有任何老师资料时，下方的 return 阻断了队列运行，导致定时榜单功能彻底瘫痪
    processScheduledLeaderboards();
  try { processScheduledStatusTasks(); } catch (e) {}
  try { processAutoDeleteQueue(); } catch (e) {}
  try { processChannelIndexRetry(); } catch (e) {}

  // 🟢 核心修复：双保险执行关键词自动删除队列（即使专用触发器异常，也能在这里兜底清理）
  try { processAutoDeleteQueue(); } catch(e) {}

  const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};

  // 只取有 message_id 的老师（没有的已是干净状态，不需要验证）
  const teachersWithMsg = Object.keys(allTeachers)
    .filter(name => allTeachers[name].message_id)
    .map(name => ({ 
      name: name, 
      message_id: allTeachers[name].message_id,
      channel_id: allTeachers[name].channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID // 🟢 核心修复：提取老师专属的精准频道 ID，兼容老数据
    }));
  if (teachersWithMsg.length === 0) return;

  let hasDeleted = false;

  // 🟢 核心修复：引入游标 (Cursor) 缓存，每次执行只验证少量老师，摒弃一次性循环所有人。
  // 彻底解决 UrlFetchApp 超过每日 20,000 次配额上限的崩溃问题。
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  let lastIndex = parseInt(props.getProperty('cleanup_cursor')) || 0;
  
  // 如果游标超出了目前资料库的最大长度，重置回 0，从头开始查
  if (lastIndex >= teachersWithMsg.length) {
    lastIndex = 0;
  }

  // 🟢 升级：每批次从10提升至20，每2分钟扫描20条
  //    100名老师约10分钟完成全量验证，管理员删帖后最长10分钟内数据库自动同步
  const chunkSize = 20;
  const chunk = teachersWithMsg.slice(lastIndex, lastIndex + chunkSize);
  const validateBatch = chunk.map(t => ({
    url: TG_API_URL + 'editMessageReplyMarkup',
    method: 'post',
    contentType: 'application/json',
    payload: JSON.stringify({
      chat_id: t.channel_id, // 🟢 核心修复：使用精准的频道 ID 校验，彻底避免全局频道修改导致的跨服误删灾难
      message_id: t.message_id,
      reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [] }
    }),
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  }));

  try {
    const batchResults = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(validateBatch);
    chunk.forEach((t, j) => {
      try {
        // 修复后
        const d = JSON.parse(batchResults[j].getContentText());
        const desc = (d.description || "").toLowerCase();
        
        // 🟢 核心修复：如果发现是频道级别不可用，拒绝判定为“已删除”，保护数据库
        const isChatDead = desc.includes('chat not found') || desc.includes('kicked') || desc.includes('deactivated') || desc.includes('not a member');
        const isDeleted = !d.ok && !isChatDead && desc && (desc.includes('message to edit not found') || desc.includes('message not found') || desc.includes('deleted'));
        
        if (isDeleted) {
          // 🟢 核心升级：不仅清除老师节点，直接把整个老师节点及关联所有车评清除，防止残留废数据
          deleteTeacherAndAssociatedData(t.name);
          hasDeleted = true;
          Logger.log(`🗑️ 已清理：老师 [${t.name}] 的频道帖子及所有关联车评已被删除`);
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    });
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('cleanupDeletedTeachers 批量验证异常: ' + e);
  }

  // 保存下一次要检查的起始游标位置
  props.setProperty('cleanup_cursor', lastIndex + chunkSize);

  // 🟢 只要发现有老师被清理，立刻原位刷新所有群内已呼出的在岗榜，让废按钮时时消失
  if (hasDeleted) {
    refreshAllTeacherStatusBoards();
  }
}

// 🟢 新增：处理定时发送和到期自动删除的队列任务
function processScheduledLeaderboards() {
  const tasks = readFromFirebase('schedule_queue');
  if (!tasks) return;
  
  for (let id in tasks) {
    let task = tasks[id];
    const now = new Date().getTime(); // 🟢 核心修复：把获取当前时间移入循环体内，避免任务休眠叠加导致共用旧时间轴引起判定乱窜
    // 1. 全局检查：是否到达任务结束时间（适用于单次和循环）
            if (task.endTime && now >= task.endTime) {
              // 自动删除群里残留的最后一条消息
              const msgToDelete = task.messageId || task.lastMessageId;
              if (msgToDelete) {
                 try { 
                   sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: task.target, message_id: msgToDelete }); 
                   // 🛠️ 核心修复：增加 100 毫秒微延迟，防止同秒大批量到期时，瞬间密集删除触发 API 限流
                   Utilities.sleep(100); 
                 } catch(e) {}
              }
              deleteFirebase(`schedule_queue/${id}`);
              continue; // 终止此任务本次循环
            }

    // 2. 到达发送时间且尚未发送 (循环任务发送后状态会重新变回 pending 并更新 sendTime)
    if (task.status === 'pending' && now >= task.sendTime) {
      // 🟢 互斥锁修复 1：立即将状态变更为 processing，防止并发触发器重复抓取旧任务！
      updateFirebase(`schedule_queue/${id}`, { status: 'processing' });
      
      let payload = { chat_id: task.target, parse_mode: "HTML" };
      // 解析动态排版的内联按钮
      if (task.buttonsRaw) {
        let keyboard = [];
        task.buttonsRaw.split('\n').forEach(row => {
          let btnRow = [];
          row.split('|').forEach(btn => {
            let parts = btn.split('-');
            if (parts.length >= 2) {
              let urlObj = "";
              let styleObj = null;
              let statusBind = null;
              let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
              
              let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
              if (lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                  statusBind = lastP.substring(7).trim();
                  urlParts.pop();
                  lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
              }
              if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
                  styleObj = lastP.toLowerCase();
                  urlParts.pop();
                  lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                  if (!statusBind && lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                      statusBind = lastP.substring(7).trim();
                      urlParts.pop();
                  }
              }
              urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();
           
              if (!urlObj.startsWith('http') && !urlObj.startsWith('tg://')) {
                urlObj = 'https://' + urlObj;
              }
              
              let btnText = parts[0].trim();
              if (statusBind) {
                 const tData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${statusBind}`) || {};
                 let sText = "🔴下班";
                 if (tData.status === 'online') sText = "🟢在岗";
                 else if (tData.status === 'leave') sText = "🟡休息";
                 btnText += ` [${sText}]`;
              }
              
              let btnDef = { text: btnText, url: urlObj };
              if (styleObj) btnDef.style = styleObj;
              btnRow.push(btnDef);
            }
          });
          if (btnRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(btnRow);
        });
        if (keyboard.length > 0) payload.reply_markup = { inline_keyboard: keyboard };
      }

      let resStr = null;
      if (task.mediaId) {
         payload.caption = task.text;
         if (task.mediaType === 'photo') { payload.photo = task.mediaId; resStr = sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload);
         }
         if (task.mediaType === 'video') { payload.video = task.mediaId;
         resStr = sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload); }
      } else {
         payload.text = task.text;
         resStr = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
      }

      // 🟢 核心修复 3：处理发送结果，增加对“发送失败”情况的拦截拦截，防止无效任务卡在队列引发死循环
              try {
                const resultData = JSON.parse(resStr.getContentText());
                if (resultData.ok && resultData.result) {
                  const sentMsgId = resultData.result.message_id;
                  // 🟢 循环机制核心：发新消息前，删掉上一条残留消息
                  if (task.lastMessageId) {
                     try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: task.target, message_id: task.lastMessageId }); } catch(e) {}
                  }

                  // 🟢 判断是否为循环任务并更新状态
                  if (task.loopInterval && task.loopInterval > 0) {
                     let interval = Number(task.loopInterval);
                     let baseTime = Number(task.sendTime);
                     
                     // 🟢 真正的核心修复：必须基于任务原本设定的起跑线 (baseTime) 进行步进累加。
                     // 通过死循环推算它在未来的下一个绝对节点，彻底避免多任务向触发器的当前时间 (now) 坍塌对齐。
                     let nextSendTime = baseTime + interval;
                     while (nextSendTime <= now) {
                         nextSendTime += interval;
                     }

                     updateFirebase(`schedule_queue/${id}`, {
                        status: 'pending', // 🟢 互斥锁修复 2：循环任务发送完毕后，释放锁回 pending 状态
                        sendTime: nextSendTime,
                        lastMessageId: sentMsgId
                     });
                  } else {
                     // 单次任务：状态置为 sent，存入 messageId 供未来结束时间到期后清理
                     updateFirebase(`schedule_queue/${id}`, { status: 'sent', messageId: sentMsgId });
                  }
                  
                  if (task.pin) {
                    sendTelegramMessage("pinChatMessage", { chat_id: task.target, message_id: sentMsgId, disable_notification: true });
                  }
                  
                  // 🟢 终极修复 2：将基础延迟与随机抖动结合（3500ms + 0~1000ms随机）
                  // 彻底打散可能扎堆执行的并发任务，完美避开 429 刷屏封控及 Firebase 并发覆写！
                  Utilities.sleep(3500 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000));
                } else {
                  // ❌ 发送失败
                  const errDesc = resultData.description || 'API Error';
                  Logger.log(`⚠️ 榜单任务 ${id} 发送失败: ${errDesc}`);
                  
                  // 🛠️ 核心修复：遇到官方频率超发限制时，绝不能标记为 failed！保留 pending 让其下周期平滑重试
                  if (errDesc.includes("Too Many Requests") || errDesc.includes("retry after")) {
                      updateFirebase(`schedule_queue/${id}`, { status: 'pending' }); // 🟢 互斥锁修复 3：限流时释放锁
                      Utilities.sleep(3000); // 避避风头，休眠3秒
                  } else {
                      updateFirebase(`schedule_queue/${id}`, { status: 'failed', error: errDesc });
                  }
                }
              } catch (e) {
                 Logger.log("发榜单异常: " + e);
                 updateFirebase(`schedule_queue/${id}`, { status: 'pending' }); // 🟢 互斥锁修复 4：网络异常时释放锁
                 // 🛠️ 核心修复：遭遇偶发网络波动时暂不判死刑，保留下周期重试机会
                 Utilities.sleep(2000);
              }
    }
  }
}

// 🟢 新增：智能时间解析器 (支持相对格式 "10分钟后" 和绝对格式 "2026-06-25 10:00")
function parseCustomTime(timeStr) {
  if (!timeStr) return null;
  const now = new Date().getTime();
  
  // 匹配相对时间
  const relMatch = timeStr.match(/^(\d+)(分钟|小时|天|周|月)后$/);
  if (relMatch) {
    const val = parseInt(relMatch[1]);
    const unit = relMatch[2];
    if (unit === '分钟') return now + val * 60 * 1000;
    if (unit === '小时') return now + val * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    if (unit === '天') return now + val * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    if (unit === '周') return now + val * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    if (unit === '月') return now + val * 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 粗略计算
  }
  
  // 匹配绝对时间
  // 🟢 核心修复 4：明确追加 GMT+0800 时区标识
  // 保证用户无论输入 "2026-06-25 10:00" 还是 "2026/06/25 10:00"，都能精准按【北京时间】解析执行
  const absTime = new Date(timeStr.replace(/-/g, '/') + ' GMT+0800').getTime();
  if (!isNaN(absTime)) return absTime;
  return null;
}

// 🟢 新增：老师打卡状态变动时，遍历所有关注该老师的用户并私信推送通知
function notifyTeacherFollowers(teacherName, statusText) {
  const allProfiles = readFromFirebase('user_profiles');
  if (!allProfiles) return;
  const channelNumericId = String(PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID).replace(/^-100/, '');
  const td = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`) || {};
  const profileUrl = td.message_id ? `https://t.me/c/${channelNumericId}/${td.message_id}` : null;

  for (let uid in allProfiles) {
    const profile = allProfiles[uid];
    if (profile.follows && profile.follows[teacherName]) {
      const notifyText = `🔔 <b>关注动态提醒</b>\n\n您关注的老师 <b>${escapeHtml(teacherName)}</b> 刚刚更新了状态：\n${statusText}\n\n点击下方按钮查看资料详情 👇`;
      const notifyKeyboard = profileUrl
        ? { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: `📋 查看 ${teacherName} 资料`, url: profileUrl }]] }
        : null;
      try { sendMessage(uid, notifyText, notifyKeyboard, "HTML"); } catch (e) { /* 用户屏蔽机器人则静默忽略 */ }
    }
  }
}

// 🟢 新增辅助函数：渲染管理员用的老师状态列表面板
// 供 SETTINGS_TSTATUS_ 和 ATSS.两处共用，避免代码重复
function sendAdminTeacherStatusList(chatId, messageId, page) {
  const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
  // 🟢 动态抓取当前会话中是否含有搜索关键词
  const keyword = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('tstatus_kw_' + chatId) || "";

  // 🟢 核心修复：加入 filter 拦截，并叠加关键词过滤
  const teacherList = Object.keys(allTeachers)
    .filter(name => {
      const isValid = allTeachers[name].message_id || allTeachers[name].tg_id;
      if (!isValid) return false;
      if (keyword && !name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword.toLowerCase())) return false; // 命中关键词
      return true;
    }) 
    .map(name => ({ 
      name, 
      status: allTeachers[name].status,
      // 🟢 补充 message_id 和 channel_id，用于实时帖子存活验证
      message_id: allTeachers[name].message_id || null,
      channel_id: allTeachers[name].channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => (allTeachers[a.name].added_at || 0) - (allTeachers[b.name].added_at || 0));

  const pageSize   = 5;
  const totalCount = teacherList.length;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalCount / pageSize) || 1;
  const safePage   = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(page) || 0, 0), totalPages - 1);
  const display    = teacherList.slice(safePage * pageSize, safePage * pageSize + pageSize);

  // 🟢 核心新增：实时验证当前页老师的频道帖子是否仍然存在，同步清理已被手动删除的数据
  let validDisplay = display;
  if (display.length > 0) {
    let checkRequests = [];
    let checkMap = []; // 记录请求与 display 索引的映射关系

    display.forEach((t, idx) => {
      // 只有已经发过资料帖（存在 message_id）的才发起网络校验，避免纯绑定老师校验报错
      if (t.message_id) {
        checkRequests.push({
          url: TG_API_URL + 'editMessageReplyMarkup',
          method: 'post',
          contentType: 'application/json',
          payload: JSON.stringify({
            chat_id: String(t.channel_id),
            message_id: t.message_id,
            reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: [] }
          }),
          muteHttpExceptions: true
        });
        checkMap.push(idx);
      }
    });

    let adminLiveResults = [];
    if (checkRequests.length > 0) {
       try { adminLiveResults = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(checkRequests); } catch (e) {}
    }

    let adminDeletedNames = [];
    validDisplay = display.filter((t, idx) => {
      // 如果该老师没有频道帖子（仅绑定阶段），直接放行保留显示
      if (!t.message_id) return true;

      // 找到该老师对应的请求结果索引
      const resultIndex = checkMap.indexOf(idx);
      if (resultIndex === -1 || !adminLiveResults[resultIndex]) return true; 

      try {
        const d = JSON.parse(adminLiveResults[resultIndex].getContentText());
        const desc = (d.description || "").toLowerCase();
        
        // 🟢 核心修复：保护面板打开时不引发误杀
        const isChatDead = desc.includes('chat not found') || desc.includes('kicked') || desc.includes('deactivated') || desc.includes('not a member');
        if (!d.ok && !isChatDead && desc && (
          desc.includes('message to edit not found') ||
          desc.includes('message not found') ||
          desc.includes('deleted')
        )) { 
          adminDeletedNames.push(t.name); 
          return false; 
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      return true;
    });

    if (adminDeletedNames.length > 0) {
      adminDeletedNames.forEach(name => deleteTeacherAndAssociatedData(name));
      Logger.log(`🗑️ STATUS面板实时清理了 ${adminDeletedNames.length} 位频道已删老师及所有关联车评`);
    }
  }

  const validAdminTotalCount = Math.max(0, totalCount - (display.length - validDisplay.length));
  
  let text = `📋 <b>老师状态管理 (第 ${safePage + 1}/${totalPages} 页)</b>\n`;
  if (keyword) {
    text += `🔍 当前搜索：<code>${escapeHtml(keyword)}</code> (找到 ${validAdminTotalCount} 位)\n`;
  } else {
    text += `共 ${validAdminTotalCount} 位老师。\n`;
  }
  text += `🔴红=下班 🟢绿=在岗 🟡黄=休息\n👇 点击按钮直接代替老师打卡：\n\n`;

  let keyboard = [];

  validDisplay.forEach(t => {
    const emoji = getTeacherStatusEmoji(t.status);
    text += `${emoji} <b>${escapeHtml(t.name)}</b>\n`;

    // 用"."做分隔符，因为 base64WebSafe 编码结果不含"."，绝对不会解析歧义
    const enc = encodeTeacherForDeepLink(t.name);
    keyboard.push([
      { text: `🟢 在岗`, callback_data: `ATSS.${enc}.online.${safePage}` },
      { text: `🔴 下班`, callback_data: `ATSS.${enc}.offline.${safePage}` },
      { text: `🟡 休息`, callback_data: `ATSS.${enc}.leave.${safePage}` }
    ]);
  });

  // 翻页控制
  let navRow = [];
  if (safePage > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `SETTINGS_TSTATUS_${safePage - 1}` });
  if (safePage < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `SETTINGS_TSTATUS_${safePage + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(navRow);
  
  // 🟢 加入搜索与清除按钮
  if (keyword) {
    keyboard.push([{ text: "✖️ 清除搜索结果", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSTATUS_SEARCH_CLEAR" }]);
  } else {
    keyboard.push([{ text: "🔍 搜索老师", callback_data: "SETTINGS_TSTATUS_SEARCH_START" }]);
  }
  keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回面板主页", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]);

  sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
    chat_id: chatId,
    message_id: messageId,
    text: text,
    parse_mode: "HTML",
    reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: keyboard }
  });
}

// ============= 在线列表功能结束 =============

// 🟢 新增：把老师名字编码成 Telegram Deep Link 允许的安全字符（仅支持 A-Z a-z 0-9 _ -）
// 因为老师名字通常是中文，必须先转码才能塞进 ?start= 链接参数
function encodeTeacherForDeepLink(name) {
  // 🟢 关键修复：必须显式指定 Utilities.Charset.UTF_8，否则 GAS 默认用 US-ASCII，会导致中文被破坏成 "??" 乱码
  return Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(name, Utilities.Charset.UTF_8).replace(/=+$/, '');
}

// 🟢 新增：把 Deep Link 里的编码还原成老师的真实名字
function decodeTeacherFromDeepLink(encoded) {
  // 解码前把被去掉的 = 填充符补回来，否则 Base64 解码会报错
  let padded = encoded;
  while (padded.length % 4 !== 0) {
    padded += '=';
  }
  const bytes = Utilities.base64DecodeWebSafe(padded);
  // 🟢 关键修复：解码时声明这是一个包含中文的 UTF-8 文本
  return Utilities.newBlob(bytes, 'text/plain', 'UTF-8').getDataAsString();
}

function sendMessage(chatId, text, keyboard, parseMode, replyToMsgId) {
  const payload = { chat_id: chatId, text: text };
  if (keyboard) payload.reply_markup = keyboard;
  if (parseMode) payload.parse_mode = parseMode;
  // 🟢 新增：支持引用回复，群组中按钮响应时传入 message_id 即可触发
  if (replyToMsgId) payload.reply_to_message_id = replyToMsgId;
  // 🟢 把结果 return 出去，方便调用方判断这条私聊消息到底有没有发成功
  return sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
}

// 🟢 核心升级：增加 parseMode 默认值，并捕获 TG 官方的"图文转化错误"
function editMessageText(chatId, messageId, text, keyboard, parseMode = "HTML") {
  const payload = { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: text, parse_mode: parseMode };
  
  // 满足 TG API 的键盘抹除标准
  if (keyboard === null) {
    payload.reply_markup = { inline_keyboard: [] };
  } else if (keyboard !== undefined) {
    payload.reply_markup = keyboard;
  }
  
  const res = sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", payload);

  // 🟢 核心防卡死机制：当我们在预览榜单（带图）后点击"返回"，
  // TG 官方会报错"there is no text in the message to edit"（不准把图片原地变成纯字）。
  // 这里自动捕获该报错，将"编辑"动作智能降级为"删除旧图 + 发送新字"，实现无缝回退！
  try {
    const resObj = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
    if (!resObj.ok && resObj.description.includes("there is no text in the message to edit")) {
      sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId });
      const fallbackRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
      // 🟢 核心修复：降级会删掉旧消息、改发一条全新消息，必须把新消息真实的 message_id 吐出去给调用方
      // 否则调用方手里攥着的还是这个已经被删掉的旧 messageId，后续想清理"过渡提示"时会扑空，新消息反而没人删
      const fallbackObj = JSON.parse(fallbackRes.getContentText());
      if (fallbackObj.ok) return fallbackObj.result.message_id;
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  return messageId; // 🟢 原地编辑成功，或异常兜底情况下，消息ID不变
}

function answerCallbackQuery(callbackQueryId, text, showAlert, url) {
  const payload = { callback_query_id: callbackQueryId };
  if (text) payload.text = text;
  // 🟢 新增：showAlert 为 true 时弹出需要用户手动点掉的弹窗，比普通 toast 更显眼，适合放重要提示
  if (showAlert) payload.show_alert = true;
  // 🟢 核心新增：url 字段会让 Telegram 客户端在用户点击按钮后自动跳转过去。
  // 官方文档明确说明：这里只支持跳转到「自己机器人的 t.me 链接」(可带 ?start= 参数)，不支持跳任意网址/频道，否则会报 URL_Invalid
  if (url) payload.url = url;
  sendTelegramMessage("answerCallbackQuery", payload);
}

// ================= 新增：手动运行的 Webhook 管理函数 =================

/**
 * 第一步：请先点击右上角“部署” -> “新建部署”，获取 Web 应用的 URL。
 * 第二步：在 Apps Script 编辑器左侧点击齿轮 (项目设置) -> 脚本属性，将 URL 填入 WEBAPP_URL。
 * 第三步：在编辑器上方选择 "setWebhook" 函数，点击“运行”。
 */
const WEBAPP_URL = scriptProps.getProperty('WEBAPP_URL');
// 👈 改为从项目属性动态读取部署后得到的网页应用 URL


// 🟢 核心修复补充：重新计算老师全维度评分并同步到频道的辅助函数（用于删除/撤回车评时引发的均分变动）
function recalculateAndSyncTeacherScore(teacherName) {
  if (!teacherName) return;
  const allReviews = readFromFirebase('reviews');
  let totalReviews = 0;
  let newAvgScores = { photos: 0, looks: 0, body: 0, service: 0, attitude: 0, env: 0, cp: 0 };
  let newValidCounts = { photos: 0, looks: 0, body: 0, service: 0, attitude: 0, env: 0, cp: 0 };
  let latestProcess = '暂无';
  let latestTimestamp = 0;

  if (allReviews) {
    for (let key in allReviews) {
      let r = allReviews[key];
      // 提取目前数据库中剩余的、已审核通过的、且属于该老师的所有车评
      if (r.status === 'approved' && r.teacher === teacherName) {
        totalReviews++;
        const reviewWeight = r.weight || 1.0; // 兼容没有权重的老数据
        ['photos', 'looks', 'body', 'service', 'attitude', 'env', 'cp'].forEach(f => {
          const match = String(r[f] || '').match(/[\d.]+/);
          if (match) {
            // 🟢 核心修改：重算时也必须乘上当年提交时的权重
            newAvgScores[f] += parseFloat(match[0]) * reviewWeight;
            newValidCounts[f] += reviewWeight; // 累加权重和
          }
        });
        // 抓取剩余最新的一条过程描述
        if (r.timestamp && r.timestamp > latestTimestamp && r.process) {
          latestTimestamp = r.timestamp;
          latestProcess = r.process;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // 重新核实平均分
  let totalValidAvg = 0, validFieldCount = 0;
  ['photos', 'looks', 'body', 'service', 'attitude', 'env', 'cp'].forEach(f => {
    if (newValidCounts[f] > 0) {
      newAvgScores[f] = newAvgScores[f] / newValidCounts[f];
      totalValidAvg += newAvgScores[f];
      validFieldCount++;
    } else {
      newAvgScores[f] = 0;
    }
  });

  const currentOverallAvg = validFieldCount > 0 ? (totalValidAvg / validFieldCount) : 0;

  // 1. 将新核对的数据写入覆盖 Firebase 中的老师节点
  // 🟢 新增：重算好评计数（单次车评均分≥8.0 视为好评）
  let recalcGoodCount = 0;
  if (allReviews) {
    for (let key in allReviews) {
      const r = allReviews[key];
      if (r.status === 'approved' && r.teacher === teacherName) {
        let rTotal = 0, rCount = 0;
        ['photos', 'looks', 'body', 'service', 'attitude', 'env', 'cp'].forEach(f => {
          const m = String(r[f] || '').match(/[\d.]+/);
          if (m) { rTotal += parseFloat(m[0]); rCount++; }
        });
        if (rCount > 0 && rTotal / rCount >= 8.0) recalcGoodCount++;
      }
    }
  }

  updateFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`, {
    total_reviews: totalReviews,
    avg_scores: newAvgScores,
    valid_counts: newValidCounts,
    average_score: currentOverallAvg.toFixed(2),
    good_review_count: recalcGoodCount // 🟢 新增：同步更新好评计数
  });

  // 2. 静默同步刷新频道原帖，如果车评被删光了，会直接退回“暂无 (0 份真实车评)” 的状态
  const teacherData = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`) || {};
  if (teacherData.message_id && teacherData.original_text) {
    let ratingLabel = "";
    if (currentOverallAvg > 0) {
      if (currentOverallAvg >= 8.00) ratingLabel = " 🌟<b>[好评]</b>";
      else if (currentOverallAvg >= 5.00) ratingLabel = " ⭐<b>[中评]</b>";
      else ratingLabel = " 💔<b>[差评]</b>";
    }

    const profileScorePanel = `📊 <b>【全维度评分】: ${currentOverallAvg > 0 ? currentOverallAvg.toFixed(2) + ' 分' : '暂无'}</b>${ratingLabel} (基于 ${totalReviews} 份真实车评)\n` +
                              `📷人照: ${newAvgScores.photos > 0 ? newAvgScores.photos.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `✨颜值: ${newAvgScores.looks > 0 ? newAvgScores.looks.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `💃身材: ${newAvgScores.body > 0 ? newAvgScores.body.toFixed(1) : '-'}\n` +
                              `💋服务: ${newAvgScores.service > 0 ? newAvgScores.service.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `💓态度: ${newAvgScores.attitude > 0 ? newAvgScores.attitude.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `🏡环境: ${newAvgScores.env > 0 ? newAvgScores.env.toFixed(1) : '-'} | ` +
                              `💰性价比: ${newAvgScores.cp > 0 ? newAvgScores.cp.toFixed(1) : '-'}\n` +
                              `💬 最新过程描述: ${latestProcess && latestProcess !== '暂无' && latestProcess.length > 500 ? '\n<blockquote expandable>' + escapeHtml(latestProcess) + '</blockquote>' : escapeHtml(latestProcess)}\n` +
                              `-------------------\n`;

    let safeOriginalText = teacherData.original_text.replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
    safeOriginalText = safeOriginalText.replace(/📊[\s\S]*?【全维度评分】[\s\S]*?-------------------\n?/g, '').trim();

    let profileLines = safeOriginalText.split('\n');
    // 🟢 优先寻找“编号”行，如果没有再寻找名字行
    let pIdx = profileLines.findIndex(line => line.includes('编号'));
    if (pIdx === -1) pIdx = profileLines.findIndex(line => line.includes(teacherName) || line.includes('名字') || line.includes('姓名') || line.includes('老师'));
    
    if (pIdx !== -1) {
      profileLines.splice(pIdx + 1, 0, '\n' + profileScorePanel.trim() + '\n');
    } else {
      profileLines.unshift(profileScorePanel.trim());
    }

    let newProfileText = profileLines.join('\n');
    const editPayload = { chat_id: teacherData.channel_id || PUBLISH_CHANNEL_ID, message_id: teacherData.message_id, parse_mode: "HTML" };
    if (teacherData.has_media) {
      editPayload.caption = newProfileText;
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageCaption", editPayload);
    } else {
      editPayload.text = newProfileText;
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", editPayload);
    }
  }
}
// 🟢 新增：同步更新公网频道中所有定时消息的动态状态按钮
function syncScheduledMessagesStatus(teacherName) {
  const tasks = readFromFirebase('schedule_queue');
  if (!tasks) return;

  // ✅ 【BUG修复】提前一次性读取全部老师状态，避免函数内部对每个 statusBind 单独请求 Firebase
  // 同时将匹配逻辑从"仅匹配本次打卡老师"扩展为"包含任意 STATUS 绑定的消息都刷新"
  // 确保同一条消息里多个 STATUS 按钮在任何一位老师打卡时都能被同步更新
  const allTeachersData = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};

  for (let id in tasks) {
    let task = tasks[id];
    // ✅ 修复：从"只刷新含 teacherName 的任务"改为"刷新所有含任意 STATUS 绑定的任务"
    if ((task.messageId || task.lastMessageId) && task.buttonsRaw && task.buttonsRaw.toUpperCase().includes('STATUS:')) {
       let keyboard = [];
       task.buttonsRaw.split('\n').forEach(row => {
          let btnRow = [];
          row.split('|').forEach(btn => {
            let parts = btn.split('-');
            if (parts.length >= 2) {
              let urlObj = "";
              let styleObj = null;
              let statusBind = null;
              let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
              
              let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
              if (lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                  statusBind = lastP.substring(7).trim();
                  urlParts.pop();
                  lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
              }
              if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
                  styleObj = lastP.toLowerCase();
                  urlParts.pop();
                  lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
                  if (!statusBind && lastP.toUpperCase().startsWith('STATUS:')) {
                      statusBind = lastP.substring(7).trim();
                      urlParts.pop();
                  }
              }
              urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();
              if (!urlObj.startsWith('http') && !urlObj.startsWith('tg://')) urlObj = 'https://' + urlObj;
              
              let btnText = parts[0].trim();
              if (statusBind) {
                 // ✅ 【BUG修复】直接从已缓存的 allTeachersData 读取，避免每个按钮单独请求 Firebase
                 const tData = allTeachersData[statusBind] || {};
                 let sText = "🔴下班";
                 if (tData.status === 'online') sText = "🟢在岗";
                 else if (tData.status === 'leave') sText = "🟡休息";
                 btnText += ` [${sText}]`;
              }

              let btnDef = { text: btnText, url: urlObj };
              if (styleObj) btnDef.style = styleObj;
              btnRow.push(btnDef);
            }
          });
          if (btnRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(btnRow);
       });
       
       if (keyboard.length > 0) {
         const msgToEdit = task.messageId || task.lastMessageId;
         try {
           sendTelegramMessage("editMessageReplyMarkup", {
             chat_id: task.target,
             message_id: msgToEdit,
             reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: keyboard }
           });
         } catch(e) {}
       }
    }
  }
}

// 设置 Webhook 并在代码中自动注入定时触发器
function setWebhook() {
  if (!WEBAPP_URL || WEBAPP_URL === 'YOUR_WEBAPP_URL_HERE' || WEBAPP_URL === '') {
    Logger.log("❌ 错误：请先在“项目设置” -> “脚本属性”中配置真实的 WEBAPP_URL 部署链接！");
    return;
  }
  
  // 1. 设置 Telegram Webhook 链接
  // 🟢 核心修复：显式声明 allowed_updates，将 chat_member 加入订阅列表！
  //    不加这个参数，Telegram 默认不推送 chat_member 事件，
  //    导致群组开启"隐藏群成员"后欢迎消息彻底失效。
  const url = TG_API_URL + 'setWebhook?url=' + encodeURIComponent(WEBAPP_URL) 
    + '&allowed_updates=' + encodeURIComponent(
        JSON.stringify([
          "message",
          "edited_message",
          "callback_query",
          "channel_post",
          "edited_channel_post",
          "chat_member"        // ← 关键！开启群成员变动事件推送
        ])
      );
  const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url);
  Logger.log("设置 Webhook 结果: " + response.getContentText());

  // 2. 自动注入 cleanupDeletedTeachers 定时触发器
  try {
    const triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
    let isTriggerExists = false;
    
    // 检查是否已经存在该触发器，防止重复创建导致额度超限
    for (let i = 0; i < triggers.length; i++) {
      if (triggers[i].getHandlerFunction() === 'cleanupDeletedTeachers') {
        isTriggerExists = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (!isTriggerExists) {
              // 🟢 修复：GAS API 不支持传 2。先设为基准 1 分钟，配合上方的奇数分钟拦截逻辑，实现完美 2 分钟周期
              ScriptApp.newTrigger('cleanupDeletedTeachers').timeBased().everyMinutes(1).create();
              Logger.log("✅ 成功自动注入：cleanupDeletedTeachers 定时触发器已同步创建 (基准1分钟)");
            }
        
        // 🟢 自动注入：滚动跑批高并发计算任务
        const triggersNow = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
            // batchRecalcTeacherScores 已随车评系统移除，不再注入

    // 🟢 核心修复：自动注入关键词自动删除队列处理器（此前只有注释要求手动加触发器，导致时间到了从不删除）
    const triggersFinal = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
    let delQueueExists = triggersFinal.some(t => t.getHandlerFunction() === 'processAutoDeleteQueue');
    if (!delQueueExists) {
      ScriptApp.newTrigger('processAutoDeleteQueue').timeBased().everyMinutes(1).create();
      Logger.log("✅ 成功自动注入：processAutoDeleteQueue 定时触发器 (每1分钟)");
    }
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log("❌ 自动注入触发器时发生异常: " + error.toString());
  }
}

// 查看当前 Webhook 状态 (用于排查问题)
function getWebhookInfo() {
  const url = TG_API_URL + 'getWebhookInfo';
  const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url);
  Logger.log("当前 Webhook 状态: " + response.getContentText());
}

// 删除 Webhook (如果想停用机器人可以运行这个)
function deleteWebhook() {
  const url = TG_API_URL + 'deleteWebhook';
  const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url);
  Logger.log("删除 Webhook 结果: " + response.getContentText());
}
// ================= 🟢 架构优化：用户等级与懒加载计算 =================
function getUserLevelByExp(exp) {
  const e = parseInt(exp) || 0;
  if (e <= 100) return { level: 1, label: '🐣 萌新小白' };
  if (e <= 500) return { level: 2, label: '🚗 寻花使者' };
  if (e <= 1500) return { level: 3, label: '🔍 扫街判官' };
  if (e <= 4000) return { level: 4, label: '🏪 摆龙门掌柜' };
  return { level: 5, label: '🐺 龙门老狼' };
}

// 🟢 修复：补回缺失的函数——按"积分"计算等级与评分权重
// （💰积分 和 👤个人中心 两处菜单都依赖它，此前因未定义导致点击后机器人无任何回复）
function getUserLevelAndWeight(points) {
  const p = Number(points) || 0;
  if (p >= 500) return { level: 'Lv4 车神', weight: 2.0 };
  if (p >= 300) return { level: 'Lv3 达人', weight: 1.5 };
  if (p >= 150) return { level: 'Lv2 老司机', weight: 1.2 };
  return { level: 'Lv1 小白', weight: 1.0 };
}

function calculatePast90DaysActive(userId) {
  // 此处为活跃度测算打桩，实际可拓展查询 checkin_logs 或其他交互日志
  return 30; // 默认满活跃
}

// 🟢 懒加载核心：将全量扫库平摊到用户交互触发点，避免 GAS 运行超时
function getUserProfileWithLazyCalc(userId) {
  let profile = readFromFirebase(`user_profiles/${userId}`) || {};
  
  // 初始默认值熔断
  if (profile.points === undefined) profile.points = 100;
  if (profile.experience === undefined) profile.experience = 0;
  if (profile.credit_score === undefined) profile.credit_score = 100;
  if (!profile.stats) profile.stats = {};

  const now = Date.now();
  const lastRecalc = profile.stats.last_active_days_recalc || 0;
  
  // 阈值校验：距离上次刷新活跃系数超过24小时，执行异步无感刷新
  if (now - lastRecalc > 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
    let realActiveDays = calculatePast90DaysActive(userId);
    
    // 软降级信誉风控
    let credit = profile.credit_score;
    let penaltyMultiplier = 1.0;
    if (credit < 30) penaltyMultiplier = 0.1; // 影子禁言状态

    // 提取等级基准权重
    let lvInfo = getUserLevelByExp(profile.experience);
    let baseWeight = 1.0;
    if (lvInfo.level === 3) baseWeight = 1.2;
    if (lvInfo.level === 4) baseWeight = 1.5;
    if (lvInfo.level === 5) baseWeight = 2.0; // 龙门老狼
    if (credit < 60) baseWeight = 1.0; // 信誉低于60强行剥夺权重特权

    // 活跃度衰减修正
    let activeFactor = Math.min(1.0, realActiveDays / 30);
    let realWeight = Math.round((baseWeight * activeFactor * penaltyMultiplier) * 100) / 100;
    
    profile.weight_base = baseWeight;
    profile.weight_active = activeFactor;
    profile.weight_real = realWeight;
    profile.stats.active_days_90 = realActiveDays;
    profile.stats.last_active_days_recalc = now;
    profile.level = lvInfo.level;
    profile.level_label = lvInfo.label;
    
    // 异步更新数据库
    updateFirebase(`user_profiles/${userId}`, {
      weight_base: baseWeight,
      weight_active: activeFactor,
      weight_real: realWeight,
      "stats/active_days_90": realActiveDays,
      "stats/last_active_days_recalc": now,
      level: lvInfo.level,
      level_label: lvInfo.label
    });
  }
  return profile;
}
// ================= 🟢 新增：群成员变动与欢迎消息底层处理 =================

// 监听由于“隐藏群成员”开启导致退化的 chat_member 底层推送事件
function handleChatMember(chatMember) {
  const chat = chatMember.chat;
  const newStatus = chatMember.new_chat_member.status;
  const oldStatus = chatMember.old_chat_member.status;
  const user = chatMember.new_chat_member.user;

  // 仅放行普通用户进退群
  if (!user.is_bot && (chat.type === 'group' || chat.type === 'supergroup')) {
    // 进群判定
    if (newStatus === 'member' && oldStatus !== 'member') {
      triggerWelcomeMessage(chat.id, user);
    }
  }
}

// 欢迎消息渲染及投递调度
function triggerWelcomeMessage(chatId, user) {
  // 🟢 极速锁：防御 `chat_member` 和 `message.new_chat_members` 同时抵达导致的双重投递
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const lockKey = `welcome_${chatId}_${user.id}`;
  if (cache.get(lockKey)) return;
  cache.put(lockKey, "1", 60);

  const welcomeConfig = readFromFirebase('system_config/welcome_msg');
  // 🟢 总开关：未配置 / 无正文 / 明确关闭 时均不发送（旧数据无 enabled 字段视为开启）
  if (!welcomeConfig || !welcomeConfig.text) return;
  if (welcomeConfig.enabled === false) return;
  
  // 1. 删除前一次投递的欢迎消息（纯净防刷屏）
  // 🟢 核心修复：分离 lastMsgId 状态存储，从外层独立节点读取（兼容旧版 lastMsgIds 数据避免断层）
  const lastMsgId = readFromFirebase(`welcome_last_msgs/${chatId}`) || (welcomeConfig.lastMsgIds && welcomeConfig.lastMsgIds[chatId]);
  if (lastMsgId) {
    try { sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: lastMsgId }); } catch(e) {}
  }

  // 2. 渲染动态变量（将 {user} 替换为原生高亮链接）
  const userName = escapeHtml(user.first_name + (user.last_name ? ' ' + user.last_name : ''));
  const mentionLink = `<a href="tg://user?id=${user.id}">${userName}</a>`;
  const text = welcomeConfig.text.replace(/\{user\}/g, mentionLink);
  
  let payload = { chat_id: chatId, parse_mode: "HTML" };
  // 3. 沿用底层按钮渲染引擎
  if (welcomeConfig.buttonsRaw) {
    let keyboard = [];
    welcomeConfig.buttonsRaw.split('\n').forEach(row => {
      let btnRow = [];
      row.split('|').forEach(btn => {
        let parts = btn.split('-');
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
          let urlObj = "";
          let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
          let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
          let styleObj = null; 
        if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
         styleObj = lastP.toLowerCase(); 
         urlParts.pop();
        }
         urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();
        if (!urlObj.startsWith('http') && !urlObj.startsWith('tg://')) urlObj = 'https://' + urlObj;
        let btnDef = { text: parts[0].trim(), url: urlObj }; 
      
        if (styleObj) btnDef.style = styleObj;               
        btnRow.push(btnDef);                                  
        }
      });
      if (btnRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(btnRow);
    });
    if (keyboard.length > 0) payload.reply_markup = { inline_keyboard: keyboard };
  }

  let sendRes = null;
  // 4. 执行媒体/文本投递
  if (welcomeConfig.mediaId) {
     payload.caption = text;
     if (welcomeConfig.mediaType === 'photo') { payload.photo = welcomeConfig.mediaId; sendRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload);
     }
     else { payload.video = welcomeConfig.mediaId; sendRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload);
     }
  } else {
     payload.text = text;
     sendRes = sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
  }

  // 5. 抓取新消息 ID 并注册销毁队列
  try {
    const resData = JSON.parse(sendRes.getContentText());
    if (resData.ok && resData.result) {
      const sentMsgId = resData.result.message_id;
      
      // 🟢 核心修复：独立存入 welcome_last_msgs 节点，不再嵌套到 system_config 下
      // 彻底避免双库切换时发生对象合并污染导致欢迎配置丢失
      writeToFirebase(`welcome_last_msgs/${chatId}`, sentMsgId);
      
      // 完美复用原有发榜基建，无缝下发异步自毁指令
      if (welcomeConfig.autoDeleteTime > 0) {
        const taskId = 'WELDEL_' + new Date().getTime() + '_' + user.id;
        writeToFirebase(`schedule_queue/${taskId}`, {
           target: chatId,
           messageId: sentMsgId,
           endTime: new Date().getTime() + welcomeConfig.autoDeleteTime
        });
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}
// ================= 🟢 架构优化：高并发滚动跑批队列 =================
function batchRecalcTeacherScores() {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const lock = cache.get("recalc_lock");
  if (lock) return; // 互斥锁防御：防止前一个任务未结束导致并发冲突
  cache.put("recalc_lock", "true", 240);

  try {
    const queueData = readFromFirebase('queue/pending_reviews');
    if (!queueData || Object.keys(queueData).length === 0) {
      cache.remove("recalc_lock");
      return;
    }

    let teacherUpdates = {};
    let processedReviews = [];

    // 内存归集与增量累加
    for (let queueId in queueData) {
      const item = queueData[queueId];
      const tId = item.teacherId;
      
      if (!teacherUpdates[tId]) {
        let tRes = readFromFirebase(`teachers/${tId}/rolling_metrics`) || { total_weighted_score_sum: 0, total_weight_sum: 0 };
        teacherUpdates[tId] = tRes;
      }
      teacherUpdates[tId].total_weighted_score_sum += Number(item.weighted_score || 0);
      teacherUpdates[tId].total_weight_sum += Number(item.weight_snapshot || 0);
      processedReviews.push(queueId);
    }

    // 构建统一的 PATCH 负荷，避免对每个老师单独建立 Http 请求
    let globalPatch = {};
    for (let tId in teacherUpdates) {
      const metrics = teacherUpdates[tId];
      let finalAvg = 0;
      if (metrics.total_weight_sum > 0) {
        finalAvg = Math.round((metrics.total_weighted_score_sum / metrics.total_weight_sum) * 100) / 100;
      }
      globalPatch[`teachers/${tId}/rolling_metrics`] = metrics;
      globalPatch[`teachers/${tId}/average_score`] = finalAvg.toFixed(2);
      globalPatch[`teachers/${tId}/last_recalc_at`] = Date.now();
    }

    // 清空已被消费的队列
    processedReviews.forEach(qId => { globalPatch[`queue/pending_reviews/${qId}`] = null; });
    
    // Firebase Root 级更新提交
    updateFirebase('', globalPatch); 
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log("滚动跑批执行错误：" + e.toString());
  } finally {
    cache.remove("recalc_lock");
  }
}
// ================= 关键词管理模块 =================

// 🟢 渲染并发送关键词管理分页列表
function sendKwManageList(chatId, page, messageId) {
  const kwReplies = readFromFirebase('system_config/keyword_replies') || {};
  const keys = Object.keys(kwReplies);
  
  if (keys.length === 0) {
    const emptyKb = { inline_keyboard: [[{ text: "➕ 立即添加", callback_data: "SETTINGS_KW_START" }], [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]] };
    const textStr = "📭 <b>当前系统没有配置任何关键词回复。</b>";
    if (messageId) {
       try { sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: textStr, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: emptyKb }); } catch(e) { sendMessage(chatId, textStr, emptyKb, "HTML"); }
    } else {
       sendMessage(chatId, textStr, emptyKb, "HTML");
    }
    return;
  }

  const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 5;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(keys.length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
  if (page >= totalPages) page = totalPages - 1;
  if (page < 0) page = 0;

  const startIndex = page * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
  const pageKeys = keys.slice(startIndex, startIndex + ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
  
  let msgText = `⚙️ <b>关键词回复管理 (第 ${page + 1}/${totalPages} 页)</b>\n\n请选择要操作的关键词：\n`;
  let inline_keyboard = [];

  pageKeys.forEach((key, index) => {
    const kwConf = kwReplies[key];
    const modeText = kwConf.mode === 'exact' ? '精准' : '模糊';
    msgText += `\n${startIndex + index + 1}. <code>${kwConf.keyword}</code> [${modeText}]`;
    
    // 排列紧凑的三操作位按钮组
    inline_keyboard.push([
      { text: `👁️ 预览`, callback_data: `KW_PREVIEW_${key}` },
      { text: `✏️ 编辑`, callback_data: `KW_EDIT_${key}` },
      { text: `🗑️ 删除`, callback_data: `KW_DEL_${key}` }
    ]);
  });

  // 安全分页导航组件
  let navRow = [];
  if (page > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `SETTINGS_KW_MANAGE_${page - 1}` });
  if (page < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `SETTINGS_KW_MANAGE_${page + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) inline_keyboard.push(navRow);

  inline_keyboard.push([{ text: "➕ 新增关键词", callback_data: "SETTINGS_KW_START" }, { text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]);

  const keyboard = { inline_keyboard: inline_keyboard };
  
  if (messageId) {
    try {
       sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: msgText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: keyboard });
    } catch (e) {
       sendMessage(chatId, msgText, keyboard, "HTML");
    }
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, msgText, keyboard, "HTML");
  }
}

// 🟢 解析并安全投递预览实例
function previewKeywordReply(chatId, safeKey) {
  const kwConf = readFromFirebase(`system_config/keyword_replies/${safeKey}`);
  if (!kwConf) return;

  let kwKb = {};
  if (kwConf.buttonsRaw) {
    let keyboard = [];
    kwConf.buttonsRaw.split('\n').forEach(row => {
      let btnRow = [];
      row.split('|').forEach(btn => {
        let parts = btn.split('-');
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
          let urlParts = parts.slice(1);
          let lastP = urlParts.length > 0 ? urlParts[urlParts.length - 1].trim() : "";
          let styleStr = ""; 
          if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(lastP.toLowerCase())) {
            styleStr = lastP.toLowerCase(); urlParts.pop();
          }
          let urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();
          if (!urlObj.startsWith('http') && !urlObj.startsWith('tg://')) urlObj = 'https://' + urlObj;
          let btnConf = { text: parts[0].trim(), url: urlObj };
          if (styleStr) btnConf.style = styleStr;
          btnRow.push(btnConf);
        }
      });
      if (btnRow.length > 0) keyboard.push(btnRow);
    });
    if (keyboard.length > 0) kwKb = { inline_keyboard: keyboard };
  }

  let payload = { chat_id: chatId, caption: `👁️ <b>[预览视图] 触发词: ${kwConf.keyword}</b>\n\n${kwConf.text || ""}`, parse_mode: "HTML" };
  if (Object.keys(kwKb).length > 0) payload.reply_markup = kwKb;

  if (kwConf.mediaId) {
    if (kwConf.mediaType === 'photo') { payload.photo = kwConf.mediaId; sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", payload); }
    else { payload.video = kwConf.mediaId; sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", payload); }
  } else {
    payload.text = `👁️ <b>[预览视图] 触发词: ${kwConf.keyword}</b>\n\n${kwConf.text || ""}`;
    delete payload.caption;
    sendTelegramMessage("sendMessage", payload);
  }
}
// ================= 🟢 定时切换老师状态：草稿（必须全局，供 sendSchStatusPicker 等调用） =================
/** 定时状态向导草稿：改存 Firebase，避免老师过多时 UserProperties 超限导致点击无响应 */
function getSchsDraft(userId) {
  try {
    const d = readFromFirebase('admin_drafts/' + userId + '/schs');
    if (d && typeof d === 'object') return d;
  } catch (e) {}
  // 兼容旧版 UserProperties
  try {
    const draftStr = PropertiesService.getUserProperties().getProperty('draft_schs_' + userId);
    if (draftStr) return JSON.parse(draftStr);
  } catch (e) {}
  return { selected: {} };
}
function setSchsDraft(userId, draft) {
  try {
    writeToFirebase('admin_drafts/' + userId + '/schs', draft || { selected: {} });
  } catch (e) {
    try {
      PropertiesService.getUserProperties().setProperty('draft_schs_' + userId, JSON.stringify(draft || { selected: {} }));
    } catch (e2) {}
  }
}
function clearSchsDraft(userId) {
  try { writeToFirebase('admin_drafts/' + userId + '/schs', null); } catch (e) {}
  try { PropertiesService.getUserProperties().deleteProperty('draft_schs_' + userId); } catch (e) {}
}
// ================= 🟢 定时切换老师状态：UI + 队列 =================

function sendSchStatusPicker(chatId, messageId, userId, page) {
  const allTeachers = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};
  // 🟢 核心修复：与「管理老师状态」统一严格过滤
  // 只保留仍有频道帖（message_id）或仍绑定老师（tg_id）的记录
  // 频道已删 + 解绑后残留的 original_text / status / teacher_code 不再进入列表
  const names = Object.keys(allTeachers)
    .filter(n => {
      const t = allTeachers[n];
      if (!t || typeof t !== 'object') return false;
      return !!(t.message_id || t.tg_id);
    })
    .sort((a, b) => (allTeachers[a].added_at || 0) - (allTeachers[b].added_at || 0));

  const draft = getSchsDraft(userId);
  const selected = draft.selected || {};
  // 🟢 只统计仍在有效名单中的已选，并顺带剔除幽灵残留，避免「共 47 / 已选 48」
  const validNameSet = {};
  names.forEach(n => { validNameSet[n] = true; });
  let selCount = 0;
  let needPrune = false;
  for (let n in selected) {
    if (validNameSet[n]) selCount++;
    else needPrune = true;
  }
  if (needPrune) {
    const cleaned = {};
    for (let n in selected) {
      if (validNameSet[n]) cleaned[n] = true;
    }
    draft.selected = cleaned;
    setSchsDraft(userId, draft);
  }

  const PAGE_SIZE = 12;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(names.length / PAGE_SIZE) || 1;
  const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(page) || 0, 0), totalPages - 1);
  const slice = names.slice(safePage * PAGE_SIZE, safePage * PAGE_SIZE + PAGE_SIZE);

  let text = `⏰ <b>定时切换状态 (1/3) 选老师</b>\n` +
    `共 <b>${names.length}</b> 位 | 已选 <b>${selCount}</b> 位 | 第 ${safePage + 1}/${totalPages} 页\n` +
    `点击名字切换勾选，支持单选/批量/全选：\n\n`;

  let keyboard = [];
  let row = [];
  slice.forEach((name, idx) => {
    // 🟢 修复：callback_data 只用「页码_页内序号」，避免中文 Base64 超 Telegram 64 字节导致点击无响应
    const mark = selected[name] ? "✅ " : "⬜ ";
    const label = (mark + name).length > 30 ? (mark + name).slice(0, 28) + "…" : (mark + name);
    row.push({ text: label, callback_data: `SCHS_TOGGLE_${safePage}_${idx}` });
    if (row.length === 2) { keyboard.push(row); row = []; }
  });
  if (row.length) keyboard.push(row);

  let nav = [];
  if (safePage > 0) nav.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `SCHS_PAGE_${safePage - 1}` });
  if (safePage < totalPages - 1) nav.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `SCHS_PAGE_${safePage + 1}` });
  if (nav.length) keyboard.push(nav);

  keyboard.push([
    { text: "✅ 全选全部", callback_data: "SCHS_ALL" },
    { text: "清空", callback_data: "SCHS_CLEAR" }
  ]);
  keyboard.push([{ text: "➡️ 下一步：选状态", callback_data: "SCHS_GOTO_STATUS" }]);
  keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]);

  if (messageId) {
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, text, { inline_keyboard: keyboard }, "HTML");
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, text, { inline_keyboard: keyboard }, "HTML");
  }
}

function sendSchStatusTimePicker(chatId, messageId, status, userId) {
  const statusMap = { online: "🟢在岗", offline: "🔴下班", leave: "🟡休息" };
  // 从草稿读循环开关（默认关）
  let loopOn = false;
  try {
    const d = getSchsDraft(userId || '');
    loopOn = !!(d && d.loop);
  } catch (e) {}

  let text =
    `⏰ <b>定时切换状态 (3/3) 选时间</b>\n\n` +
    `目标状态：<b>${statusMap[status] || status}</b>\n` +
    `🔁 每日循环：<b>${loopOn ? "✅ 开" : "❌ 关"}</b>\n\n` +
    `可选手动延时，或选「每天固定时刻」（北京时间）。\n` +
    `开启循环后：固定时刻每天重复；延时则每次执行后再顺延同等时长。`;

  let keyboard = [];

  // 循环开关
  keyboard.push([{
    text: loopOn ? "🔁 每日循环：✅ 开（点此关闭）" : "🔁 每日循环：❌ 关（点此开启）",
    callback_data: "SCHS_LOOP_TOGGLE"
  }]);

  // —— 每天固定时刻（北京时间）——
  keyboard.push([{ text: "📅 每天固定时刻（北京时间）", callback_data: "SCHS_AT_INFO" }]);
  const atHours = [
    ["00:00", "03:00", "06:00", "09:00"],
    ["12:00", "15:00", "18:00", "21:00"],
    ["22:00", "23:00", "01:00", "02:00"]
  ];
  atHours.forEach(row => {
    keyboard.push(row.map(t => {
      const [hh, mm] = t.split(":");
      return { text: t, callback_data: `SCHS_T_at_${hh}${mm}` };
    }));
  });

  // —— 相对延时（原有）——
  keyboard.push([{ text: "⏳ 相对延时（从现在起算）", callback_data: "SCHS_REL_INFO" }]);
  keyboard.push([
    { text: "5分钟", callback_data: "SCHS_T_m_5" },
    { text: "10分钟", callback_data: "SCHS_T_m_10" },
    { text: "15分钟", callback_data: "SCHS_T_m_15" }
  ]);
  keyboard.push([
    { text: "30分钟", callback_data: "SCHS_T_m_30" },
    { text: "45分钟", callback_data: "SCHS_T_m_45" },
    { text: "60分钟", callback_data: "SCHS_T_m_60" }
  ]);
  let hrRow = [];
  for (let h = 1; h <= 12; h++) {
    hrRow.push({ text: h + "小时", callback_data: "SCHS_T_h_" + h });
    if (hrRow.length === 4) { keyboard.push(hrRow); hrRow = []; }
  }
  if (hrRow.length) keyboard.push(hrRow);
  hrRow = [];
  for (let h = 13; h <= 24; h++) {
    hrRow.push({ text: h + "小时", callback_data: "SCHS_T_h_" + h });
    if (hrRow.length === 4) { keyboard.push(hrRow); hrRow = []; }
  }
  if (hrRow.length) keyboard.push(hrRow);
  keyboard.push([
    { text: "1天", callback_data: "SCHS_T_d_1" },
    { text: "2天", callback_data: "SCHS_T_d_2" },
    { text: "3天", callback_data: "SCHS_T_d_3" },
    { text: "7天", callback_data: "SCHS_T_d_7" }
  ]);
  keyboard.push([
    { text: "1周", callback_data: "SCHS_T_w_1" },
    { text: "2周", callback_data: "SCHS_T_w_2" }
  ]);
  keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回选状态", callback_data: "SCHS_GOTO_STATUS" }]);
  keyboard.push([{ text: "✖️ 取消", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]);

  editMessageText(chatId, messageId, text, { inline_keyboard: keyboard }, "HTML");
}

/** 计算下一个「每天 HH:MM」（北京时间）的时间戳 */
function getNextDailyExecuteAt(hh, mm) {
  const tz = "Asia/Shanghai";
  const now = new Date();
  const todayStr = Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  const h2 = String(hh).padStart(2, "0");
  const m2 = String(mm).padStart(2, "0");
  let candidate = new Date(todayStr + " " + h2 + ":" + m2 + ":00 GMT+0800");
  if (isNaN(candidate.getTime()) || candidate.getTime() <= now.getTime()) {
    // 已过则推到明天同一时刻
    candidate = new Date(candidate.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  }
  return candidate.getTime();
}

function sendSchStatusTaskList(chatId, messageId) {
  const tasks = readFromFirebase('status_schedule_queue') || {};
  const ids = Object.keys(tasks).sort((a, b) => (tasks[a].execute_at || 0) - (tasks[b].execute_at || 0));
  const statusMap = { online: "🟢在岗", offline: "🔴下班", leave: "🟡休息" };

  if (ids.length === 0) {
    const kb = { inline_keyboard: [
      [{ text: "⏰ 新建定时任务", callback_data: "SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_0" }],
      [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
    ]};
    editMessageText(chatId, messageId, "📭 当前没有待执行的定时状态任务。", kb, "HTML");
    return;
  }

  let text = `📋 <b>定时状态任务</b>（共 ${ids.length} 条）\n\n`;
  let keyboard = [];
  ids.forEach((id, idx) => {
    const t = tasks[id];
    const timeStr = new Date(t.execute_at).toLocaleString("zh-CN", { timeZone: "Asia/Shanghai" });
    const names = (t.teacher_names || []).join("、");
    const loopTag = t.loop ? " 🔁循环" : "";
    text += `${idx + 1}. ${statusMap[t.target_status] || t.target_status} | ${escapeHtml(names)}${loopTag}\n   🕒 ${timeStr}（${t.delay_label || ""}）\n`;
    keyboard.push([{ text: `🗑️ 取消 #${idx + 1}`, callback_data: `SCHS_DEL_${id}` }]);
  });
  keyboard.push([{ text: "⏰ 新建定时任务", callback_data: "SETTINGS_SCH_STATUS_0" }]);
  keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]);
  editMessageText(chatId, messageId, text, { inline_keyboard: keyboard }, "HTML");
}
