function processScheduledStatusTasks() {
  const tasks = readFromFirebase('status_schedule_queue');
  if (!tasks) return;

  const now = new Date().getTime();
  const statusMap = { online: "🟢 在岗", offline: "🔴 下班", leave: "🟡 休息" };
  let anyDone = false;
  // 🟢 批量结束后只同步一次定时消息按钮 + 一次在岗榜，避免 N 个老师各自全表扫 / 刷 API
  const changedNames = [];

  for (let id in tasks) {
    const task = tasks[id];
    if (!task || !task.execute_at || now < task.execute_at) continue;
    if (!task.teacher_names || !task.target_status) {
      writeToFirebase(`status_schedule_queue/${id}`, null);
      continue;
    }

    // 防并发：先摘掉任务节点
    writeToFirebase(`status_schedule_queue/${id}`, null);

    // 🟢 一次拉取全表，避免每位老师单独 readFromFirebase
    const allTeachersSnap = readFromFirebase('teachers') || {};

    task.teacher_names.forEach(teacherName => {
      try {
        if (!allTeachersSnap[teacherName]) return;

        updateFirebase(`teachers/${teacherName}`, {
          status: task.target_status,
          last_check_in: now
        });

        writeToFirebase(`checkin_logs/${teacherName}/${now}_${Math.floor(Math.random()*1000)}`, {
          status: task.target_status,
          status_text: statusMap[task.target_status],
          operated_by: "schedule_auto",
          timestamp: now,
          time_string: new Date(now).toLocaleString("zh-CN", { timeZone: "Asia/Shanghai" })
        });

        changedNames.push(teacherName);
        anyDone = true;
      } catch (e) {
        Logger.log("定时状态切换失败 " + teacherName + ": " + e);
      }
    });

    // 🟢 关注通知：仍按人发，但加微延迟，降低 Telegram 429 风险
    const stText = statusMap[task.target_status];
    changedNames.forEach((teacherName, idx) => {
      try {
        if (idx > 0 && idx % 5 === 0) Utilities.sleep(300);
        notifyTeacherFollowers(teacherName, stText);
      } catch (e) {}
    });

    // 🔁 循环：执行完后预约下一次
    if (task.loop) {
      let nextAt = null;
      if (task.schedule_type === "daily_at" && task.daily_hh != null && task.daily_mm != null) {
        const base = getNextDailyExecuteAt(task.daily_hh, task.daily_mm);
        nextAt = base <= now ? base + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 : base;
        if (nextAt - now < 60 * 1000) nextAt += 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
      } else if (task.interval_ms && task.interval_ms > 0) {
        nextAt = now + Number(task.interval_ms);
      }
      if (nextAt) {
        const nextPayload = Object.assign({}, task, {
          execute_at: nextAt,
          last_run_at: now
        });
        writeToFirebase(`status_schedule_queue/${id}`, nextPayload);
      }
    }
  }

  if (anyDone) {
    // 🟢 只调用一次：内部已按 STATUS 绑定刷新所有相关定时消息
    try { syncScheduledMessagesStatus(changedNames[0] || ""); } catch (e) {}
    try { refreshAllTeacherStatusBoards(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}
// ================= 🟢 新增：后台垃圾回收队列处理 (用于定时删除关键词及触发词) =================
// ⚠️ 部署说明：由于GAS性能限制，请前往 Google Apps Script 左侧菜单的“触发器 (Triggers)”，
// 为此函数 `processAutoDeleteQueue` 添加一个【时间驱动 (Time-driven)】 -> 【每分钟执行一次】 的触发器。
function processAutoDeleteQueue() {
  const queueData = readFromFirebase('delete_queue');
  if (!queueData) return;
  
  const now = new Date().getTime();
  
  for (let key in queueData) {
    const task = queueData[key];
    if (!task || !task.expire_at) continue;
    
    // 如果当前时间已超过设定的过期时间
    if (now >= task.expire_at) {
      // 🟢 核心修复：强制 Number(message_id) + String(chat_id)，与全项目其它 deleteMessage 保持一致，防止类型导致 API 静默失败
      try {
        if (task.user_msg_id) {
          sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", {
            chat_id: String(task.chat_id),
            message_id: Number(task.user_msg_id)
          });
        }
      } catch(e) {}
      
      try {
        if (task.bot_msg_id) {
          sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", {
            chat_id: String(task.chat_id),
            message_id: Number(task.bot_msg_id)
          });
        }
      } catch(e) {}
      
      // 从数据库队列中彻底移除该任务，防止重复处理
      writeToFirebase(`delete_queue/${key}`, null);
    }
  }
}
// 🟢 频道收录锁超时重试：把漏掉的 channel_post 补写进 teachers
function processChannelIndexRetry() {
  const queue = readFromFirebase('channel_index_retry');
  if (!queue) return;

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  let got = false;
  try {
    got = lock.tryLock(2000);
  } catch (e) { return; }
  if (!got) return;

  try {
    initGlobalConfig();
    const now = new Date().getTime();
    for (let id in queue) {
      const item = queue[id];
      if (!item || !item.message_id) {
        writeToFirebase(`channel_index_retry/${id}`, null);
        continue;
      }
      // 超过 30 分钟仍失败则丢弃，避免死队列
      if (item.created_at && now - item.created_at > 30 * 60 * 1000) {
        writeToFirebase(`channel_index_retry/${id}`, null);
        continue;
      }
      // 构造成最小 post 对象，复用 handleChannelPost
      const fakePost = {
        chat: { id: item.chat_id },
        message_id: item.message_id,
        media_group_id: item.media_group_id || null,
        text: item.text || "",
        caption: item.text || "",
        photo: item.photo_file_id ? [{ file_id: item.photo_file_id }] : null,
        video: item.video_file_id ? { file_id: item.video_file_id } : null,
        document: item.document_file_id ? { file_id: item.document_file_id } : null
      };
      try {
        handleChannelPost(fakePost);
        writeToFirebase(`channel_index_retry/${id}`, null);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("processChannelIndexRetry:", err);
      }
      Utilities.sleep(500);
    }
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}
// ================= 🛡️ 防垃圾模块（可扩展） =================
// Firebase: system_config/antispam
// 子项: forbidden_words / block_links / block_channel_forward / block_contacts
// 每项: enabled, max_warns(1-3), action(kick|ban|mute)
// forbidden_words 另有: mode(exact|fuzzy), words:[]

function getDefaultAntispamConfig() {
  return {
    forbidden_words: { enabled: false, mode: "fuzzy", words: [], max_warns: 3, action: "mute" },
    block_links: { enabled: false, max_warns: 2, action: "kick" },
    block_channel_forward: { enabled: false, max_warns: 1, action: "kick" },
    block_contacts: { enabled: false, max_warns: 1, action: "kick" },
    // 🟢 新增：禁止表情包/贴纸/GIF动图（含 animated/video sticker）
    block_stickers: { enabled: false, max_warns: 2, action: "kick" },
    // 🟢 新增：禁止 Telegram Premium 会员自定义表情 (custom_emoji)
    block_custom_emoji: { enabled: false, max_warns: 2, action: "kick" },
    // 🟢 新增：禁止引用外部消息入群（external_reply：从其它会话/话题引用进本群）
    block_external_reply: { enabled: false, max_warns: 2, action: "kick" }
  };
}

function getAntispamConfig() {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const def = getDefaultAntispamConfig();
  let raw = {};

  // 🟢 修复：即使命中缓存，也必须与默认项 merge，避免旧缓存缺少 block_contacts 等新字段导致面板崩溃/点击无响应
  const cached = cache.get("antispam_cfg_v2");
  if (cached) {
    try { raw = JSON.parse(cached) || {}; } catch (e) { raw = {}; }
  } else {
    raw = readFromFirebase("system_config/antispam") || {};
  }

  const cfg = {
    forbidden_words: Object.assign({}, def.forbidden_words, raw.forbidden_words || {}),
    block_links: Object.assign({}, def.block_links, raw.block_links || {}),
    block_channel_forward: Object.assign({}, def.block_channel_forward, raw.block_channel_forward || {}),
    block_contacts: Object.assign({}, def.block_contacts, raw.block_contacts || {}),
    block_stickers: Object.assign({}, def.block_stickers, raw.block_stickers || {}),
    block_custom_emoji: Object.assign({}, def.block_custom_emoji, raw.block_custom_emoji || {}),
    block_external_reply: Object.assign({}, def.block_external_reply, raw.block_external_reply || {})
  };
  if (!Array.isArray(cfg.forbidden_words.words)) cfg.forbidden_words.words = [];

  // 仅在非缓存路径或首次合并后回写新 key（旧 v1 自然过期）
  try { cache.put("antispam_cfg_v2", JSON.stringify(cfg), 120); } catch (e) {}
  return cfg;
}

function invalidateAntispamCache() {
  try {
    const c = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    c.remove("antispam_cfg_v1");
    c.remove("antispam_cfg_v2");
  } catch (e) {}
}

function saveAntispamSection(sectionKey, patch) {
  const cfg = getAntispamConfig();
  cfg[sectionKey] = Object.assign({}, cfg[sectionKey] || {}, patch);
  writeToFirebase("system_config/antispam/" + sectionKey, cfg[sectionKey]);
  invalidateAntispamCache();
  return cfg;
}

function actionLabel(action) {
  if (action === "ban") return "永久封锁";
  if (action === "mute") return "禁言";
  return "踢出";
}

function hasLinkInMessage(message) {
  const entities = message.entities || message.caption_entities || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
    const t = entities[i].type;
    if (t === "url" || t === "text_link") return true;
  }
  const text = message.text || message.caption || "";
  // 常见链接形态
  if (/(https?:\/\/|t\.me\/|tg:\/\/|www\.)/i.test(text)) return true;
  return false;
}

/** 是否为联系人卡片（Telegram contact 消息） */
function isContactCardMessage(message) {
  // Bot API: message.contact 存在即为联系人卡片
  return !!(message && message.contact);
}

/** 是否为贴纸/表情包 或 GIF 动图（animation） */
function isStickerOrGifMessage(message) {
  if (!message) return false;
  // sticker：普通/动态/视频贴纸；animation：GIF 动图
  return !!(message.sticker || message.animation);
}

/** 是否包含 Premium 自定义表情 (custom_emoji) */
function hasCustomEmoji(message) {
  if (!message) return false;
  const entities = message.entities || message.caption_entities || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
    if (entities[i].type === "custom_emoji") return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * 是否为「引用外部消息入群」
 * Bot API：message.external_reply 表示回复/引用的来源可能在其它会话或话题
 * 不拦截本群内普通 reply_to_message（同群引用）
 */
function isExternalReplyMessage(message) {
  if (!message) return false;
  // 关联讨论组自动同步帖不拦，避免破坏老师资料收录
  if (message.is_automatic_forward) return false;
  if (message.external_reply) return true;
  return false;
}

function isChannelForwardMessage(message) {
  // ⚠️ 不拦截关联讨论组的「自动同步」帖，避免破坏老师资料收录
  if (message.is_automatic_forward) return false;

  // 1) 旧字段：来源是频道 / 无个人用户信息的来源聊天
  if (message.forward_from_chat) {
    const t = String(message.forward_from_chat.type || "");
    if (t === "channel") return true;
    // 无 forward_from（不是从个人号转）→ 按频道/匿名来源拦截
    if (!message.forward_from && message.forward_from_chat.id) return true;
  }

  // 2) Bot API 7+：forward_origin
  if (message.forward_origin) {
    const origin = message.forward_origin;
    if (origin.type === "channel") return true;
    if (origin.type === "chat" && origin.sender_chat && origin.sender_chat.type === "channel") return true;
    if (origin.chat && origin.chat.type === "channel") return true;
    // 隐藏署名的频道帖：type 可能是 hidden_user，但常伴随 channel 相关字段
    if (origin.type === "hidden_user" && message.forward_from_chat) return true;
  }

  // 3) external_reply 带来源频道
  if (message.external_reply && message.external_reply.chat && message.external_reply.chat.type === "channel") {
    return true;
  }

  // 4) 🟢 关键兜底：有转发标记，但不是「从个人用户转发」
  //    频道转发常见形态 = 有 forward_date，且没有 forward_from（用户对象）
  //    这样可覆盖各客户端字段差异，避免误判成违禁词
  if (message.forward_date && !message.forward_from) {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

function matchForbiddenWords(text, words, mode) {
  if (!text || !words || !words.length) return null;
  const raw = String(text);
  const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
    const w = String(words[i] || "").trim();
    if (!w) continue;
    if (mode === "exact") {
      if (raw === w || lower === w.toLowerCase()) return w;
    } else {
      if (lower.includes(w.toLowerCase())) return w;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * 执行处罚。返回 true 表示 TG API 确认成功；false 表示失败（此时不清零警告，避免“显示已处罚实际没罚”）。
 * 🟢 禁止 Number(userId)：safeParseUpdate 已把 id 变成字符串，Number 会截断 64 位大 ID 导致 ban/mute 静默失败。
 */
/**
 * 执行处罚。返回 { ok, reason }：ok=true 表示 TG API 确认成功；
 * ok=false 时 reason 携带 Telegram 返回的真实失败原因。
 * 🟢 2026修复：仅超级群组支持 ban/restrict/unban，普通群直接短路返回明确原因。
 */
function applyAntispamPenalty(chatId, userId, action, reasonText, chatType) {
  const cid = String(chatId);
  const uid = String(userId);
  const uidNum = Number(uid);
  let ok = false;
  let reason = "";

  // 🟢 核心修复：普通群组（group）不支持限制成员类接口，直接返回友好错误
  if (chatType && chatType !== "supergroup") {
    reason = "本群是普通群组（非超级群），Telegram 不支持禁言/踢人/封禁。请把群升级为超级群后再使用此功能。";
    console.error("applyAntispamPenalty 跳过: chatType=" + chatType + " chatId=" + cid);
    return { ok: false, reason: reason };
  }

  try {
    if (action === "ban") {
      const res = sendTelegramMessage("banChatMember", { chat_id: cid, user_id: uidNum });
      try {
        const data = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
        ok = data.ok === true;
        if (!ok) reason = data.description || ("HTTP " + res.getResponseCode());
      } catch (e) { ok = false; reason = "响应解析失败"; }
    } else if (action === "mute") {
      const until = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 365 * 24 * 3600;
      const res = sendTelegramMessage("restrictChatMember", {
        chat_id: cid,
        user_id: uidNum,
        permissions: {
          can_send_messages: false,
          can_send_audios: false,
          can_send_documents: false,
          can_send_photos: false,
          can_send_videos: false,
          can_send_video_notes: false,
          can_send_voice_notes: false,
          can_send_polls: false,
          can_send_other_messages: false,
          can_add_web_page_previews: false
        },
        until_date: until
      });
      try {
        const data = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
        ok = data.ok === true;
        if (!ok) reason = data.description || ("HTTP " + res.getResponseCode());
      } catch (e) { ok = false; reason = "响应解析失败"; }
    } else {
      // kick：先 ban 再 unban
      const banRes = sendTelegramMessage("banChatMember", { chat_id: cid, user_id: uidNum });
      let banOk = false;
      try {
        const banData = JSON.parse(banRes.getContentText());
        banOk = banData.ok === true;
        if (!banOk) reason = banData.description || ("HTTP " + banRes.getResponseCode());
      } catch (e) { reason = "响应解析失败"; }
      if (banOk) {
        try {
          const unbanRes = sendTelegramMessage("unbanChatMember", {
            chat_id: cid,
            user_id: uidNum,
            only_if_banned: true
          });
          try {
            const unbanData = JSON.parse(unbanRes.getContentText());
            ok = unbanData.ok === true;
            if (!ok) reason = unbanData.description || ("HTTP " + unbanRes.getResponseCode());
          } catch (e) { ok = true; }
        } catch (e) {
          ok = true;
        }
      } else {
        ok = false;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("applyAntispamPenalty:", e);
    ok = false;
    reason = String(e);
  }

  if (!ok) {
    console.error("applyAntispamPenalty 失败: action=" + action + " chatId=" + cid + " userId=" + uid + " reason=" + reason);
  }

  if (ok) {
    writeToFirebase("antispam_warns/" + cid + "/" + uid, null);
  }
  return { ok: ok, reason: reason };
}

/**
 * 记录警告并决定是否处罚。返回 { punished, count, max }
 * 规则与面板文案一致：设置「警告 N 次」→ 第 N 次违规即执行处罚。
 * 例：max_warns=1 → 第1次直接处罚；max_warns=3 → 第3次处罚。
 */
function recordAntispamWarn(chatId, userId, maxWarns) {
  const cid = String(chatId);
  const uid = String(userId);
  const path = "antispam_warns/" + cid + "/" + uid;
  const prev = readFromFirebase(path) || {};
  const count = (parseInt(prev.count, 10) || 0) + 1;
  const max = Math.min(3, Math.max(1, parseInt(maxWarns, 10) || 3));
  writeToFirebase(path, { count: count, updated_at: Date.now() });
  return { punished: count >= max, count: count, max: max };
}

/**
 * 对一条群消息跑全部已开启规则。命中则删消息、警告/处罚，返回 true。
 */
function runAntispamChecks(message) {
  const cfg = getAntispamConfig();
  const chatId = message.chat.id;
  const userId = message.from.id;
  const text = message.text || message.caption || "";

  let hitReason = null;
  let rule = null;

  // 优先级：频道转发 > 外部引用 > 链接 > 联系人卡片 > 表情包/GIF > 自定义表情 > 违禁词
  const en = (v) => v === true || v === "true" || v === 1 || v === "1";

  // 1) 频道转发
  if (cfg.block_channel_forward && en(cfg.block_channel_forward.enabled) && isChannelForwardMessage(message)) {
    hitReason = "禁止转发频道消息";
    rule = cfg.block_channel_forward;
  }
  // 1.5) 🟢 禁止引用外部消息入群（其它会话/话题的 external_reply）
  else if (cfg.block_external_reply && en(cfg.block_external_reply.enabled) && isExternalReplyMessage(message)) {
    hitReason = "禁止引用外部消息";
    rule = cfg.block_external_reply;
  }
  // 2) 链接（非频道转发时才查）
  else if (cfg.block_links && en(cfg.block_links.enabled) && hasLinkInMessage(message)) {
    hitReason = "禁止发送链接";
    rule = cfg.block_links;
  }
  // 3) 联系人卡片
  else if (cfg.block_contacts && en(cfg.block_contacts.enabled) && isContactCardMessage(message)) {
    hitReason = "禁止发送联系人卡片";
    rule = cfg.block_contacts;
  }
  // 4) 🟢 表情包 / 贴纸 / GIF 动图（含 animated / video sticker）
  else if (cfg.block_stickers && en(cfg.block_stickers.enabled) && isStickerOrGifMessage(message)) {
    hitReason = "禁止发送表情包/GIF";
    rule = cfg.block_stickers;
  }
  // 5) 🟢 Premium 会员自定义表情
  else if (cfg.block_custom_emoji && en(cfg.block_custom_emoji.enabled) && hasCustomEmoji(message)) {
    hitReason = "禁止发送自定义表情";
    rule = cfg.block_custom_emoji;
  }
  // 6) 违禁词
  else if (cfg.forbidden_words && en(cfg.forbidden_words.enabled)) {
    const hit = matchForbiddenWords(text, cfg.forbidden_words.words || [], cfg.forbidden_words.mode || "fuzzy");
    if (hit) {
      hitReason = "违禁词：" + hit;
      rule = cfg.forbidden_words;
    }
  }

  if (!hitReason || !rule) return false;

  // 秒删违规消息（message_id 保持 Number，与全项目 deleteMessage 一致）
  try {
    sendTelegramMessage("deleteMessage", {
      chat_id: String(chatId),
      message_id: Number(message.message_id)
    });
  } catch (e) {}

  const mention = message.from.username
    ? "@" + message.from.username
    : escapeHtml(message.from.first_name || "用户");

  const result = recordAntispamWarn(chatId, userId, rule.max_warns);
  const act = rule.action || "kick";
  const actText = actionLabel(act);

  let tipText;
  if (result.punished) {
    // 🟢 传入 chat.type，普通群直接短路，避免无意义的 API 调用和警告次数无限累加
    const chatType = (message.chat && message.chat.type) ? message.chat.type : "";
    const penaltyResult = applyAntispamPenalty(chatId, userId, act, hitReason, chatType);
    if (penaltyResult.ok) {
      tipText =
        `🛡️ <b>防垃圾处罚</b>\n` +
        `${mention} 因「${escapeHtml(hitReason)}」已达警告上限 <b>${result.count}/${result.max}</b>\n` +
        `已执行：<b>${actText}</b>\n` +
        `<i>* 本提示将在 10 秒后自动删除</i>`;
    } else {
      const reasonHint = penaltyResult.reason ? `\n📋 原因：<code>${escapeHtml(penaltyResult.reason)}</code>` : "";
      // 普通群给出更明确的操作指引
      const isBasicGroup = chatType === "group";
      tipText =
        `🛡️ <b>防垃圾处罚失败</b>\n` +
        `${mention} 因「${escapeHtml(hitReason)}」已达上限 <b>${result.count}/${result.max}</b>\n` +
        `本应执行：<b>${actText}</b>，但未能成功。${reasonHint}\n` +
        (isBasicGroup
          ? `<i>* 请把本群「升级为超级群组」后再使用禁言/踢人功能 · 本提示 10 秒后删除</i>`
          : `<i>* 请确认机器人具备「限制成员」管理员权限 · 警告次数已保留 · 本提示 10 秒后删除</i>`);
    }
  } else {
    const remain = result.max - result.count;
    tipText =
      `⚠️ <b>防垃圾警告</b> (${result.count}/${result.max})\n` +
      `${mention}：${escapeHtml(hitReason)}\n` +
      `再违规 <b>${remain}</b> 次将执行：<b>${actText}</b>\n` +
      `<i>* 本提示将在 10 秒后自动删除</i>`;
  }

  // 发送提示，并写入 delete_queue（约 10 秒后自动删）
  let botMsgId = null;
  try {
    const res = sendMessage(chatId, tipText, null, "HTML");
    const resData = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
    if (resData.ok && resData.result) botMsgId = resData.result.message_id;
  } catch (e) {}

  if (botMsgId) {
    const expireAt = new Date().getTime() + 10 * 1000;
    const queueId = `as_tip_${new Date().getTime()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
    writeToFirebase(`delete_queue/${queueId}`, {
      chat_id: String(chatId),
      bot_msg_id: botMsgId,
      user_msg_id: null,
      expire_at: expireAt
    });
  }
  return true;
}

function sendAntispamPanel(chatId, messageId) {
  const cfg = getAntispamConfig();
  const fw = cfg.forbidden_words;
  const bl = cfg.block_links;
  const cf = cfg.block_channel_forward;
  const bc = cfg.block_contacts;
  const bs = cfg.block_stickers;
  const be = cfg.block_custom_emoji;
  const ber = cfg.block_external_reply;

  const onOff = (v) => (v ? "✅开" : "❌关");
  const text =
    `🛡️ <b>防垃圾设置</b>\n\n` +
    `可独立开关；警告次数 1～3：第 N 次违规即执行处罚（踢出 / 永封 / 禁言）。\n` +
    `例：设为 1 = 首次违规直接处罚；设为 3 = 第 3 次违规处罚。\n\n` +
    `1️⃣ <b>违禁词</b> ${onOff(fw.enabled)}\n` +
    `　模式：${fw.mode === "exact" ? "精准" : "模糊"} · 词数：${(fw.words || []).length}\n` +
    `　警告 ${fw.max_warns || 3} 次 → ${actionLabel(fw.action)}\n\n` +
    `2️⃣ <b>禁止链接</b> ${onOff(bl.enabled)}\n` +
    `　警告 ${bl.max_warns || 2} 次 → ${actionLabel(bl.action)}\n\n` +
    `3️⃣ <b>禁止频道转发</b> ${onOff(cf.enabled)}\n` +
    `　警告 ${cf.max_warns || 1} 次 → ${actionLabel(cf.action)}\n\n` +
    `4️⃣ <b>禁止联系人卡片</b> ${onOff(bc.enabled)}\n` +
    `　警告 ${bc.max_warns || 1} 次 → ${actionLabel(bc.action)}\n\n` +
    `5️⃣ <b>禁止表情包/GIF</b> ${onOff(bs.enabled)}\n` +
    `　含贴纸、动态贴纸、视频贴纸、GIF 动图\n` +
    `　警告 ${bs.max_warns || 2} 次 → ${actionLabel(bs.action)}\n\n` +
    `6️⃣ <b>禁止自定义表情</b> ${onOff(be.enabled)}\n` +
    `　Telegram Premium 会员自定义表情\n` +
    `　警告 ${be.max_warns || 2} 次 → ${actionLabel(be.action)}\n\n` +
    `7️⃣ <b>禁止引用外部消息</b> ${onOff(ber.enabled)}\n` +
    `　从其它会话/话题引用进本群（external_reply）\n` +
    `　警告 ${ber.max_warns || 2} 次 → ${actionLabel(ber.action)}`;

  const kb = {
    inline_keyboard: [
      [{ text: `违禁词 ${onOff(fw.enabled)}`, callback_data: "AS_TOGGLE_forbidden_words" },
       { text: "📝 管理词库", callback_data: "AS_FW_MANAGE" }],
      [{ text: `模式:${fw.mode === "exact" ? "精准" : "模糊"}`, callback_data: "AS_MODE_forbidden_words" },
       { text: `警告${fw.max_warns || 3}次`, callback_data: "AS_WARN_forbidden_words" },
       { text: actionLabel(fw.action), callback_data: "AS_ACT_forbidden_words" }],
      [{ text: `禁链接 ${onOff(bl.enabled)}`, callback_data: "AS_TOGGLE_block_links" },
       { text: `警告${bl.max_warns || 2}次`, callback_data: "AS_WARN_block_links" },
       { text: actionLabel(bl.action), callback_data: "AS_ACT_block_links" }],
      [{ text: `禁频道转发 ${onOff(cf.enabled)}`, callback_data: "AS_TOGGLE_block_channel_forward" },
       { text: `警告${cf.max_warns || 1}次`, callback_data: "AS_WARN_block_channel_forward" },
       { text: actionLabel(cf.action), callback_data: "AS_ACT_block_channel_forward" }],
      [{ text: `禁联系人卡片 ${onOff(bc.enabled)}`, callback_data: "AS_TOGGLE_block_contacts" },
       { text: `警告${bc.max_warns || 1}次`, callback_data: "AS_WARN_block_contacts" },
       { text: actionLabel(bc.action), callback_data: "AS_ACT_block_contacts" }],
      // 🟢 新增：表情包/GIF
      [{ text: `禁表情包/GIF ${onOff(bs.enabled)}`, callback_data: "AS_TOGGLE_block_stickers" },
       { text: `警告${bs.max_warns || 2}次`, callback_data: "AS_WARN_block_stickers" },
       { text: actionLabel(bs.action), callback_data: "AS_ACT_block_stickers" }],
      // 🟢 新增：自定义表情
      [{ text: `禁自定义表情 ${onOff(be.enabled)}`, callback_data: "AS_TOGGLE_block_custom_emoji" },
       { text: `警告${be.max_warns || 2}次`, callback_data: "AS_WARN_block_custom_emoji" },
       { text: actionLabel(be.action), callback_data: "AS_ACT_block_custom_emoji" }],
      // 🟢 新增：禁止引用外部消息入群
      [{ text: `禁外部引用 ${onOff(ber.enabled)}`, callback_data: "AS_TOGGLE_block_external_reply" },
       { text: `警告${ber.max_warns || 2}次`, callback_data: "AS_WARN_block_external_reply" },
       { text: actionLabel(ber.action), callback_data: "AS_ACT_block_external_reply" }],
      [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
    ]
  };

  if (messageId) {
    try {
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
        chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: text, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: kb
      });
    } catch (e) {
      sendMessage(chatId, text, kb, "HTML");
    }
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, text, kb, "HTML");
  }
}

function sendForbiddenWordsManage(chatId, messageId) {
  const cfg = getAntispamConfig();
  const words = cfg.forbidden_words.words || [];
  let text = `📝 <b>违禁词词库</b>（${cfg.forbidden_words.mode === "exact" ? "精准" : "模糊"}）\n\n`;
  if (words.length === 0) {
    text += "（空）请点下方添加。";
  } else {
    words.forEach((w, i) => { text += `${i + 1}. <code>${escapeHtml(w)}</code>\n`; });
  }
  const kb = { inline_keyboard: [] };
  // 每词一个删除按钮（最多展示前 20 个）
  words.slice(0, 20).forEach((w, i) => {
    kb.inline_keyboard.push([{ text: `🗑️ ${w}`, callback_data: "AS_FW_DEL_" + i }]);
  });
  kb.inline_keyboard.push([{ text: "➕ 添加违禁词", callback_data: "AS_FW_ADD" }]);
  kb.inline_keyboard.push([{ text: "🔙 返回防垃圾", callback_data: "SETTINGS_ANTISPAM" }]);

  if (messageId) {
    try {
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
        chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: text, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: kb
      });
    } catch (e) {
      sendMessage(chatId, text, kb, "HTML");
    }
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, text, kb, "HTML");
  }
}
// 🟢 /menu 启动提示语（可在设置面板修改）
function getDefaultMenuPromptText() {
  return "📋群内回复关键词查找老师！\n例如 东莞 或 深圳 等：";
}
function getMenuPromptText() {
  try {
    const v = readFromFirebase('system_config/menu_prompt_text');
    if (v != null && String(v).trim() !== '') return String(v);
  } catch (e) {}
  return getDefaultMenuPromptText();
}
// 🟢 菜单键盘：每行按钮数量（1～8，默认 3）
function getMenuKeyboardCols() {
  let n = parseInt(readFromFirebase('system_config/menu_keyboard_cols'), 10);
  if (isNaN(n) || n < 1) n = 3;
  if (n > 8) n = 8; // Telegram ReplyKeyboard 过宽体验差，上限 8
  return n;
}
/** 从 MENU_PREVIEW_/EDIT_/DEL_ 的「页码_序号」还原 Firebase 节点 key */
function resolveMenuButtonKeyFromCallback(suffix) {
  // 新格式：page_index；旧格式：整段 key（兼容极少数已发出的旧按钮）
  const parts = String(suffix || '').split('_');
  if (parts.length >= 2 && /^\d+$/.test(parts[0]) && /^\d+$/.test(parts[1])) {
    const page = parseInt(parts[0], 10) || 0;
    const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10) || 0;
    const menuBtns = readFromFirebase('system_config/menu_buttons') || {};
    const keys = Object.keys(menuBtns).sort((a, b) => (menuBtns[a].order || 0) - (menuBtns[b].order || 0));
    const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 5;
    return keys[page * ITEMS_PER_PAGE + index] || null;
  }
  return suffix || null; // 旧版整 key 兜底
}
// 🟢 菜单键盘管理列表
function sendMenuManageList(chatId, page, messageId) {
  const menuBtns = readFromFirebase('system_config/menu_buttons') || {};
  const keys = Object.keys(menuBtns).sort((a, b) => (menuBtns[a].order || 0) - (menuBtns[b].order || 0));
  const cols = getMenuKeyboardCols();

  // 🟢 每行按钮数快捷选择（1～8），当前值带 ✅
  function buildColsRow() {
    let row = [];
    for (let n = 1; n <= 8; n++) {
      row.push({
        text: (n === cols ? "✅ " : "") + n,
        callback_data: `SETTINGS_MENU_COLS_${n}`
      });
    }
    return row;
  }

  if (keys.length === 0) {
    const emptyKb = { inline_keyboard: [
      [{ text: "📐 每行按钮数（当前 " + cols + "）", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_COLS_INFO" }],
      buildColsRow(),
      [{ text: "➕ 立即添加", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_START" }],
      [{ text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }]
    ]};
    const textStr =
      "📭 <b>当前没有配置任何菜单键盘按钮。</b>\n用户发送 /menu 将提示暂无按钮。\n\n" +
      `📐 底部键盘每行排列：<b>${cols}</b> 个按钮（点数字即可切换）`;
    if (messageId) {
      try { sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: textStr, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: emptyKb }); }
      catch(e) { sendMessage(chatId, textStr, emptyKb, "HTML"); }
    } else {
      sendMessage(chatId, textStr, emptyKb, "HTML");
    }
    return;
  }

  const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 5;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(keys.length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
  if (page >= totalPages) page = totalPages - 1;
  if (page < 0) page = 0;
  const startIndex = page * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
  const pageKeys = keys.slice(startIndex, startIndex + ITEMS_PER_PAGE);

  let msgText =
    `⌨️ <b>菜单键盘管理 (第 ${page + 1}/${totalPages} 页)</b>\n` +
    `📐 每行排列：<b>${cols}</b> 个按钮（下方点数字切换）\n\n` +
    `请选择要操作的按钮：\n`;
  let inline_keyboard = [];

  pageKeys.forEach((key, index) => {
    const conf = menuBtns[key];
    const delMin = parseInt(conf.autoDeleteMinutes, 10) || 0;
    const delHint = delMin > 0 ? ` · ⏱${delMin}分钟删` : ` · ⏱关`;
    msgText += `\n${startIndex + index + 1}. <code>${escapeHtml(conf.label)}</code>${delHint}`;
    // 🟢 修复：callback_data 只用「页码_页内序号」，永远远短于 64 字节
    // 兼容历史超长 Base64 key；点击后再按同一排序规则还原真实 Firebase key
    inline_keyboard.push([
      { text: `👁️ 预览`, callback_data: `MENU_PREVIEW_${page}_${index}` },
      { text: `✏️ 编辑`, callback_data: `MENU_EDIT_${page}_${index}` },
      { text: `🗑️ 删除`, callback_data: `MENU_DEL_${page}_${index}` }
    ]);
  });

  let navRow = [];
  if (page > 0) navRow.push({ text: "⬅️ 上一页", callback_data: `SETTINGS_MENU_MANAGE_${page - 1}` });
  if (page < totalPages - 1) navRow.push({ text: "下一页 ➡️", callback_data: `SETTINGS_MENU_MANAGE_${page + 1}` });
  if (navRow.length > 0) inline_keyboard.push(navRow);

  // 🟢 列数调节区
  inline_keyboard.push([{ text: `📐 每行按钮数（当前 ${cols}）`, callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_COLS_INFO" }]);
  inline_keyboard.push(buildColsRow());

  inline_keyboard.push([
    { text: "➕ 新增按钮", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_START" },
    { text: "🔙 返回控制面板", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MAIN" }
  ]);

  const keyboard = { inline_keyboard: inline_keyboard };
  if (messageId) {
    try {
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", { chat_id: chatId, message_id: messageId, text: msgText, parse_mode: "HTML", reply_markup: keyboard });
    } catch (e) {
      sendMessage(chatId, msgText, keyboard, "HTML");
    }
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, msgText, keyboard, "HTML");
  }
}

function previewMenuButton(chatId, safeKey, messageId) {
  const conf = readFromFirebase(`system_config/menu_buttons/${safeKey}`);
  if (!conf) return;

  // 解析内联按钮（含颜色 style）
  let inlineRows = [];
  if (conf.buttonsRaw) {
    conf.buttonsRaw.split('\n').forEach(row => {
      let btnRow = [];
      row.split('|').forEach(btn => {
        const normalizedBtn = String(btn).replace(/[－—–]/g, '-');
        let parts = normalizedBtn.split('-');
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
          let urlParts = parts.slice(1).map(p => p.trim()).filter(p => p !== '');
          let styleStr = "";
          for (let i = urlParts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const token = urlParts[i].toLowerCase();
            if (['primary', 'success', 'danger'].includes(token)) {
              styleStr = token;
              urlParts.splice(i, 1);
              break;
            }
          }
          let urlObj = urlParts.join('-').trim();
          if (!urlObj.startsWith('http') && !urlObj.startsWith('tg://')) urlObj = 'https://' + urlObj;
          let btnConf = { text: parts[0].trim(), url: urlObj };
          if (styleStr) btnConf.style = styleStr;
          btnRow.push(btnConf);
        }
      });
      if (btnRow.length > 0) inlineRows.push(btnRow);
    });
  }

  // 底部固定「返回列表」
  inlineRows.push([{ text: "🔙 返回列表", callback_data: "SETTINGS_MENU_MANAGE_0" }]);
  const replyMarkup = { inline_keyboard: inlineRows };

  const styleHint = conf.style ? `\n🎨 键盘颜色：<code>${escapeHtml(String(conf.style))}</code>` : "";
  const mediaHint = conf.mediaId ? `\n📎 含${conf.mediaType === 'photo' ? '图片' : '视频'}附件（已另发一条预览）` : "";
  const delMin = parseInt(conf.autoDeleteMinutes, 10) || 0;
  const delHint = `\n⏱ 自动删除：${delMin > 0 ? (delMin + ' 分钟后（回复 + 触发消息）') : '关闭'}`;
  const previewText = `👁️ <b>[预览] 按钮: ${escapeHtml(conf.label)}</b>${styleHint}${mediaHint}${delHint}\n\n${conf.text || "（无文字内容）"}`;

  // 🟢 有 messageId 时原地编辑；失败再发新消息
  if (messageId) {
    try {
      sendTelegramMessage("editMessageText", {
        chat_id: chatId,
        message_id: messageId,
        text: previewText,
        parse_mode: "HTML",
        reply_markup: replyMarkup
      });
    } catch (e) {
      sendMessage(chatId, previewText, replyMarkup, "HTML");
    }
  } else {
    sendMessage(chatId, previewText, replyMarkup, "HTML");
  }

  // 媒体无法把文字消息改成图/视频，单独再发一条真实预览
  if (conf.mediaId) {
    let mediaPayload = {
      chat_id: chatId,
      caption: `👁️ <b>[媒体预览] ${escapeHtml(conf.label)}</b>\n\n${conf.text || ""}`,
      parse_mode: "HTML"
    };
    if (inlineRows.length > 1) {
      mediaPayload.reply_markup = { inline_keyboard: inlineRows.slice(0, -1) };
    }
    if (conf.mediaType === 'photo') {
      mediaPayload.photo = conf.mediaId;
      sendTelegramMessage("sendPhoto", mediaPayload);
    } else {
      mediaPayload.video = conf.mediaId;
      sendTelegramMessage("sendVideo", mediaPayload);
    }
  }
}
